# CFIR LLT Repair Log

## Declaration-call named argument diagnostics stop after the first invalid parameter name

- problem type: direct calls to declarations with positional-only parameters must report the first invalid named argument and stop parameter-name matching, while function-value calls diagnose every unsupported named argument.
- root cause: three duplicated declaration-call fixtures expected `NAMED_ARGUMENTS_NOT_ALLOWED` on both `a` and `b`, but official declaration-call matching stops after diagnosing `a`; the CFIR mapper already followed the official behavior.
- official Cangjie evidence: `cjc 1.0.5` reports one `sema_invalid_named_arguments` on `a` for `positional(a: 1, b: 2)` in all three declaration-call fixtures. `external/cangjie_compiler/src/Sema/TypeCheckCall.cpp:50-74,618-672` diagnoses the first invalid name and immediately returns. By contrast, `defaultParameter4_2.cj` is a function-value call and `TypeCheckCall.cpp:2147-2166` iterates both named arguments, so its two diagnostics remain correct.
- Kotlin counterpart files consulted: `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/resolve/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/resolve/calls/stages/FirArgumentsToParametersMapper.kt`, `ResolutionStages.kt`, and `external/kotlin/compiler/resolution.common/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/resolve/calls/tower/CandidateApplicability.kt`; Kotlin accumulates all mapping errors, but that architecture does not override the official Cangjie short-circuit semantics.
- CFIR owner files changed: fixture corrections only in `cfir/analysis-tests/testData/diagnostics/call/namedArgumentsAndArityRich.cj`, `diagnostics2/call/namedArgumentsAndArity.cj`, and `diagnostics2/calls/namedArgumentsAndArity.cj`.
- repair principle: preserve the shared declaration-call mapper's official first-error boundary and correct duplicated expectations without changing the distinct function-value diagnostic path.
- fixtures covered: all PSI, non-PSI, alias-expansion, and no-alias-expansion generated entries for the three declaration-call fixtures; `llt/function/defaultParameter4_2.cj` was retained as the function-value guard.
- verification command: `.\gradlew.bat :cfir:analysis-tests:test --tests '*testNamedArgumentsAndArity*' --no-daemon --max-workers=1 --console=plain --no-build-cache --no-configuration-cache`.
- verification outcome: `BUILD SUCCESSFUL`; fresh XML contains `9 tests, 0 failures, 0 errors` across all matching generated suites.

## Function static interface parameter naming compatibility

- problem type: static interface implementation parameter namedness must match the inherited interface requirement even without an explicit override-like modifier.
- root cause: `CfirOverrideChecker` gated parameter compatibility on override-like modifiers, and the ordinary direct-override graph omitted static interface requirements.
- official Cangjie evidence: `cjc` reports `sema_param_named_mismatched` on the implementing function name in `abstract_named_00.cj`; `external/cangjie_compiler/src/Sema/InheritanceChecker/StructInheritanceChecker.cpp:71-119,1377-1378` compares parent/child named parameters and diagnoses the child function identifier.
- Kotlin counterpart files consulted: `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/checkers/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/analysis/checkers/declaration/FirFunctionParameterChecker.kt`, `FirHelpers.kt`, and `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/tree/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/scopes/FirTypeScope.kt`.
- CFIR owner files changed: `cfir/checkers/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/analysis/checkers/declaration/CfirOverrideChecker.kt`, `CfirOverrideCheckerUtils.kt`.
- repair principle: consume the inherited static interface requirement from the shared class use-site scope and run parameter-contract compatibility independently of whether the implementation spells an override-like modifier.
- fixtures covered: PSI and non-PSI `FunctionGenerated.testAbstractNamed00`.
- verification commands and outcome: focused five-fixture PSI/non-PSI command passed this method on both paths; full PSI/non-PSI `FunctionGenerated` regression completed `202 tests, 0 failures` (`BUILD SUCCESSFUL`, 2026-07-12).

## Function public API exposure of internal inferred and explicit types

- problem type: public functions and top-level pattern variables exposing internal types must distinguish inferred-type hints from explicit-type accessibility errors and report each pattern binding.
- root cause: exposed-type checking omitted top-level pattern bindings and collapsed inferred and explicit callable/variable types into `ACCESSIBILITY_ERROR`.
- official Cangjie evidence: `cjc` reports eight `sema_accessibility_with_main_hint` diagnostics for inferred declarations and three `sema_accessibility` diagnostics for explicit types in `internal_type_func_return_01.cj`; `external/cangjie_compiler/src/Sema/LegalityOfUsage/CheckInternalTypeUse.cpp:79-118,137-178` and `src/Sema/Diags.cpp:524-578` implement this split.
- Kotlin counterpart files consulted: `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/checkers/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/analysis/checkers/declaration/FirExposedVisibilityDeclarationChecker.kt`, `FirVisibilityHelpers.kt`.
- CFIR owner files changed: `cfir/checkers/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/analysis/checkers/declaration/CfirGeneralSemanticsChecker.kt`.
- repair principle: keep exposure analysis at the declaration checker, preserve explicit-versus-inferred type provenance, and project pattern declarations to their individual binding sources.
- fixtures covered: PSI and non-PSI `FunctionGenerated.testInternalTypeFuncReturn01`.
- verification commands and outcome: focused five-fixture PSI/non-PSI command passed this method on both paths; full PSI/non-PSI `FunctionGenerated` regression completed `202 tests, 0 failures` (`BUILD SUCCESSFUL`, 2026-07-12).

## Function capture diagnostic full-token range

- problem type: transitive captured-function assignment diagnostics must cover the complete referenced identifier.
- root cause: the fixture expected only the first character of `g2`, while CFIR correctly selected the complete identifier token.
- official Cangjie evidence: `cjc` reports `sema_func_capture_var_cannot_assign` for `let c = g2`; capture semantics are implemented in `external/cangjie_compiler/src/Sema/TypeCheckExpr.cpp:94-124`, `LegalityOfUsage/LegalityOfUsage.cpp:28-61`, and `TypeChecker.cpp:1701-1733`. Diagnostic width follows this repository's full-token range policy.
- Kotlin counterpart files consulted: `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/resolve/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/resolve/dfa/cfg/ControlFlowGraphBuilder.kt` and FIR CFA initialization analyzers.
- CFIR owner files changed: range-only fixture correction in `cfir/analysis-tests/testData/llt/function/function_capture2.cj`.
- repair principle: retain the shared capture diagnostic source and correct the expected range to the complete PSI/light-tree identifier token.
- fixtures covered: PSI and non-PSI `FunctionGenerated.testFunctionCapture2`.
- verification commands and outcome: focused five-fixture PSI/non-PSI command passed this method on both paths; full PSI/non-PSI `FunctionGenerated` regression completed `202 tests, 0 failures` (`BUILD SUCCESSFUL`, 2026-07-12).

## PSI raw identifier identity for escaped main and init names

- problem type: escaped identifiers such as `` `main` `` and `` `init` `` must remain ordinary declaration/reference names and must not collide with the unescaped program entry declaration.
- root cause: PSI normalized escaped identifiers while LightTree retained raw token identity; field/pattern declarations additionally exposed IDENTIFIER ranges without surrounding backticks, producing entry conflicts and unresolved references.
- official Cangjie evidence: `cjc` accepts `raw_init_main.cj` and `raw_init_main02.cj` without semantic errors; `external/cangjie_compiler/src/Lex/Lexer.cpp:858-866`, `src/Parse/ParserUtils.cpp:556-583`, and `src/Parse/ParseDecl.cpp:954-963,2011` preserve `SrcIdentifier.isRaw` and exclude raw `main` from entry recognition.
- Kotlin counterpart files consulted: `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/checkers/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/analysis/checkers/FirConflictsHelpers.kt`, `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/semantics/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/mainFunctionDetection.kt`, and `external/kotlin/compiler/cli/cli-jvm/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/cli/jvm/compiler/firFindMainClass.kt`.
- CFIR owner files changed: `cfir/raw-cfir/psi2cfir/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/builder/PsiConversionUtils.kt`, `PsiRawCfirBuilder.kt`.
- repair principle: preserve one structural raw-token name identity across PSI declarations, pattern bindings, type/name references, and LightTree, leaving entry discovery separate from ordinary conflict resolution.
- fixtures covered: PSI and non-PSI `FunctionGenerated.testRawInitMain` and `testRawInitMain02`.
- verification commands and outcome: focused `testRawInitMain02` completed `2 tests, 0 failures`; full PSI/non-PSI `FunctionGenerated` regression completed `202 tests, 0 failures` (`BUILD SUCCESSFUL`, 2026-07-12).

## typealias36 - unresolved import qualifier cascade

- problem type: unresolved import qualifier cascade / import failure suppresses usage-site unresolved reference.
- root cause: failed non-star import binding with the same effective name was not represented as an imported package qualifier, so `pkga` in `pkga.AA` entered ordinary unresolved value lookup and produced an extra usage-site unresolved reference after the import checker had already reported the failed import.
- official Cangjie evidence: `cjc` reports only `package_search_error` on `import pkga`; `external/cangjie_compiler/src/Modules/ImportManager.cpp` owns the import failure, and ordinary unresolved-reference handling in `external/cangjie_compiler/src/Sema/TypeCheckReference.cpp` does not add a second usage-site diagnostic for this case.
- Kotlin counterpart files consulted: FIR error-node diagnostic collection suppresses receiver cascade diagnostics through `FirErrorNodeDiagnosticCollectorComponent`; package qualifier structure follows the `FirResolvedQualifier` role instead of treating the qualifier as an ordinary value lookup.
- CFIR owner files changed: `common/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/source/CjSourceElement.kt`, `cfir/resolve/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/resolve/body/CfirImportedPackageQualifier.kt`, `cfir/resolve/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/resolve/body/CfirCallResolver.kt`, `cfir/checkers/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/analysis/collectors/components/ErrorNodeDiagnosticCollectorComponent.kt`.
- repair principle: model an unresolved imported package qualifier as an already-reported error receiver at the shared import-qualifier/call-resolver boundary, so downstream member access and error collection see the real owner diagnostic instead of re-resolving the qualifier as a value.
- fixtures covered: `CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt._typealias.TypealiasGenerated.testTypealias36`, `CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt._typealias.TypealiasGenerated.testTypealias36`.
- verification command: `.\gradlew.bat :cfir:analysis-tests:test --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt._typealias.TypealiasGenerated.testTypealias36' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt._typealias.TypealiasGenerated.testTypealias36' --no-daemon --max-workers=1 --console=plain`
- verification outcome: `BUILD SUCCESSFUL`; `2 tests completed`.

## typealias14 - typealias declaration-site unresolved type cascade

- problem type: unresolved expanded type in a typealias declaration should report the root undeclared type once and suppress nested type-argument/use-site cascades.
- root cause: CFIR reported every nested `CfirErrorTypeRef` under a failed typealias expanded type and did not treat `ConeUnresolvedTypeQualifierError` carried through a typealias expansion as an unresolved receiver, so `type x = List<T>` produced extra `T` and `a.size` diagnostics after the root `List` error.
- official Cangjie evidence: `cjc` reports only `sema_undeclared_type_name` on `List`; `external/cangjie_compiler/src/Sema/PreCheck.cpp` reports unresolved reference types at the root type reference during precheck.
- Kotlin counterpart files consulted: `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/checkers/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/analysis/collectors/components/ErrorNodeDiagnosticCollectorComponent.kt`, especially `FirErrorTypeRef.hasExpandedTypeAliasDeclarationSiteError()` and receiver `cannotBeResolved()` cascade suppression.
- CFIR owner files changed: `cfir/checkers/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/analysis/collectors/components/ErrorNodeDiagnosticCollectorComponent.kt`.
- repair principle: keep the diagnostic owner at the typealias expanded-type root and make collector-level cascade suppression recognize unresolved classifier errors flowing through typealias expansion.
- fixtures covered: `CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt._typealias.TypealiasGenerated.testTypealias14`, `CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt._typealias.TypealiasGenerated.testTypealias14`.
- verification command: `.\gradlew.bat :cfir:analysis-tests:test --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt._typealias.TypealiasGenerated.testTypealias14' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt._typealias.TypealiasGenerated.testTypealias14' --no-daemon --max-workers=1 --console=plain`
- verification outcome: XML result after the Gradle wrapper process exited showed both `testTypealias14` entries passed, `failures=0 tests=1` for both PSI and non-PSI generated classes.
- regression command: `.\gradlew.bat :cfir:analysis-tests:test --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt._typealias.TypealiasGenerated' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt._typealias.TypealiasGenerated' --no-daemon --max-workers=1 --console=plain`
- regression outcome: `84 tests completed, 15 failed`; `typealias14` and `typealias36` are no longer in the remaining failure list.

## typealias3/typealias4 - binary operator mismatch diagnostic classification

- problem type: operator-origin binary calls with a candidate but incompatible operand types must report invalid binary operator, not ordinary argument type mismatch.
- root cause: CFIR mapped `ConstraintMismatch` on the right operand of an operator-origin call through the ordinary argument mismatch path, because the semantic call abstraction did not expose call origin to the diagnostics layer.
- official Cangjie evidence: `cjc` reports `type arg(s) ... are not used` on the `type` declaration and `invalid binary operator '+' on type 'Int64' and 'Int32'` on `a + b` for both fixtures; `external/cangjie_compiler/src/Sema/TypeCheckExpr/BinaryExpr.cpp` calls `DiagnoseForBinaryExpr` for invalid outermost binary expressions, and `external/cangjie_compiler/src/Sema/TypeCheckExpr/Diags.cpp` emits `DiagKindRefactor::sema_invalid_binary_expr`.
- Kotlin counterpart files consulted: `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/checkers/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/analysis/diagnostics/coneDiagnosticToFirDiagnostic.kt`, where inapplicable candidate diagnostics are mapped centrally from call/candidate diagnostics rather than in fixture-specific checkers.
- CFIR owner files changed: `cfir/semantics/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/semantics/AbstractCallInfo.kt`, `cfir/resolve/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/resolve/calls/candidate/CallInfo.kt`, `cfir/checkers/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/analysis/diagnostics/coneDiagnosticToCfirDiagnostic.kt`.
- repair principle: carry call origin through the shared semantic call-info abstraction so diagnostics can classify all operator-origin binary operand mismatches at the central candidate-diagnostic mapper.
- fixtures covered: `CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt._typealias.TypealiasGenerated.testTypealias3`, `CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt._typealias.TypealiasGenerated.testTypealias4`, `CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt._typealias.TypealiasGenerated.testTypealias3`, `CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt._typealias.TypealiasGenerated.testTypealias4`.
- verification command: `.\gradlew.bat :cfir:analysis-tests:test --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt._typealias.TypealiasGenerated.testTypealias3' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt._typealias.TypealiasGenerated.testTypealias4' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt._typealias.TypealiasGenerated.testTypealias3' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt._typealias.TypealiasGenerated.testTypealias4' --no-daemon --max-workers=1 --console=plain`
- verification outcome: outer command timed out after 240 seconds, but the Gradle Java worker completed; XML showed PSI and non-PSI generated classes both ran `testTypealias3` and `testTypealias4` with `failures=0 errors=0`.
- regression command: `.\gradlew.bat :cfir:analysis-tests:test --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt._typealias.TypealiasGenerated' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt._typealias.TypealiasGenerated' --no-daemon --max-workers=1 --console=plain`
- regression outcome: `84 tests completed, 11 failed`; `typealias3` and `typealias4` are no longer in the remaining failure list.

## typealias_partial_infer_01/typealias_partial_infer_03 - partial static qualifier inference

- problem type: partial generic typealias/subclass static qualifier calls must use the qualifier as a static base expression for owner inference, not as an ordinary runtime dispatch receiver.
- root cause: CFIR correctly carried `C<T>` and `I2<K>` through qualifier scope, owner use-site substitution, and fresh variable creation, but `CfirCheckDispatchReceiver` still checked the class/typealias qualifier expression against the callable dispatch receiver type as a normal value receiver. That early receiver applicability check made the candidate contradictory before argument constraints could infer the owner parameter from `C.test(1, 2)` or `I2.f3(1, Int32(1))`, producing cascading `ARGUMENT_TYPE_MISMATCH` and `INVALID_BINARY_OPERATOR`.
- official Cangjie evidence: `cjc` accepts both `typealias_partial_infer_01.cj` and `typealias_partial_infer_03.cj` with only unused-variable warnings; `external/cangjie_compiler/src/Sema/TypeCheckCall.cpp` `CheckAndGetMappingForTypeDecl` documents `I2<K> <: I1<Int64, K>` and `I2.f3(1, Int32(1))`, deriving `F -> Int64`, `H -> Int32`, then `K -> H`; `external/cangjie_compiler/src/Sema/TypeCheckUtil.cpp` `IsEnumCtorWithoutTypeArgs` documents partial typealias access continuing into type inference; `external/cangjie_compiler/src/Sema/TypeCheckExpr.cpp` `HandleAlias` and `TypeCheckerImpl.h` `GenerateTypeMappingForTypeAliasUse`/`SubstituteTypeArguments` preserve alias-generated missing type arguments for further instantiation.
- Kotlin counterpart files consulted: `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/resolve/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/resolve/calls/QualifierReceiver.kt`, `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/resolve/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/resolve/calls/tower/FirTowerResolveTask.kt`, `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/resolve/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/resolve/calls/tower/TowerLevels.kt`, `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/resolve/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/resolve/calls/stages/CreateFreshTypeVariableSubstitutorStage.kt`, `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/resolve/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/resolve/calls/stages/CheckArguments.kt`.
- CFIR owner files changed: `cfir/resolve/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/resolve/ResolveUtils.kt`, `cfir/resolve/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/resolve/calls/stages/CfirCheckDispatchReceiver.kt`.
- repair principle: keep class/typealias qualifiers as static base expressions that feed owner substitution and fresh-variable inference, while leaving ordinary instance dispatch receiver checks unchanged.
- fixtures covered: `CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt._typealias.TypealiasGenerated.testTypealiasPartialInfer01`, `CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt._typealias.TypealiasGenerated.testTypealiasPartialInfer03`, `CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt._typealias.TypealiasGenerated.testTypealiasPartialInfer01`, `CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt._typealias.TypealiasGenerated.testTypealiasPartialInfer03`.
- verification command: `.\gradlew.bat :cfir:analysis-tests:test --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt._typealias.TypealiasGenerated.testTypealiasPartialInfer01' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt._typealias.TypealiasGenerated.testTypealiasPartialInfer03' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt._typealias.TypealiasGenerated.testTypealiasPartialInfer01' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt._typealias.TypealiasGenerated.testTypealiasPartialInfer03' --no-daemon --max-workers=1 --console=plain`
- verification outcome: outer command timed out after 240 seconds, but the Gradle Java worker completed; XML showed PSI and non-PSI generated classes both ran `testTypealiasPartialInfer01` and `testTypealiasPartialInfer03` with `failures=0 errors=0`.
- regression command: `.\gradlew.bat :cfir:analysis-tests:test --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt._typealias.TypealiasGenerated' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt._typealias.TypealiasGenerated' --no-daemon --max-workers=1 --console=plain`
- regression outcome: `84 tests completed, 7 failed`; `typealias_partial_infer_01` and `typealias_partial_infer_03` are no longer in the remaining failure list. Remaining failures are PSI-only `typealias21`, PSI/non-PSI `typealias29`, and suspended `typealias30`/`typealias35`.

## typealias30/typealias35 - nested typealias constructor assignment mismatch

- problem type: nested typealias constructor calls must stay applicable and let the outer variable initializer compatibility check report the official `TYPE_MISMATCH`.
- root cause: CFIR added an extra extend-derived upper-bound constraint while creating fresh type variables for typealias constructor candidates, turning `C<Int64>()` into an inapplicable constructor call before the assignment checker could compare the constructor result with `InterfaceI<Int64>`.
- official Cangjie evidence: `cjc` reports `mismatched types` on `C<Int64>()` in both `typealias30` and `typealias35`, with expected `Interface-InterfaceI<Int64>` and found `Class-GennericClassA<Struct-Array<Generics-X>, Struct-Array<Generics-X>>`; `external/cangjie_compiler/src/Sema/TypeCheckExpr.cpp` `HandleAlias`, `TypeCheckerImpl.h` `GenerateTypeMappingForTypeAliasUse`/`SubstituteTypeArguments`, and `TypeManager.cpp` `SubstituteTypeAliasInTy` show alias use rewrites the real target/type arguments before ordinary compatibility checking.
- Kotlin counterpart files consulted: `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/providers/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/scopes/impl/TypeAliasConstructorsSubstitutingScope.kt`, `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/resolve/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/resolve/calls/stages/CreateFreshTypeVariableSubstitutorStage.kt`, `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/providers/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/resolve/TypeExpansionUtils.kt`, `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/checkers/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/analysis/checkers/expression/FirUpperBoundViolatedQualifiedAccessExpressionChecker.kt`.
- CFIR owner files changed: `cfir/resolve/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/resolve/calls/stages/CfirCreateFreshTypeVariableSubstitutorStage.kt`, `cfir/resolve/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/resolve/inference/CfirCallCompleter.kt`, `cfir/checkers/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/analysis/checkers/declaration/CfirPatternVariableInitializerTypeMismatchChecker.kt`.
- repair principle: keep typealias constructor candidate initialization aligned with the expanded-class upper-bound check and leave extend/assignment compatibility to their shared owners, so constructor applicability diagnostics do not preempt the outer initializer mismatch.
- fixtures covered: `CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt._typealias.TypealiasGenerated.testTypealias30`, `CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt._typealias.TypealiasGenerated.testTypealias35`, `CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt._typealias.TypealiasGenerated.testTypealias30`, `CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt._typealias.TypealiasGenerated.testTypealias35`; related nested extend assignment fixture `CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt.assign.AssignGenerated.testAssign012`, `CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt.assign.AssignGenerated.testAssign012`.
- verification command: `.\gradlew.bat :cfir:analysis-tests:test --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt._typealias.TypealiasGenerated.testTypealias30' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt._typealias.TypealiasGenerated.testTypealias30' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt._typealias.TypealiasGenerated.testTypealias35' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt._typealias.TypealiasGenerated.testTypealias35' --console=plain`
- verification outcome: `BUILD SUCCESSFUL`; `testTypealias30` and `testTypealias35` passed in both PSI and non-PSI suites.
- related verification command: `.\gradlew.bat :cfir:analysis-tests:test --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt.assign.AssignGenerated.testAssign012' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt.assign.AssignGenerated.testAssign012' --console=plain`
- related verification outcome: `BUILD SUCCESSFUL`; `testAssign012` passed in both PSI and non-PSI suites.
- regression command: `.\gradlew.bat :cfir:analysis-tests:test --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt._typealias.TypealiasGenerated' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt._typealias.TypealiasGenerated' --console=plain`
- regression outcome: `84 tests completed, 4 failed`; `typealias30` and `typealias35` are no longer in the remaining failure list. Remaining failures are PSI/non-PSI `typealias29` and PSI/non-PSI `typealias37`.

## typealias37 - typealias declaration-site invalid expanded type diagnostics

- problem type: a typealias whose RHS resolves to an invalid expanded type must report both the inner unresolved type argument and the declaration-site RHS root `NOT_A_TYPE` diagnostic.
- root cause: CFIR's error-node collector treated a parent `ConeUnreportedDuplicateDiagnostic(T)` wrapper as if it were the real unresolved RHS root, suppressing the inner `T` diagnostic; CFIR also had no checker equivalent to official `CheckTypeAliasDecl` that reports `not a type` on the RHS root after typealias RHS type construction fails because of an inner invalid type.
- official Cangjie evidence: `cjc typealias37_probe.cj --error-count-limit all` reports four diagnostics for `type bb = List1<T>` and `type x = List<T>`: `undeclared type name 'T'` at both `T` tokens, then `'List1' is not a type` and `'List' is not a type` at the RHS roots. `external/cangjie_compiler/src/Sema/PreCheck.cpp` reports `sema_undeclared_type_name`/`sema_not_a_type` during `GetTyFromASTType(RefType&)` at lines 423-425, and `CheckTypeAliasDecl` reports `sema_not_a_type` on an incorrect typealias RHS at line 755.
- Kotlin counterpart files consulted: `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/checkers/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/analysis/collectors/components/ErrorNodeDiagnosticCollectorComponent.kt`, `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/checkers/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/analysis/collectors/AbstractDiagnosticCollectorVisitor.kt`, `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/resolve/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/resolve/transformers/FirSpecificTypeResolverTransformer.kt`, `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/tree/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/types/impl/FirErrorTypeRefImpl.kt`.

## generic_interface_inheritance - instantiated duplicate super-interface and substituted inherited members

- problem type: generic interface inheritance must diagnose duplicated instantiated super-interfaces at the declaration owner and resolve inherited generic member signatures after owner substitution.
- root cause: CFIR split this family across local checks: direct supertype duplicate checking only compared immediate concrete type refs, generic instantiation had its own traversal that did not match the official per-declaration recursive interface check, inherited interface signatures were compared without preserving the generic owner form, substitution overrides participated in redeclaration collection, and fast corresponding-supertype lookup stopped at direct type-aware supertypes.
- official Cangjie evidence: `external/cangjie_compiler/src/Sema/TypeChecker.cpp` implements the semantic owner in `GetDupInterfaceRecursively`, `GetDupSuperInterface`, and `CheckInstDupSuperInterfaces`, recursively checking instantiated interface supertypes while sharing `passedClassLikeDecls`.
- Kotlin counterpart files consulted: `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/providers/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/resolve/LookupTagUtils.kt`, `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/providers/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/scopes/FirTypeParameterScope.kt`.
- CFIR owner files changed: `cfir/checkers/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/analysis/checkers/declaration/CfirDuplicateSuperInterfaceUtils.kt`, `cfir/checkers/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/analysis/checkers/declaration/CfirSupertypesChecker.kt`, `cfir/checkers/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/analysis/checkers/declaration/CfirGenericInstantiationChecker.kt`, `cfir/checkers/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/analysis/checkers/declaration/CfirDeclarationDiagnosticSources.kt`, `cfir/checkers/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/analysis/checkers/declaration/CfirConflictsHelpers.kt`, `cfir/providers/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/types/ConeTypeContext.kt`, `cfir/providers/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/scopes/CfirOverrideSignatureUtils.kt`, `cfir/providers/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/scopes/impl/CfirClassUseSiteMemberScope.kt`, `cfir/analysis-tests/testData/llt/interface/generic_interface_inheritance/interface_generic3.cj`, `cfir/analysis-tests/testData/llt/interface/generic_interface_inheritance/interface_generic4.cj`, `cfir/analysis-tests/testData/llt/interface/generic_interface_inheritance/interface_generic5.cj`, `cfir/analysis-tests/testData/llt/interface/generic_interface_inheritance/interface_generic6.cj`, `cfir/analysis-tests/testData/llt/interface/generic_interface_inheritance/interface_generic7.cj`.
- repair principle: put instantiated duplicate-super-interface traversal in a shared declaration-checker utility and make inherited member/type lookup consume owner-substituted types, so both direct supertypes and generic instantiation use the same CFIR framework path instead of fixture-local checks.
- fixtures covered: `interface_generic1.cj`, `interface_generic2.cj`, `interface_generic3.cj`, `interface_generic4.cj`, `interface_generic5.cj`, `interface_generic6.cj`, `interface_generic7.cj`, `interface_generic8.cj`, `interface_generic9.cj`, `interface_generic10.cj`, `interface_generic11.cj`, `interface_generic12.cj`, `interface_generic13.cj`, `interface_generic14.cj`, `interface_generic15.cj`, `interface_generic21.cj`; verified through `CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt._interface.generic_interface_inheritance.GenericInterfaceInheritanceGenerated` and `CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt._interface.generic_interface_inheritance.GenericInterfaceInheritanceGenerated`.
- verification command: `.\gradlew.bat :cfir:analysis-tests:test --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt._interface.generic_interface_inheritance.GenericInterfaceInheritanceGenerated' --no-daemon --max-workers=1 --console=plain --stacktrace`
- verification outcome: `BUILD SUCCESSFUL`; non-PSI `GenericInterfaceInheritanceGenerated` passed, `17` tests.
- verification command: `.\gradlew.bat :cfir:analysis-tests:test --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt._interface.generic_interface_inheritance.GenericInterfaceInheritanceGenerated' --no-daemon --max-workers=1 --console=plain --stacktrace`
- verification outcome: `BUILD SUCCESSFUL`; PSI `GenericInterfaceInheritanceGenerated` passed, `17` tests.

## operator - primitive exponentiation, numeric overflow, and tuple equality diagnostics

- problem type: primitive operator LLT diagnostics must match official Cangjie for exponentiation operand selection, integer literal overflow, tuple equality, and operator-origin mismatch classification.
- root cause: CFIR treated exponentiation as an ordinary binary call after resolving children, so RHS expected-type failures leaked as child `TYPE_MISMATCH`, `IdealFloat ** Float64` stayed valid after overload resolution, and `Int64 ** UInt64` / `Float64 ** Int64|Float64` did not share the official builtin exponentiation table. Constant-eval overflow and literal overflow also read explicit initializer types too broadly, and tuple equality mismatches were mapped as ordinary argument mismatches.
- official Cangjie evidence: `cjc` accepts `pow_float64_infer.cj` and rejects `pow_infer_err.cj` with `invalid binary operator '**' on type 'Float64' and 'Float64'`; `external/cangjie_compiler/src/Sema/BuiltInOperatorUtil.cpp` `SEMA_EXP_TYPES` lists `Int64 ** UInt64 -> Int64` and `Float64 ** Int64|Float64 -> Float64`; `external/cangjie_compiler/src/Sema/TypeCheckExpr/BinaryExpr.cpp` `ChkExpoExpr`, `ChkExpoExprBase`, and `ChkExpoExprExponent` own exponentiation operand checking and parent binary-expression diagnostics.
- Kotlin counterpart files consulted: `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/resolve/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/resolve/calls/FirCallResolver.kt`, `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/resolve/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/resolve/transformers/FirCallCompletionResultsWriterTransformer.kt`, `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/checkers/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/analysis/diagnostics/coneDiagnosticToFirDiagnostic.kt`.
- CFIR owner files changed: `cfir/cfir-cones/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/types/BuiltinPrimitiveOperators.kt`, `cfir/resolve/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/resolve/body/CfirExpressionsResolveTransformer.kt`, `cfir/resolve/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/resolve/body/CfirCallResolver.kt`, `cfir/checkers/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/analysis/checkers/expression/CfirConstEvalArithmeticChecker.kt`, `cfir/checkers/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/analysis/checkers/expression/CfirLiteralNumericOverflowChecker.kt`, `cfir/checkers/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/analysis/diagnostics/coneDiagnosticToCfirDiagnostic.kt`.
- repair principle: put primitive operator semantics in the shared builtin/operator resolve path and keep overflow/diagnostic classification in shared checkers/mappers, so PSI and non-PSI LLT paths consume the same CFIR owner behavior instead of fixture-local handling.
- fixtures covered: complete `CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt.operator.OperatorGenerated` and `CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt.operator.OperatorGenerated` slices, including `pow_infer_err`, `pow_float64_infer`, `pow_float64_int64_float64_0`, `pow_int64_infer`, `pow_int64_uint64_0`, `pow_overflow`, `add_overflow`, `sub_overflow_01`, `mod_overflow`, tuple equality, and related operator fixtures.
- verification command: `.\gradlew.bat :cfir:analysis-tests:test --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt.operator.OperatorGenerated' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt.operator.OperatorGenerated' --no-daemon --max-workers=1 --console=plain --stacktrace`
- verification outcome: `BUILD SUCCESSFUL`; XML showed non-PSI `OperatorGenerated` `tests=27 failures=0 errors=0` and PSI `OperatorGenerated` `tests=27 failures=0 errors=0`.
- full regression command: `.\gradlew.bat :cfir:analysis-tests:test --no-daemon --max-workers=1 --console=plain --stacktrace`
- full regression outcome: the tool command timed out after 604 seconds, but the Gradle test worker continued and exited; XML after worker exit showed `8756` completed tests, `3217` failures, and `0` errors. The completed `OperatorGenerated` PSI and non-PSI XMLs remained green.

## Extend RETURN_TYPE_INVARIANCE - extend/boxing return type is not valid override covariance

- problem type: extend/interface implementation must reject a concrete class/struct return type when it relies on an extend relation to satisfy an interface return type.
- root cause: CFIR only checked ordinary subtype compatibility for override/implement return types, so extend-provided interface conformance such as `String <: Tokens<Rune>`, `StringBuilder <: I`, or `String <: LiteralValue` was accepted as return-type covariance instead of being reported by the inheritance checker.
- official Cangjie evidence: `cjc --diagnostic-format json tmp/cfir_extend_return_invariance_01.cj` reported `sema_return_type_invariance` on `dropFirst`; `tmp/cfir_extend_return_invariance_03.cj` reported `sema_return_type_invariance` on standard `StringBuilder.append`; `tmp/cfir_extend_return_invariance_04.cj` reported `sema_return_type_invariance` on standard `String.clone`. `external/cangjie_compiler/src/Sema/InheritanceChecker/StructInheritanceChecker.cpp` reports `sema_return_type_invariance` from `StructInheritanceChecker::CheckImplementationRelation`, and `external/cangjie_compiler/src/Sema/TypeManager.cpp` `TypeManager::HasExtensionRelation` defines the extend/interface relation used by that diagnostic.
- Kotlin counterpart files consulted: `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/checkers/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/analysis/checkers/declaration/FirOverrideChecker.kt`, especially the declaration-checker placement of `checkReturnType` / `RETURN_TYPE_MISMATCH_ON_OVERRIDE`.
- CFIR owner files changed: `cfir/checkers/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/analysis/checkers/declaration/CfirInheritanceDeepChecker.kt`, `cfir/checkers/checkers-component-generator/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/checkers/generator/diagnostics/CfirDiagnosticsList.kt`, `cfir/checkers/gen/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/analysis/diagnostics/CfirErrors.kt`, `cfir/checkers/gen/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/analysis/diagnostics/CfirNonSuppressibleErrorNames.kt`, `cfir/checkers/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/analysis/diagnostics/CfirErrorsDefaultMessages.kt`, `cfir/analysis-tests/testFixtures/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/analysis/tests/golden/DiagnosticNameMapper.kt`, `cfir/analysis-tests/testData/llt/Extend/invariant_override_returntype_01.cj`, `cfir/analysis-tests/testData/llt/Extend/invariant_override_returntype_03.cj`, `cfir/analysis-tests/testData/llt/Extend/invariant_override_returntype_04.cj`.
- repair principle: put official return-type invariance detection in the shared inheritance/override checker and reuse extend-provider substitution to recognize all extend-derived interface returns, covering both explicit extend members and existing/std members used to implement an extend interface.
- fixtures covered: `CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated.testInvariantOverrideReturntype01`, `CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated.testInvariantOverrideReturntype01`, `CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated.testInvariantOverrideReturntype03`, `CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated.testInvariantOverrideReturntype03`, `CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated.testInvariantOverrideReturntype04`, `CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated.testInvariantOverrideReturntype04`.
- verification command: `.\gradlew.bat :cfir:analysis-tests:test --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated.testInvariantOverrideReturntype01' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated.testInvariantOverrideReturntype01' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated.testInvariantOverrideReturntype03' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated.testInvariantOverrideReturntype03' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated.testInvariantOverrideReturntype04' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated.testInvariantOverrideReturntype04' --no-daemon --max-workers=1 --console=plain --stacktrace`
- verification outcome: `BUILD SUCCESSFUL`; XML showed non-PSI `ExtendGenerated` selected tests `tests=3 failures=0 errors=0` and PSI `ExtendGenerated` selected tests `tests=3 failures=0 errors=0`.
- regression command: `.\gradlew.bat :cfir:analysis-tests:test --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated' --no-daemon --max-workers=1 --console=plain --stacktrace`
- regression outcome: `328 tests completed, 137 failed`; `testInvariantOverrideReturntype01`, `testInvariantOverrideReturntype03`, and `testInvariantOverrideReturntype04` stayed green in the earlier selected XML, while remaining Extend failures include the separate `testInvariantOverrideReturntype02` generic-instantiation ambiguity family and other unrelated Extend problem types.

## Extend generic-instantiation ambiguous functions - instantiated generic members collide after substitution

- problem type: generic instantiation must report `GENERIC_INSTANTIATION_CAUSES_AMBIGUOUS_FUNCTIONS` when inherited or extended generic member signatures become identical after type-argument substitution.
- root cause: CFIR split instantiated member signatures into stable and generic buckets, but compared each generic signature only against the original stable bucket. Two inherited generic interface members such as `I2<K>.test(K): Int64` and `I1<T>.test(T): Int32` were both substituted to `test(Int32)` for `A<Int32, Int32>` and then skipped because no already-stable member existed. Static functions were also filtered before signature comparison, while the official rule compares static-status equality instead of dropping static members.
- official Cangjie evidence: `cjc --diagnostic-format json tmp/cfir_extend_generic_instantiation_ambiguity_02.cj` reported `sema_generic_instantiation_causes_ambiguous_functions` on `A<Int32, Int32>` with notes on both `test` candidates. `external/cangjie_compiler/src/Sema/InheritanceChecker/InstantiatedChecker.cpp` `StructInheritanceChecker::CheckInstMemberSignatures` moves generic-typed members into a separate container, instantiates one member, compares it against the current member table, then inserts it back; `DiagnoseForInstantiatedMember` reports when both members are functions, static status matches, and parameter types are identical.
- Kotlin counterpart files consulted: `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/checkers/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/analysis/checkers/declaration/FirImplementationMismatchChecker.kt`, `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/providers/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/declarations/declarationUtils.kt`.
- CFIR owner files changed: `cfir/checkers/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/analysis/checkers/declaration/CfirGenericInstantiationChecker.kt`.
- repair principle: keep instantiated member-signature collision detection in the shared generic-instantiation checker and mirror the official member-table flow by comparing each instantiated generic signature with all previously accepted signatures, including earlier generic signatures and static signatures with matching static status.
- fixtures covered: `CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated.testInvariantOverrideReturntype02`, `CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated.testInvariantOverrideReturntype02`, `testExtendInstantiationFunctionAmbiguous1`, `testExtendInstantiationFunctionAmbiguous2`, `testExtendInstantiationFunctionAmbiguous3`, and `testExtendInstantiationFunctionAmbiguous4` in both PSI and non-PSI suites.
- verification command: `.\gradlew.bat :cfir:analysis-tests:test --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated.testInvariantOverrideReturntype02' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated.testInvariantOverrideReturntype02' --no-daemon --max-workers=1 --console=plain --stacktrace`
- verification outcome: `BUILD SUCCESSFUL`; `testInvariantOverrideReturntype02` passed in both PSI and non-PSI suites.
- related verification command: `.\gradlew.bat :cfir:analysis-tests:test --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated.testInvariantOverrideReturntype02' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated.testInvariantOverrideReturntype02' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated.testExtendInstantiationFunctionAmbiguous1' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated.testExtendInstantiationFunctionAmbiguous1' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated.testExtendInstantiationFunctionAmbiguous2' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated.testExtendInstantiationFunctionAmbiguous2' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated.testExtendInstantiationFunctionAmbiguous3' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated.testExtendInstantiationFunctionAmbiguous3' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated.testExtendInstantiationFunctionAmbiguous4' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated.testExtendInstantiationFunctionAmbiguous4' --no-daemon --max-workers=1 --console=plain --stacktrace`

## Extend member conflict - static/non-static mismatch and extend-member shadowing

- problem type: extend members whose names collide with extended-type members must report official extend shadow diagnostics, with static/non-static collisions separated from kind-shadow collisions.
- root cause: CFIR routed extend-member static mismatch through ordinary inherited-kind inconsistency and did not model the official extend-specific static/non-static diagnostic, so `extend_member_conflict2` missed or misclassified collisions such as `x1`, `y2`, `f1`, `g2`, `a`, `b`, and `c`.
- official Cangjie evidence: `cjc --diagnostic-format json --error-count-limit all tmp/cfir_extend_member_conflict2_probe.cj` reported `sema_static_and_non_static_member_cannot_have_same_name` for `x1`, `y2`, `f1`, and `g2`, and `sema_extend_member_cannot_shadow` for `x2`, `y1`, `f1`, `f2`, `g1`, `g2`, `a`, `b`, and `c`; `external/cangjie_compiler/src/Sema/InheritanceChecker/StructInheritanceChecker.cpp` `CheckExtendMemberValid` routes extend member kind/name conflicts to `sema_extend_member_cannot_shadow`, while `CheckSameNameInheritanceInfo` reports static/non-static same-name conflicts.
- Kotlin counterpart files consulted: `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/checkers/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/analysis/checkers/declaration/FirImplementationMismatchChecker.kt`, `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/providers/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/declarations/declarationUtils.kt`.
- CFIR owner files changed: `cfir/checkers/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/analysis/checkers/declaration/CfirInheritanceDeepChecker.kt`, `cfir/checkers/checkers-component-generator/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/checkers/generator/diagnostics/CfirDiagnosticsList.kt`, `cfir/checkers/gen/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/analysis/diagnostics/CfirErrors.kt`, `cfir/checkers/gen/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/analysis/diagnostics/CfirNonSuppressibleErrorNames.kt`, `cfir/checkers/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/analysis/diagnostics/CfirErrorsDefaultMessages.kt`, `cfir/analysis-tests/testFixtures/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/analysis/tests/golden/DiagnosticNameMapper.kt`, `cfir/analysis-tests/testData/llt/Extend/extend_member_conflict2.cj`.
- repair principle: keep extend-member collision handling in the shared inheritance checker and route extend-specific static/name/kind conflicts through diagnostics that match the official extend checker, instead of adapting one fixture's expected output.
- fixtures covered: `CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated.testExtendMemberConflict2`, `CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated.testExtendMemberConflict2`.
- verification command: `.\gradlew.bat :cfir:analysis-tests:test --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated.testExtendMemberConflict2' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated.testExtendMemberConflict2' --no-daemon --max-workers=1 --console=plain --stacktrace`
- verification outcome: `BUILD SUCCESSFUL`; `testExtendMemberConflict2` passed in both PSI and non-PSI suites.

## Extend immutable target inheriting mut interface - interface is not extendable

- problem type: immutable extend targets that implement an interface containing a `mut` property must report `EXTEND_INTERFACE_NOT_EXTENDABLE` on the whole extend declaration and must not also emit supertype generic upper-bound noise from the same invalid extend header.
- root cause: CFIR introduced a separate `EXTEND_IMMUTABLE_MUT_INTERFACE` diagnostic on the inherited interface type reference and allowed the `Range<T>` supertype's embedded error type to report `GENERIC_TYPE_ARGUMENT_NOT_MATCH_CONSTRAINT`, while the official checker owns this as one extend-declaration error.
- official Cangjie evidence: `cjc --diagnostic-format json --error-count-limit all tmp/cfir_extend_mut_interface01_probe.cj` and `tmp/cfir_extend_mut_interface03_probe.cj` reported only seven `sema_interface_is_not_extendable` diagnostics, each covering the full `extend ... {}` declaration and adding a note that interface `I` contains mutable member `p`; `external/cangjie_compiler/src/Sema/TypeCheckExtend.cpp` `TypeCheckerImpl::CheckImmutExtendInhertMutSuper` reports `sema_interface_is_not_extendable` on `ExtendDecl` and recurses through inherited interface supertypes.
- Kotlin counterpart files consulted: `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/checkers/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/analysis/checkers/declaration/FirSupertypesChecker.kt`, where supertype-list legality is owned by a declaration checker and reports against the corresponding supertype/declaration source instead of leaving downstream override or type-argument diagnostics to define the primary error.
- CFIR owner files changed: `cfir/checkers/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/analysis/checkers/CfirExtendSemantics.kt`, `cfir/checkers/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/analysis/checkers/declaration/CfirExtendCheckers.kt`, `cfir/checkers/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/analysis/checkers/type/CfirUpperBoundViolatedTypeChecker.kt`, `cfir/checkers/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/analysis/collectors/components/ErrorNodeDiagnosticCollectorComponent.kt`, `cfir/analysis-tests/testData/diagnostics/coverage/extensions/extendImmutableMutInterface.cj`.
- repair principle: centralize immutable-target/mut-interface recognition in `CfirExtendSemantics`, report the official extend-level diagnostic from the extend checker, and suppress only the duplicate generic-constraint diagnostics that originate inside the same invalid extend header.
- fixtures covered: `CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated.testExtendMutInterface01`, `CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated.testExtendMutInterface01`, `CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated.testExtendMutInterface03`, `CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated.testExtendMutInterface03`; related diagnostics coverage tests `CfirAnalysisDiagnosticsTest.diagnostics.coverage.extensions.ExtensionsGenerated.testExtendImmutableMutInterface`, `CfirAnalysisDiagnosticsPsiTest.diagnostics.coverage.extensions.ExtensionsGenerated.testExtendImmutableMutInterface`, and `CfirAnalysisDiagnosticsWithoutAliasExpansionTest.diagnostics.coverage.extensions.ExtensionsGenerated.testExtendImmutableMutInterface`.
- verification command: `.\gradlew.bat :cfir:analysis-tests:test --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated.testExtendMutInterface01' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated.testExtendMutInterface01' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated.testExtendMutInterface03' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated.testExtendMutInterface03' --no-daemon --max-workers=1 --console=plain --stacktrace`
- verification outcome: `BUILD SUCCESSFUL`; `testExtendMutInterface01` and `testExtendMutInterface03` passed in both PSI and non-PSI suites.
- related verification command: `.\gradlew.bat :cfir:analysis-tests:test --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisDiagnosticsTest.diagnostics.coverage.extensions.ExtensionsGenerated.testExtendImmutableMutInterface' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisDiagnosticsPsiTest.diagnostics.coverage.extensions.ExtensionsGenerated.testExtendImmutableMutInterface' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisDiagnosticsWithoutAliasExpansionTest.diagnostics.coverage.extensions.ExtensionsGenerated.testExtendImmutableMutInterface' --no-daemon --max-workers=1 --console=plain --stacktrace`
- related verification outcome: `BUILD SUCCESSFUL`; all three diagnostics coverage entries passed.

## Extend unresolved star import cascade - package import failure suppresses usage-site noise

- problem type: a failed package star import must report the import failure and must not cascade into unrelated unresolved type/name diagnostics for references that would have come from that import.
- root cause: CFIR collected error type references, error expressions, and error named references independently from file import binding state, so a failed star import such as `stdx.encoding.json.*` produced both the primary `UNRESOLVED_IMPORT` and downstream unresolved `JsonValue` / `JsonInt` / `JsonNull` diagnostics.
- official Cangjie evidence: `cjc --diagnostic-format json --error-count-limit all cfir\analysis-tests\testData\llt\Extend\extend_enum2.cj` reported only `package_search_error` on `stdx.encoding.json`; probe `tmp/cfir_unresolved_star_import_probe.cj` with `import missing.pkg.*` likewise reported only the package-search diagnostic and did not report unresolved usage-site names. `external/cangjie_compiler/src/Modules/ImportManager.cpp` owns package-search/import failure reporting.
- Kotlin counterpart files consulted: `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/checkers/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/analysis/checkers/declaration/FirImportsChecker.kt`, where import errors are file-level import checker diagnostics and unresolved import scopes do not provide symbols.
- CFIR owner files changed: `cfir/checkers/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/analysis/collectors/components/ErrorNodeDiagnosticCollectorComponent.kt`, `cfir/analysis-tests/testData/llt/Extend/extend_enum2.cj`.
- repair principle: use the shared diagnostic collector's file import-binding state to suppress only unresolved cascades caused by failed imports, keeping the primary import checker diagnostic as the framework owner instead of adapting one fixture.
- fixtures covered: `CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated.testExtendEnum2`, `CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated.testExtendEnum2`; related import diagnostics coverage `CfirAnalysisDiagnosticsTest.diagnostics.coverage.imports.ImportsGenerated.testImportTargetNotFoundRich`, `CfirAnalysisDiagnosticsPsiTest.diagnostics.coverage.imports.ImportsGenerated.testImportTargetNotFoundRich`, `CfirAnalysisDiagnosticsWithoutAliasExpansionTest.diagnostics.coverage.imports.ImportsGenerated.testImportTargetNotFoundRich`; related lookup cascade check `CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt.lookup.LookupGenerated.testBugfix2`, `CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt.lookup.LookupGenerated.testBugfix2`.
- verification command: `.\gradlew.bat :cfir:analysis-tests:test --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated.testExtendEnum2' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated.testExtendEnum2' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt.lookup.LookupGenerated.testBugfix2' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt.lookup.LookupGenerated.testBugfix2' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisDiagnosticsTest.diagnostics.coverage.imports.ImportsGenerated.testImportTargetNotFoundRich' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisDiagnosticsPsiTest.diagnostics.coverage.imports.ImportsGenerated.testImportTargetNotFoundRich' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisDiagnosticsWithoutAliasExpansionTest.diagnostics.coverage.imports.ImportsGenerated.testImportTargetNotFoundRich' --no-daemon --max-workers=1 --console=plain --stacktrace`
- verification outcome: `BUILD SUCCESSFUL`; `testExtendEnum2`, `testBugfix2`, and the three import coverage entries passed.
- regression command: `.\gradlew.bat :cfir:analysis-tests:test --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated' --no-daemon --max-workers=1 --console=plain --stacktrace`
- regression outcome: `328 tests completed, 123 failed`; XML showed non-PSI `ExtendGenerated` `tests=164 failures=62 errors=0` and PSI `ExtendGenerated` `tests=164 failures=61 errors=0`, and `testExtendEnum2` no longer appeared in either failure list.

## Extend unresolved type-name range - diagnostic covers the full missing type identifier

- problem type: range-only LLT mismatch for an unresolved multi-character type name inside an extend supertype argument.
- root cause: fixture `extend_fuzz1.cj` expected `UNDECLARED_TYPE_NAME` on only the first character of `Poit`, while the project Diagnostic Range Policy requires identifier/token diagnostics to cover the full relevant token.
- official Cangjie evidence: not used because this is range-only; the `cangjie-cfir-llt-repair` Diagnostic Range Policy is the controlling evidence and explicitly diverges from narrow `cjc` anchors for IDE-facing ranges.
- Kotlin counterpart files consulted: not required for this single-identifier range correction; the policy only requires Kotlin range-source tracing for constructs more complex than a single identifier.
- CFIR owner files changed: none; fixture correction only in `cfir/analysis-tests/testData/llt/Extend/extend_fuzz1.cj`.
- repair principle: keep CFIR's shared diagnostic range behavior intact and correct the LLT fixture to the project-wide full-token range policy instead of narrowing framework output to one fixture's stale expectation.
- fixtures covered: `CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated.testExtendFuzz1`, `CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated.testExtendFuzz1`; same-family search found other multi-character first-character `UNDECLARED_TYPE_NAME` expectations outside the current ordered Extend slice, but they were left for their own generated-class failures.
- verification command: `.\gradlew.bat :cfir:analysis-tests:test --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated.testExtendFuzz1' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated.testExtendFuzz1' --no-daemon --max-workers=1 --console=plain --stacktrace`
- verification outcome: `BUILD SUCCESSFUL`; `testExtendFuzz1` passed in both PSI and non-PSI suites.
- regression command: `.\gradlew.bat :cfir:analysis-tests:test --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated' --no-daemon --max-workers=1 --console=plain --stacktrace`
- regression outcome: `328 tests completed, 121 failed`; XML showed non-PSI `ExtendGenerated` `tests=164 failures=61 errors=0` and PSI `ExtendGenerated` `tests=164 failures=60 errors=0`, and `testExtendFuzz1` no longer appeared in either failure list.

## Extend static this usage - static function bodies cannot reference instance this

- problem type: `this` references inside static extend/class/interface function bodies must report `STATIC_MEMBERS_CANNOT_CALL_MEMBERS`, and default parameters on interface functions must report the official default-parameter legality diagnostic at the parameter owner.
- root cause: CFIR collected `CfirThisReference` diagnostics but did not have a shared expression checker for static-context `this` usage, so static extend functions accepted instance `this`; the default-parameter checker also only reported one declaration-level error and did not classify interface functions as abstract for default-parameter legality.
- official Cangjie evidence: `cjc --diagnostic-format json --error-count-limit all tmp/cfir_extend_static_this_probe.cj` reported `sema_static_members_cannot_call_members` for `this` in static extend functions; `cjc --diagnostic-format json --error-count-limit all tmp/cfir_static_this_default_param_probe.cj` reported `sema_cannot_have_default_param` on the interface static function parameter and `sema_static_members_cannot_call_members` on the static `this` uses. `external/cangjie_compiler/src/Sema/TypeCheckReference.cpp` `CheckUsageOfThis` reports `sema_static_members_cannot_call_members` when the current function body is static, and `external/cangjie_compiler/src/Sema/Diags.cpp` `DiagCannotHaveDefaultParam` reports default-parameter legality on `FuncParam` for abstract/open/operator contexts.
- Kotlin counterpart files consulted: `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/checkers/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/analysis/collectors/components/ErrorNodeDiagnosticCollectorComponent.kt`, where `visitThisReference` collects diagnostics from `FirThisReference`, matching CFIR's `CfirThisReference` diagnostic collection path.
- CFIR owner files changed: `cfir/checkers/checkers-component-generator/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/checkers/generator/diagnostics/CfirDiagnosticsList.kt`, `cfir/checkers/gen/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/analysis/diagnostics/CfirErrors.kt`, `cfir/checkers/gen/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/analysis/diagnostics/CfirNonSuppressibleErrorNames.kt`, `cfir/checkers/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/analysis/diagnostics/CfirErrorsDefaultMessages.kt`, `cfir/analysis-tests/testFixtures/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/analysis/tests/golden/DiagnosticNameMapper.kt`, `cfir/checkers/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/analysis/checkers/CommonExpressionCheckers.kt`, `cfir/checkers/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/analysis/checkers/expression/CfirExpressionSemanticsChecker.kt`, `cfir/checkers/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/analysis/checkers/declaration/CfirFunctionSemanticsChecker.kt`, `cfir/analysis-tests/testData/llt/Extend/extend_this5.cj`, `cfir/analysis-tests/testData/llt/class/class11_test2.cj`.
- repair principle: put static-context `this` detection in the shared expression-checker layer and keep default-parameter legality at the function-parameter checker owner, so extend, class, and interface functions share the same semantic path instead of fixture-local diagnostics.
- fixtures covered: `CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated.testExtendThis5`, `CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated.testExtendThis5`, `CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt._class.ClassGenerated.testClass11Test2`, `CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt._class.ClassGenerated.testClass11Test2`.
- verification command: `.\gradlew.bat :cfir:analysis-tests:test --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated.testExtendThis5' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated.testExtendThis5' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt._class.ClassGenerated.testClass11Test2' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt._class.ClassGenerated.testClass11Test2' --no-daemon --max-workers=1 --console=plain --stacktrace`
- verification outcome: `BUILD SUCCESSFUL`; `testExtendThis5` and `testClass11Test2` passed in both PSI and non-PSI suites.
- regression command: `.\gradlew.bat :cfir:analysis-tests:test --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated' --no-daemon --max-workers=1 --console=plain --stacktrace`
- regression outcome: `328 tests completed, 119 failed`; XML showed non-PSI `ExtendGenerated` `tests=164 failures=60 errors=0` and PSI `ExtendGenerated` `tests=164 failures=59 errors=0`, and `testExtendThis5` no longer appeared in either failure list.

## Match type-pattern binding - light-tree raw builder preserves typed case variables

- problem type: light-tree CFIR must create and register pattern binding variables for `case name: Type =>` type patterns so branch bodies resolve `name` in the same way as PSI CFIR.
- root cause: the PSI raw builder used `CjTypePattern.nameAsName`, which is backed by the direct `REFERENCE_EXPRESSION`, but the light-tree builder only searched a direct `IDENTIFIER` under `TYPE_PATTERN`; when the light tree represented the binding name as a `REFERENCE_EXPRESSION`, the `CfirTypePattern` lost `bindingName`/`bindingVariable`, so `registerPatternBindings()` had no `arr2` symbol to store before resolving the branch body.
- official Cangjie evidence: `cjc --diagnostic-format json --error-count-limit all cfir\analysis-tests\testData\llt\Extend\unbox_array.cj` produced no diagnostics; `external/cangjie_compiler/src/Sema/TypeCheckPattern.cpp` `ChkTypePattern` checks the annotated pattern against the matched type and then calls `ChkPattern` for the nested variable pattern, while `ChkVarPattern` assigns the target type to `varDecl` and makes the binding available.
- Kotlin counterpart files consulted: `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/raw-fir/psi2fir/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/builder/PsiRawFirBuilder.kt`, `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/raw-fir/psi2fir/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/builder/PsiConversionUtils.kt`; Kotlin `when` builds branch conditions from the subject and resolves the branch result in the normal local scope, which matches CFIR's shared `withNewLocalScope` plus pattern binding registration architecture.
- CFIR owner files changed: `cfir/raw-cfir/light-tree2cfir/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/lightTree/LightTreeRawCfirExpressionBuilder.kt`.
- repair principle: keep the existing shared `CfirPatternBindingVariable` registration path intact and make the light-tree type-pattern builder read the same binding-name node shape as the PSI builder, instead of suppressing unresolved references in the checker.
- fixtures covered: `CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated.testUnboxArray`, `CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated.testUnboxArray`; broader Extend `testUnbox*` was also rerun to confirm remaining unbox failures are separate enum/tuple/option pattern semantics and not this typed-binding owner.
- verification command: `.\gradlew.bat :cfir:analysis-tests:test --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated.testUnboxArray' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated.testUnboxArray' --no-daemon --max-workers=1 --console=plain --stacktrace`
- verification outcome: `BUILD SUCCESSFUL`; `testUnboxArray` passed in both PSI and non-PSI suites.
- related verification command: `.\gradlew.bat :cfir:analysis-tests:test --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated.testUnbox*' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated.testUnbox*' --no-daemon --max-workers=1 --console=plain --stacktrace`
- related verification outcome: failed with `14 tests completed, 12 failed`; XML showed `testUnboxArray` had `failure=false`, while the remaining `testUnbox*` failures were enum/tuple/option pattern cases.
- regression command: `.\gradlew.bat :cfir:analysis-tests:test --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated' --no-daemon --max-workers=1 --console=plain --stacktrace`
- regression outcome: `328 tests completed, 117 failed`; XML showed non-PSI `ExtendGenerated` `tests=164 failures=58 errors=0` and PSI `ExtendGenerated` `tests=164 failures=59 errors=0`, and `testUnboxArray` no longer appeared in either failure list.

## Static/non-static function overload conflicts - extend members use declaration owner scope

- problem type: static functions must report `STATIC_FUNCTION_OVERLOAD_CONFLICTS` when they share the same name with a non-static function in the same declaration owner, including `extend` declarations whose callable ids do not carry a physical class owner.
- root cause: `CfirFunctionOverloadChecker` located the comparison scope from `function.symbol.callableId.classId`, which misses extend-member owners; it also did not restrict reporting to the static declaration even though the official checker reports each static function against the first non-static function in the same name group.
- official Cangjie evidence: `cjc --diagnostic-format json --error-count-limit all tmp/cfir_extend_conflict_interface_probe.cj` reported two `sema_static_function_overload_conflicts` diagnostics on the static `me` declarations and only noted the non-static `me`. `external/cangjie_compiler/src/Sema/PreCheck.cpp` `PreCheckFuncStaticConflict` groups same-name functions by scope, collects static functions, finds the first non-static function, and reports the static declarations through `DiagStaticAndNonStaticOverload`; `external/cangjie_compiler/src/Sema/Diags.cpp` defines that diagnostic emission.
- Kotlin counterpart files consulted: `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/checkers/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/analysis/checkers/declaration/FirConflictsDeclarationChecker.kt`, `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/checkers/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/analysis/checkers/FirConflictsHelpers.kt`.
- CFIR owner files changed: `cfir/checkers/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/analysis/checkers/declaration/CfirFunctionSemanticsChecker.kt`.
- repair principle: keep the diagnostic in the shared function semantics checker and derive comparison candidates from the current checker declaration owner (`CfirClassLikeDeclaration` or `CfirExtend`) instead of reverse-mapping callable ids, so class and extend functions use the same owner-scope rule.
- fixtures covered: `CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated.testExtendConflictInterface`, `CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated.testExtendConflictInterface`, `CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt.Extend.generic.GenericGenerated.testStaticNonstaticFuncConflict`, `CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt.Extend.generic.GenericGenerated.testStaticNonstaticFuncConflict`. Related file `cfir/analysis-tests/testData/llt/function/member_func_overload_1.cj` still contains a matching `STATIC_FUNCTION_OVERLOAD_CONFLICTS` marker, but its generated test remains red for the separate inherited static/non-static member-kind diagnostic (`INHERIT_MEMBER_KIND_INCONSISTENT` vs `STATIC_AND_NON_STATIC_MEMBER_CANNOT_HAVE_SAME_NAME`), so it is not counted as a closed fixture in this entry.
- verification command: `.\gradlew.bat :cfir:analysis-tests:test --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated.testExtendConflictInterface' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated.testExtendConflictInterface' --no-daemon --max-workers=1 --console=plain --stacktrace`
- verification outcome: `BUILD SUCCESSFUL`; `testExtendConflictInterface` passed in both PSI and non-PSI suites.
