## Binary operator diagnostics with classifier operands and hidden operator candidates

- problem type: binary expressions such as `A + A` must keep both operand `REF_NOT_BE_TYPE` diagnostics and the outer `INVALID_BINARY_OPERATOR`, while hidden/private operator candidates such as `A() - A()` must be reported as invalid binary operator calls instead of no-match or visibility/private diagnostics.
- root cause: `CfirClassifierAsExpressionChecker` skipped all `CfirFunctionCall` nodes and treated the left operand of a plain operator call as an outer qualified-access receiver, so `A + A` lost the left `REF_NOT_BE_TYPE` and outer binary diagnostic. Separately, `ConeHiddenCandidateError` mapped hidden operator candidates through the ordinary no-match path, which misclassified private operator syntax.
- official Cangjie evidence: focused `cjc --diagnostic-format json` probe with `struct A { public operator func +(q: A) {}; private operator func -(s: A) {} }` showed `A + A` reports `sema_invalid_binary_expr` on `+` plus `sema_ref_not_be_type` on both operands, `A() + A()` compiles, and `A() - A()` reports `sema_invalid_binary_expr` on `-`. Official implementation was traced through `external/cangjie_compiler/src/Sema/TypeCheckExpr/BinaryExpr.cpp` `SynBinaryExpr` / `DiagnoseForBinaryExpr`, `external/cangjie_compiler/src/Sema/TypeCheckExpr/Diags.cpp` `DiagInvalidBinaryExpr`, and `external/cangjie_compiler/include/cangjie/Basic/DiagRefactor/DiagnosticSema.def` `sema_invalid_binary_expr`.
- Kotlin counterpart files consulted: `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/resolve/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/resolve/transformers/body/resolve/FirExpressionsResolveTransformer.kt`, where operator syntax is represented as synthetic function calls with `FirFunctionCallOrigin.Operator`.
- CFIR owner files changed: `cfir/checkers/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/analysis/checkers/expression/CfirClassifierAsExpressionChecker.kt`, `cfir/checkers/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/analysis/diagnostics/coneDiagnosticToCfirDiagnostic.kt`.
- repair principle: preserve classifier operands as runtime expression uses for plain operator calls and classify hidden operator candidates at the shared candidate-diagnostic mapping layer, rather than adding fixture-local binary-expression checks.
- fixtures covered: `CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt.record.RecordGenerated.testRecordAccessControl`, `CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt.record.RecordGenerated.testRecordAccessControl`.
- guard fixtures covered: `CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt.binary.BinaryGenerated.testBinaryErrorReport02`, `CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt.enum.EnumGenerated.testEnum16MemberFunc`, `CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt.array.ArrayGenerated.testArrayAccess4`, `CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt.lookup.multi_files_private_00.MultiFilesPrivate00Generated.testMain`, and the corresponding PSI methods.
- verification command: `.\gradlew.bat :cfir:analysis-tests:test --tests '*RecordGenerated.testRecordAccessControl' --no-daemon --max-workers=1 --console=plain --stacktrace`
- verification outcome: `BUILD SUCCESSFUL`; XML showed both `CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt.record.RecordGenerated.testRecordAccessControl` and `CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt.record.RecordGenerated.testRecordAccessControl` passed with `tests=1 failures=0 errors=0`.
- guard verification command: `.\gradlew.bat :cfir:analysis-tests:test --tests '*BinaryGenerated.testBinaryErrorReport02' --tests '*EnumGenerated.testEnum16MemberFunc' --tests '*ArrayGenerated.testArrayAccess4' --tests '*MultiFilesPrivate00Generated.testMain' --no-daemon --max-workers=1 --console=plain --stacktrace`
- guard verification outcome: `BUILD SUCCESSFUL`; XML showed all eight PSI/non-PSI guard methods passed with `failures=0 errors=0`.
- regression command: `.\gradlew.bat :cfir:analysis-tests:test --no-daemon --max-workers=1 --console=plain --stacktrace`
- regression outcome: `BUILD FAILED` with remaining unrelated LLT failures. Aggregate XML showed `8756 tests`, `3294 failures`, `0 errors`, `311 skipped`; the covered `testRecordAccessControl` and guard methods remained passed in both PSI and non-PSI XML.

## Unit-return match branch numeric conversion expected-type mismatch

- problem type: numeric conversion expressions inside nested `match` branches in an explicit `Unit` function body must keep their synthesized primitive result type and must not report `TYPE_MISMATCH` solely because the outer function body supplies `Unit` as expected type.
- root cause: `CfirExpressionsResolveTransformer.transformTypeConversion` treated `ResolutionMode.WithExpectedType` as a local hard requirement and wrapped a valid numeric conversion result in `ConeTypeMismatchError` when the synthesized primitive type was not a subtype of the outer expected type. In `box_in_multi_matchcase.cj`, the explicit `Unit` return type reaches the nested `match` branch as expected type, so `Int64(v)` was converted into an erroneous expression before the function-body checker could apply the explicit-`Unit` rule.
- official Cangjie evidence: `cjc --diagnostic-format json --output %TEMP%\codex-box-in-multi-matchcase cfir\analysis-tests\testData\llt\box\box_in_multi_matchcase.cj` exited `0` with no diagnostics. A guard probe on `box_in_generic.cj` exited `0` with only `chir_dce_unused_expression`, confirming that non-`Unit` return target typing remains real Cangjie semantics and must not be removed from function body resolution.
- Kotlin counterpart files consulted: `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/resolve/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/resolve/ResolutionMode.kt`, `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/resolve/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/resolve/inference/FirCallCompleter.kt`, `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/resolve/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/resolve/transformers/body/resolve/FirDeclarationsResolveTransformer.kt`, `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/resolve/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/resolve/transformers/body/resolve/FirControlFlowStatementsResolveTransformer.kt`.
- CFIR owner files changed: `cfir/resolve/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/resolve/body/CfirExpressionsResolveTransformer.kt`.
- repair principle: keep Cangjie return target typing in the function-body owner, but align the expression resolver with Kotlin's expected-type contract by treating expected type as a resolution hint and leaving mismatch reporting to checker/reporting owners.
- fixtures covered: `CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt.box.BoxGenerated.testBoxInMultiMatchcase`, `CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt.box.BoxGenerated.testBoxInMultiMatchcase`.
- verification command: `.\gradlew.bat :cfir:analysis-tests:test --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt.box.BoxGenerated.testBoxInMultiMatchcase' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt.box.BoxGenerated.testBoxInMultiMatchcase' --no-daemon --max-workers=1 --console=plain --stacktrace`
- verification outcome: `BUILD SUCCESSFUL`; both PSI and non-PSI `testBoxInMultiMatchcase` methods passed.
- regression command: `.\gradlew.bat :cfir:analysis-tests:test --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt.box.BoxGenerated' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt.box.BoxGenerated' --no-daemon --max-workers=1 --console=plain --stacktrace`
- regression outcome: `BUILD FAILED` with remaining unrelated box LLT failures: `54 tests completed, 14 failed`. XML showed `testBoxInMultiMatchcase` had no failure node in either PSI or non-PSI suite; the earlier experimental `testBoxInGeneric` regression disappeared after preserving Cangjie return target typing.

## Field initializer this/super diagnostics and constructor-argument range

- problem type: field initializers must distinguish official `this` / `super` initializer diagnostics from generic illegal-this/super-call diagnostics, struct field initializers must use the struct-specific `this` diagnostic, constructor default/delegation argument diagnostics must cover the full `this` / `super` keyword, and bare `super` must not receive a cascading initializer diagnostic.
- root cause: `CfirFieldVariableThisOrSuperInitializerChecker` reported the old generic `ILLEGAL_THIS_OR_SUPER_CALL`, did not know the owning class-like for struct field initializers, treated every `CfirSuperReceiverExpression` as an initializer violation including bare `super`, and did not put ordinary `CfirFunctionCall` nodes on the qualified-access receiver stack. Constructor-member-access reporting still used first-character sources for explicit `this` / `super`.
- official Cangjie evidence: `cjc --diagnostic-format json --error-count-limit all` on `tmp\codex-cfir-this-super-initializer-probe.cj` reported `sema_this_or_super_not_allowed_to_initialize_non_static_member` for `super.val`, `sema_assignment_of_member_variable_cannot_use_this_or_super` for `this(this)`, and `sema_illegal_this_outside_struct_constructor` for a struct field initializer `this.width`. `tmp\codex-cfir-this-super-field-extra-probe.cj` showed `super.x` / `super.f()` report the non-static initializer diagnostic, bare class-field `this` reports the non-static initializer diagnostic, static `super.x` reports the static initializer diagnostic, but bare `super` in both static and non-static field initializers only reports `sema_illegal_super_alone`. `tmp\codex-cfir-record-this-probe.cj` confirmed `sema_illegal_this_outside_struct_constructor`, `sema_cannot_assign_to_immutable` on the full assignment, `sema_illegal_place_of_calling_this_or_super`, and `sema_static_members_cannot_call_members` for the corresponding struct cases. Official implementation was traced through `external/cangjie_compiler/src/Sema/TypeCheckReference.cpp` `CheckThisOrSuper`, `CheckUsageOfSuper`, and `CheckThisOrSuperInInitializer`, and `external/cangjie_compiler/src/Sema/TypeCheckAccess.cpp` `CheckIllegalMemberWalker` / `CheckIllegalMemberHelper`.
- Kotlin counterpart files consulted: `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/checkers/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/analysis/cfa/VariableInitializationCheckProcessor.kt` and `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/checkers/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/analysis/checkers/declaration/FirMemberPropertiesChecker.kt`.
- CFIR owner files changed: `cfir/checkers/checkers-component-generator/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/checkers/generator/diagnostics/CfirDiagnosticsList.kt`, `cfir/checkers/gen/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/analysis/diagnostics/CfirErrors.kt`, `cfir/checkers/gen/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/analysis/diagnostics/CfirNonSuppressibleErrorNames.kt`, `cfir/checkers/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/analysis/diagnostics/CfirErrorsDefaultMessages.kt`, `cfir/analysis-tests/testFixtures/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/analysis/tests/golden/DiagnosticNameMapper.kt`, `cfir/checkers/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/analysis/checkers/declaration/CfirFieldVariableThisOrSuperInitializerChecker.kt`, and `cfir/checkers/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/analysis/checkers/declaration/CfirInitializationCheckers.kt`.
- repair principle: keep the official classification at the field-initializer and constructor-member-access checker owners, using owner-aware struct handling and receiver-stack shape instead of fixture-local diagnostics or fallback cascades.
- fixtures covered: `CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt._class.ClassGenerated.testClassShadow9`, `testClass5`, `testClass6`, `testClassSuperAloneInvalid1`, `testInvalidSuperCall`, `CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt._class.class_property.ClassPropertyGenerated.testProperty30`, `CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt._class.constructor.primaryConstructor.PrimaryConstructorGenerated.testPrimaryConstructor6`, `testPrimaryConstructor9`, and the corresponding PSI methods.
- guard fixtures covered: `CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt._class.ClassGenerated.testInvalidInitCall1`, `testInvalidSuperCall2`, `testInterfaceInitInvalid1`, `testClass8`, `CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt.record.RecordGenerated.testRecordAccessControl`, and the corresponding PSI methods.
- fixture correction: `class5.cj`, `property30.cj`, `class_shadow9.cj`, `class6.cj`, and `invalid_super_call.cj` now use the official initializer / bare-super diagnostic split. `primaryConstructor6.cj`, `primaryConstructor9.cj`, `super_this_10.cj`, and related constructor-argument markers use full-keyword ranges under the repository diagnostic range policy. Mixed fixtures `super_this_09.cj`, `record_pkg_01_2.cj`, and `record_this.cj` had only the official-evidence-backed this/super/struct markers corrected; they remain failing because they also contain separate initialization-flow, unresolved-constructor, or recursive-constructor families.

## Constructor delegation placement, implicit super, and constructor-body try cascades

- problem type: constructor delegation calls must use the official split between invalid outside-constructor calls, illegal delegation placement, missing implicit `super()`, recursive `this(...)` delegation, and target-typed `try` diagnostics inside constructor bodies.
- root cause: `CfirConstructorDelegationChecker` still checked the implicit `super()` requirement after seeing a non-first explicit `super(...)`, so illegal-position `super(...)` cascaded into `NO_NON_PARAM_CONSTRUCTOR_IN_SUPER_CLASS`. `CfirTryTargetTypeMismatchChecker` treated a constructor body like a normal function body and used the constructor `returnTypeRef` to target-type a tail `try`, so `try { this.y = x } catch { throw e }` cascaded into a bogus assignment `TYPE_MISMATCH`. `CfirRecursiveConstructorCallChecker` used the `this()` call node as the cycle head for constructor-delegation recursion, while official diagnostics report the constructor declaration.
- official Cangjie evidence: `cjc --diagnostic-format json --error-count-limit all` on `tmp\codex-cfir-constructor-delegation-diagnostics-probe.cj` reported `sema_no_non_param_constructor_in_super_class` for a constructor with no explicit super call, `sema_illegal_place_of_calling_this_or_super` for non-first `super(...)`, `sema_invalid_this_call_outside_ctor` for member `this()` / `super()`, `sema_recursive_constructor_call` on the `init` declaration, and no no-param/type-mismatch cascade for `super(x)` inside `try`. `tmp\codex-cfir-illegal-this-implicit-super-probe.cj` showed a non-first explicit `this(...)` still keeps the implicit-super diagnostic, so only explicit `super(...)` suppresses that requirement. `tmp\codex-cfir-try-assignment-unit-probe.cj` showed `try` blocks whose tail expression is assignment are valid in `Unit` constructor/function bodies. Official implementation was traced through `external/cangjie_compiler/src/Sema/TypeChecker.cpp` `CheckCallsInConstructor` / `CheckConstructorSuper`, `external/cangjie_compiler/src/Sema/TypeCheckCall.cpp` outside-constructor call handling, and `external/cangjie_compiler/src/Sema/TypeCheckExpr/AssignExpr.cpp` where assignment expression checking states the assignment expression type is always `Unit`.
- Kotlin counterpart files consulted: `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/checkers/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/analysis/checkers/declaration/FirCommonConstructorDelegationIssuesChecker.kt` and `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/resolve/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/resolve/transformers/body/resolve/FirExpressionsResolveTransformer.kt`.
- CFIR owner files changed: `cfir/checkers/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/analysis/checkers/declaration/CfirConstructorDelegationChecker.kt`, `cfir/checkers/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/analysis/checkers/expression/CfirTryTargetTypeMismatchChecker.kt`, and `cfir/checkers/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/analysis/checkers/declaration/CfirRecursiveConstructorCallChecker.kt`.
- repair principle: keep constructor-delegation diagnostics in declaration-level constructor owners, only diagnose synthesized implicit super when no explicit `super(...)` exists, exclude constructors from function-tail target typing, and keep `this(...)` delegation recursion anchored to the constructor declaration instead of the call expression.
- fixtures covered: `CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt._class.super_this.SuperThisGenerated.testSuperThis11`, `CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt._class.super_this.SuperThisGenerated.testSuperThis11`, `CfirAnalysisDiagnostics2Test.diagnostics2.invalid_declaration.InvalidDeclarationGenerated.testIllegalDelegationPlacement`, `testIllegalThisCallInMember`, `testIllegalSuperCallInMember`, `testExplicitSuperCallRequired`, `testConstructorDelegationMatrix`, `testRecursiveConstructorCall`, the corresponding `CfirAnalysisDiagnostics2PsiTest` and `CfirAnalysisDiagnostics2WithoutAliasExpansionTest` methods, `CfirAnalysisDiagnosticsTest.diagnostics.constructor.ConstructorGenerated.testIllegalDelegationPlacementRich`, `testDelegationAndConstructorsRich`, and the corresponding PSI/without-alias-expansion methods.
- fixture correction: `super_this_11.cj` now expects `ILLEGAL_PLACE_OF_CALLING_THIS_OR_SUPER`; diagnostics illegal member-call fixtures now expect `INVALID_THIS_CALL_OUTSIDE_CTOR`; missing-super fixtures now expect `NO_NON_PARAM_CONSTRUCTOR_IN_SUPER_CLASS`; `delegationAndConstructorsRich.cj` uses full-token `CONFLICTING_OVERLOADS` and preserves `RECURSIVE_CONSTRUCTOR_CALL` on `init`.
- verification command: `.\gradlew.bat :cfir:analysis-tests:test --tests '*SuperThisGenerated.testSuperThis11' --tests '*InvalidDeclarationGenerated.testIllegalDelegationPlacement' --tests '*InvalidDeclarationGenerated.testIllegalThisCallInMember' --tests '*InvalidDeclarationGenerated.testIllegalSuperCallInMember' --tests '*InvalidDeclarationGenerated.testExplicitSuperCallRequired' --tests '*InvalidDeclarationGenerated.testConstructorDelegationMatrix' --tests '*InvalidDeclarationGenerated.testRecursiveConstructorCall' --tests '*ConstructorGenerated.testIllegalDelegationPlacementRich' --tests '*ConstructorGenerated.testDelegationAndConstructorsRich' --no-daemon --max-workers=1 --console=plain --stacktrace`
- verification outcome: `BUILD SUCCESSFUL`; all focused PSI, non-PSI, and without-alias-expansion methods in the covered slice passed.
- regression command: `.\gradlew.bat :cfir:analysis-tests:test --no-daemon --max-workers=1 --console=plain --stacktrace`
- regression outcome: the outer command timed out after 604 seconds while the Gradle worker continued; current XML later completed with `8756 tests`, `3276 failures`, `0 errors`, `311 skipped`. The covered methods listed above had no failure nodes in the completed XML.

## Unused diagnostics gating and inherited-member import usage

- problem type: CHIR-DCE-style unused diagnostics must be enabled only through the extra-checker path, must follow official no-warning behavior in files with semantic errors and Unit-return bodies, and unused-import usage must count imported declaration targets reached through inherited member references such as `TypeInfo.name`.
- root cause: `CfirUnusedExpressionChecker` and `CfirDceUnusedDeclarationChecker` were registered as common checkers, so warnings appeared without `WITH_EXTRA_CHECKERS`. `CfirUnusedExpressionChecker` also used return-type and syntax rules that diverged from official DCE behavior for Unit-return bodies, bare local variable accesses, enum constructor calls, and try handlers. Several fixtures expected DCE warnings in files where official `cjc` suppresses DCE after sema errors. Separately, `CfirImportsChecker` collected source names and containing-class lookup tags but missed callable declaration owners from `CallableId` and fake/delegated override originals, so inherited properties such as `.name` failed to mark `std.reflect.TypeInfo` as used.
- official Cangjie evidence: `tmp/codex-unused-variable-probe.cj` showed ordinary unused locals report `chir_dce_unused_variable` and are suppressible with `-Woff unused`; `external/cangjie_compiler/include/cangjie/Basic/DiagRefactor/DiagnosticChir.def` defines `chir_dce_unused_variable`; `external/cangjie_compiler/src/CHIR/Transformation/DeadCodeElimination.cpp` contains `ReportUnusedCode`, `ReportUnusedExpression`, and `CheckUselessExpr`. `tmp/codex-unused-with-error-probe.cj` showed a file with sema errors reports no unused warnings. `tmp/codex-body-return-inference-official.cj` showed official reports only return/body type errors, with no unused warning for `declaredUnitTailExpression(): Unit { 1 }` and no call-site error for `unreachableTail()`. For imports, `cjc` on `cfir/analysis-tests/testData/llt/generics/generic_arrange.cj` produced no diagnostics; `tmp/codex-unused-import-typeinfo-only.cj` and `tmp/codex-unused-import-reflect-cast-only.cj` reported `sema_unused_import` for `std.reflect.TypeInfo`, while `tmp/codex-unused-import-reflect-direct-name.cj` and `tmp/codex-unused-import-reflect-cast-get-name.cj` produced no diagnostics. Official implementation was traced through `external/cangjie_compiler/src/Sema/CheckUnusedImportImpl.cpp` target recording and usage checks, and `external/cangjie_compiler/src/Sema/TypeChecker.cpp` `CheckUnusedImportSpec`.
- Kotlin counterpart files consulted: `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/checkers/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/analysis/checkers/ExtraDeclarationCheckers.kt`, `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/checkers/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/analysis/checkers/extra/FirUnusedExpressionChecker.kt`, `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/checkers/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/analysis/checkers/declaration/FirUnusedCheckerBase.kt`, and `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/checkers/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/analysis/checkers/declaration/FirImportsChecker.kt`.
- CFIR owner files changed: `cfir/checkers/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/analysis/checkers/CommonDeclarationCheckers.kt`, `cfir/checkers/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/analysis/checkers/ExtraDeclarationCheckers.kt`, `cfir/entrypoint/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/entrypoint/checkers/CheckersContainers.kt`, `cfir/checkers/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/analysis/checkers/declaration/CfirUnusedExpressionChecker.kt`, `cfir/checkers/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/analysis/checkers/declaration/CfirImportsChecker.kt`, and fixture corrections in `cfir/analysis-tests/testData/llt/if-let-expr/enhancedcondition/vars/samename_conditionandelse.cj`, `cfir/analysis-tests/testData/diagnostics2/try/finallyFlowPlaceholder.cj`, `cfir/analysis-tests/testData/diagnostics2/function/bodyReturnInference.cj`, `cfir/analysis-tests/testData/diagnostics2/effects/handleBlockMismatch.cj`, and `cfir/analysis-tests/testData/diagnostics/effects/effectsSemanticsRich.cj`.
- repair principle: place lint/DCE-style diagnostics behind the shared extra-checker registration and collect import usage from resolved declaration targets, including inherited callable owners, instead of encoding fixture-local warning exceptions.

## Generic upper-bound direct recursion and first invalid-bound reporting

- problem type: generic upper-bound graph cycles must report `GENERIC_PARAM_DIRECTLY_RECURSIVE`, not ordinary upper-bound legality diagnostics, and non-recursive invalid upper-bound intersections should emit only the first root diagnostic per type parameter.
- root cause: `CfirGenericDeepChecker` only detected direct self references such as `T <: T` and shallow type-argument recursion, so graph cycles like `K <: U, U <: K` were missed. `CfirTypeParameterBoundsChecker` then ran ordinary class/interface upper-bound legality first and produced `UPPER_BOUND_MUST_BE_CLASS_OR_INTERFACE` cascades. For intersection upper bounds such as `T <: U & R`, the checker also reported every invalid bound while official behavior reports only the first invalid upper-bound root for that type parameter.
- official Cangjie evidence: `external/cangjie_compiler/src/Sema/PreCheck.cpp` `AreUpperBoundsDirectlyRecursive` recursively follows generic upper-bound references and treats repeated generic parameters in that graph as direct recursion. `ValidRecursiveConstraintCheck` runs this declaration-level recursion check before sanity checks and returns after the first direct recursion diagnostic. `SanityCheckForOneGenericTy` contains ordinary class/interface upper-bound legality checks, so those checks are skipped for a declaration whose generic bound graph is already directly recursive. `tmp/codex-generic-direct-recursive-probe.cj` and `tmp/codex-generic-upperbound-reference10-probe.cj` compiled with `cjc --diagnostic-format json --error-count-limit all` and reported `sema_generic_param_directly_recursive` for the recursive graph roots. `tmp/codex-constraint-check-test11-4-probe.cj` showed that `T <: U & R` reports one `sema_upper_bound_must_be_class_or_interface` on the first invalid bound for `T`.
- Kotlin counterpart files consulted: `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/checkers/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/analysis/checkers/declaration/FirCyclicTypeBoundsChecker.kt`, `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/checkers/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/analysis/checkers/declaration/FirTypeParameterBoundsChecker.kt`.
- CFIR owner files changed: `cfir/checkers/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/analysis/checkers/declaration/CfirGenericUpperBoundRecursion.kt`, `cfir/checkers/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/analysis/checkers/declaration/CfirGenericDeepChecker.kt`, `cfir/checkers/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/analysis/checkers/declaration/CfirTypeParameterBoundsChecker.kt`, `cfir/checkers/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/analysis/checkers/CommonDeclarationCheckers.kt`.
- repair principle: use a shared declaration-level generic upper-bound graph root-cause check before ordinary bounds legality, and make both generic-recursion and upper-bound legality checkers consume the same owner-level result instead of suppressing individual fixture failures.
- fixtures covered: `CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt.generics.GenericsGenerated.testRecursiveGenerics`, `CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt.generics.GenericsGenerated.testRecursiveGenerics`, `CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt.generics.GenericsGenerated.testGenericUpperboundReference10`, `CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt.generics.GenericsGenerated.testGenericUpperboundReference10`, `CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt.generic_constraint.ConstraintCheckGenerated.testConstraintCheckTest114`, `CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt.generic_constraint.ConstraintCheckGenerated.testConstraintCheckTest114`, `CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt.type_args.solve_type_args.SolveTypeArgsGenerated.testFBounded2`, and `CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt.type_args.solve_type_args.SolveTypeArgsGenerated.testFBounded2`.
- verification command: `.\gradlew.bat :cfir:analysis-tests:test --tests '*GenericsGenerated.testRecursiveGenerics' --tests '*GenericsGenerated.testGenericUpperboundReference10' --tests '*ConstraintCheckGenerated.testConstraintCheckTest114' --tests '*SolveTypeArgsGenerated.testFBounded2' --no-daemon --max-workers=1 --console=plain --stacktrace`
- verification outcome: `BUILD SUCCESSFUL`; all eight focused PSI and non-PSI methods passed.
- guard command: `.\gradlew.bat :cfir:analysis-tests:test --tests '*GenericsGenerated.testRecursiveGenerics' --tests '*GenericsGenerated.testGenericUpperboundReference10' --tests '*GenericsGenerated.testGenericUpperboundCallable1' --tests '*GenericsGenerated.testFunction2' --tests '*GenericsGenerated.testGenericParameters8' --tests '*GenericConstraintGenerated.testGenericConstraint5' --tests '*GenericConstraintGenerated.testGenericConstraint6' --tests '*GenericConstraintGenerated.testGenericConstraint11' --tests '*GenericConstraintGenerated.testGenericConstraint14' --no-daemon --max-workers=1 --console=plain --stacktrace`
- guard outcome: `BUILD FAILED` with unrelated existing generics failures in `function2`, `generic_parameters8`, `generic_upperbound_callable1`, and `generic_constraint5`; XML confirmed the covered `testRecursiveGenerics` and `testGenericUpperboundReference10` methods passed in both PSI and non-PSI suites.

## Callable/function type upper bounds and invalid-bound cascade suppression

- problem type: callable/function type upper bounds such as `T <: (Int) -> Int`, plus nested bounds `U <: T` / `R <: U`, must report upper-bound legality diagnostics for the owning type parameters without crashing on raw type refs or cascading into return-type / invoke-operator diagnostics.
- root cause: several checker paths consumed `symbol.resolvedBounds` as if every declared upper bound had already resolved to `CfirResolvedTypeRef`; local nested generic declarations can still expose raw `CfirUserTypeRef`, `CfirFunctionTypeRef`, or `CfirTupleTypeRef` at checker time, which produced casts such as `CfirUserTypeRef cannot be cast to CfirResolvedTypeRef` and then secondary inference / invoke diagnostics after the invalid bound was known.
- official Cangjie evidence: `cjc --diagnostic-format json --error-count-limit all tmp\codex-generic-upperbound-callable-probe.cj` reported three `sema_upper_bound_must_be_class_or_interface` diagnostics for `T`, `U`, and `R`. The official checker path is `external/cangjie_compiler/src/Sema/PreCheck.cpp` `ValidRecursiveConstraintCheck`, `SanityCheckForOneGenericTy`, and `AssumptionSanityCheck`; `external/cangjie_compiler/include/cangjie/Basic/DiagRefactor/DiagnosticSema.def` defines `sema_upper_bound_must_be_class_or_interface`.
- Kotlin counterpart files consulted: `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/checkers/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/analysis/checkers/declaration/FirTypeParameterBoundsChecker.kt`, `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/tree/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/symbols/impl/FirTypeParameterSymbol.kt`, and `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/providers/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/types/ConeTypeContext.kt`.
- CFIR owner files changed: `cfir/checkers/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/analysis/checkers/CfirDeclaredUpperBoundContextUtils.kt`, `cfir/checkers/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/analysis/checkers/CfirUpperBoundViolatedHelpers.kt`, `cfir/checkers/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/analysis/checkers/declaration/CfirGenericUpperBoundRecursion.kt`, `cfir/checkers/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/analysis/checkers/declaration/CfirTypeParameterBoundsChecker.kt`, `cfir/checkers/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/analysis/checkers/declaration/CfirFunctionSemanticsChecker.kt`, and `cfir/checkers/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/analysis/collectors/components/ErrorNodeDiagnosticCollectorComponent.kt`.
- repair principle: expose a shared checker-context-aware declared-upper-bound view and make upper-bound legality, recursion, use-site bound checks, return inference, and invoke diagnostics consume that root-cause state instead of assuming resolved-bound storage or adding fixture-local suppressions.
- fixtures covered: `CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt.generics.GenericsGenerated.testGenericUpperboundCallable1`, `CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt.generics.GenericsGenerated.testGenericUpperboundCallable2`, `CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt.generics.GenericsGenerated.testGenericUpperboundCallable1`, and `CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt.generics.GenericsGenerated.testGenericUpperboundCallable2`.
- verification command: `.\gradlew.bat :cfir:analysis-tests:test --tests '*GenericsGenerated.testGenericUpperboundCallable1' --tests '*GenericsGenerated.testGenericUpperboundCallable2' --no-daemon --max-workers=1 --console=plain --stacktrace --rerun-tasks`
- verification outcome: the outer command timed out after 304 seconds while the Gradle Java workers continued; after the workers exited, XML under `cfir/analysis-tests/build/test-results/test` showed both `CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt.generics.GenericsGenerated` and `CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt.generics.GenericsGenerated` completed `tests=2 failures=0 errors=0 skipped=0`.

## Explicit generic argument constraint mismatch and function-value ambiguity range

- problem type: explicit generic type arguments that violate declaration constraints must surface `GENERIC_TYPE_ARGUMENT_NOT_MATCH_CONSTRAINT` ahead of arity or wrong-receiver cascades, and ambiguous function values such as `a.foo<C2>` must underline the selector function reference rather than the whole qualified receiver expression.
- root cause: the dispatch-receiver stage checked ordinary receiver subtyping after explicit type-argument substitution, so the real `C2 <: C1` contradiction was downgraded to `INAPPLICABLE_WRONG_RECEIVER`; overload selection then preferred the parameterless overload and rendered `TOO_MANY_ARGUMENTS`. Separately, ambiguity diagnostic mapping treated every non-call ambiguity as qualified-access ambiguity and expanded `a.foo<C2>` from the selector to the whole receiver expression.
- official Cangjie evidence: `cjc --diagnostic-format json --error-count-limit all tmp\codex-generic-constraint5-probe.cj` reported `sema_generic_type_argument_not_match_constraint` for `a.foo<C2>(C1())`, `A<C1>.goo<C2>`, and `B<Sub>().f<Sub2>(Sub2())`, plus `sema_ambiguous_use` for `let b = a.foo<C2>`. `external/cangjie_compiler/src/Sema/TypeCheckGeneric.cpp` `CheckGenericDeclInstantiation` reports the explicit type-argument constraint mismatch and skips only instantiated generic type arguments whose own upper bounds are illegal. `external/cangjie_compiler/src/Sema/PreCheck.cpp` performs the declaration-side upper-bound legality checks for `Y <: X`.
- Kotlin counterpart files consulted: `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/resolve/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/resolve/calls/stages/ArgumentCheckingProcessor.kt`, `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/resolve/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/resolve/calls/stages/ResolutionStages.kt`, and `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/resolve/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/resolve/calls/stages/CreateFreshTypeVariableSubstitutorStage.kt`.
- CFIR owner files changed: `cfir/resolve/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/resolve/calls/stages/CfirCheckDispatchReceiver.kt`, `cfir/checkers/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/analysis/diagnostics/coneDiagnosticToCfirDiagnostic.kt`, and the related use-site invalid-bound cascade handling in `cfir/checkers/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/analysis/checkers/CfirUpperBoundViolatedHelpers.kt`.
- repair principle: let tower/member lookup own ordinary dispatch-receiver suitability and keep this stage for receiver-only diagnostics plus fresh lambda receiver inference, so explicit type-argument contradictions remain the candidate's primary applicability error; map function-value ambiguity through the selector source because the receiver only supplies member scope.
- fixtures covered: `CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt.generics.generic_constraint.GenericConstraintGenerated.testGenericConstraint5`, `CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt.generics.generic_constraint.GenericConstraintGenerated.testGenericConstraint5`, `CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt.generics.GenericsGenerated.testGenericUpperboundCallable1`, `CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt.generics.GenericsGenerated.testGenericUpperboundCallable2`, and the corresponding PSI upper-bound callable methods.
- verification command: `.\gradlew.bat :cfir:analysis-tests:test --tests '*GenericConstraintGenerated.testGenericConstraint5' --tests '*GenericsGenerated.testGenericUpperboundCallable1' --tests '*GenericsGenerated.testGenericUpperboundCallable2' --no-daemon --max-workers=1 --console=plain --stacktrace`
- verification outcome: `BUILD SUCCESSFUL`; all six focused PSI and non-PSI methods passed.

## Generic where unresolved names, dangling bounds, and delegated error supertypes

- problem type: unresolved names in generic `where` constraints, dangling RHS type names after an invalid constraint LHS, interface supertypes whose nominal owner is hidden behind recoverable type-argument errors, and generic upper-bound operator member access must follow official diagnostic ordering without cascading into member-not-found or upper-bound-access diagnostics.
- root cause: invalid `where` LHS constraints discarded their RHS type refs before declaration diagnostics, so unresolved RHS names such as `Num` were invisible. Generic argument count errors were represented as `ConeErrorType` and therefore discarded during delegated supertype traversal and interface-supertype legality checks even when the nominal classifier owner had resolved. The generic upper-bound access mapper also swallowed operator unresolved-name errors before the invalid-binary-operator mapping could run.
- official Cangjie evidence: `cjc --diagnostic-format json --error-count-limit all tmp\codex-function2-probe.cj` reported `sema_undeclared_type_name` on `U`, `Num`, and `update`, `sema_upper_bound_must_be_class_or_interface`, `sema_overload_conflicts`, and `sema_invalid_binary_expr`; `tmp\codex-generic-parameters8-probe.cj` reported `sema_undeclared_type_name` on both `V` uses, upper-bound diagnostics, `sema_interface_inherit_non_interface`, and `sema_generic_argument_no_match`, with no member-missing cascade on `B<String, Int64>().m`; `tmp\codex-constraint-undefined-lhs-probe.cj` and `tmp\codex-constraint-undefined-lhs-extend-probe.cj` reported `sema_undeclared_type_name` on undefined where LHS `U`; `tmp\codex-constraint-lhs-defined-nontypeparam-probe.cj` reported the defined-but-not-owner-type-parameter constraint diagnostic; `tmp\codex-interface-unresolved-super-probe.cj` showed `interface X <: Missing {}` reports both undeclared type name and interface-inherits-non-interface. Official implementation was traced through `external/cangjie_compiler/src/Sema/PreCheck.cpp` undeclared type name, generic argument mismatch, and upper-bound checks; `external/cangjie_compiler/src/Sema/TypeCheckCall.cpp` generic upper-bound member access; `external/cangjie_compiler/src/Sema/TypeCheckExpr/Diags.cpp` invalid binary expression; `external/cangjie_compiler/src/Sema/TypeCheckExpr/NameReferenceExpr.cpp` upper-bound access and invalid-base cascade behavior; and `external/cangjie_compiler/src/Sema/TypeManager.cpp` invalid-supertype traversal behavior.
- Kotlin counterpart files consulted: `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/checkers/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/analysis/checkers/declaration/FirTypeConstraintsChecker.kt`, `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/checkers/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/analysis/diagnostics/coneDiagnosticToFirDiagnostic.kt`, `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/checkers/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/analysis/checkers/declaration/FirTypeParameterBoundsChecker.kt`, `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/resolve/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/resolve/calls/stages/ArgumentCheckingProcessor.kt`, `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/resolve/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/resolve/calls/stages/ResolutionStages.kt`, and `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/resolve/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/resolve/calls/stages/CreateFreshTypeVariableSubstitutorStage.kt`.
- CFIR owner files changed: `cfir/cfir-cones/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/types/ConeDiagnostic.kt`, `cfir/semantics/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/diagnostic/ConeDiagnostic.kt`, `cfir/providers/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/scopes/impl/CfirClassUseSiteMemberScope.kt`, `cfir/cfir-tree/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/declarations/CfirTypeConstraintDiagnosticData.kt`, `cfir/raw-cfir/psi2cfir/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/builder/PsiRawCfirBuilder.kt`, `cfir/raw-cfir/light-tree2cfir/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/lightTree/LightTreeRawCfirDeclarationBuilder.kt`, `cfir/checkers/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/analysis/checkers/declaration/CfirTypeConstraintsChecker.kt`, `cfir/checkers/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/analysis/checkers/declaration/CfirSupertypesChecker.kt`, and `cfir/checkers/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/analysis/diagnostics/coneDiagnosticToCfirDiagnostic.kt`.
- repair principle: preserve raw constraint RHS diagnostics at the type-constraint diagnostic-data owner, model recoverable type-argument errors with an explicit delegated-scope marker shared by supertype traversal and legality checks, and let operator unresolved names fall through to the shared invalid-binary-operator diagnostic path.
- fixtures covered: `CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt.generics.GenericsGenerated.testFunction2`, `CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt.generics.GenericsGenerated.testFunction2`, `CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt.generics.GenericsGenerated.testGenericParameters8`, `CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt.generics.GenericsGenerated.testGenericParameters8`, `CfirAnalysisDiagnosticsTest.diagnostics.constraints.ConstraintsGenerated.testNameInConstraintIsNotTypeParameter`, `CfirAnalysisDiagnosticsTest.diagnostics.constraints.ConstraintsGenerated.testNameInConstraintIsNotTypeParameterExtend`, the corresponding diagnostics PSI methods and diagnostics2 methods, `CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt.generic_access.GenericAccessGenerated.testUpperBoundsMemberAndMethod`, `testUpperBoundsMemberAndMethodRich`, `CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt.generics.generic_constraint.GenericConstraintGenerated.testGenericConstraint5`, `CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt.generics.GenericsGenerated.testGenericUpperboundCallable1`, `testGenericUpperboundCallable2`, and the corresponding PSI methods.

## Mutability diagnostics for immutable receivers and invalid struct upper-bound recovery

- problem type: immutable struct/interface member functions cannot call `mut` functions, immutable struct values cannot call `mut` functions, `mut` is allowed in struct-extend member declarations, mut override consistency applies only to struct/struct-extend implementations of interface functions, and invalid struct upper bounds must still recover mut-call and mut-reference diagnostics.
- root cause: mutability checkers only consumed resolved function symbols and resolved upper bounds, so invalid bounds such as `T <: R1` or `X <: T` left mut calls/references unresolved and dropped the official mutability diagnostics. Modifier-target validation also rejected `mut` in struct-extend bodies even though official Cangjie allows it, and inheritance mut-compatibility checking applied too broadly outside the official struct/interface implementation case.
- official Cangjie evidence: `cjc --diagnostic-format json --error-count-limit all tmp\codex-mutability-access-probe.cj` and `tmp\codex-mutability-remaining-probe.cj` showed immutable value-type calls/references to `mut` members are diagnosed on the member access/reference while struct-extend `mut` declarations are accepted. `external/cangjie_compiler/src/Sema/TypeCheckExpr.cpp` `CheckImmutableFuncAccessMutableFunc`, `external/cangjie_compiler/src/Sema/TypeCheckAccess.cpp` `CheckLetInstanceAccessMutableFunc`, and `external/cangjie_compiler/src/Sema/Diags.cpp` `DiagImmutableAccessMutableFunc` define the immutable-access checks. `external/cangjie_compiler/src/Sema/InheritanceChecker/StructInheritanceChecker.cpp` limits mut compatibility to struct or struct-extend implementations of interface functions, and `external/cangjie_compiler/src/Sema/DeclAttributeChecker.cpp` allows `mut` in struct-extend member functions.
- Kotlin counterpart files consulted: `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/checkers/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/analysis/checkers/expression/FirReassignmentAndInvisibleSetterChecker.kt`, `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/checkers/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/analysis/diagnostics/coneDiagnosticToFirDiagnostic.kt`, plus local use-site member scope/substitution patterns in `cfir/providers/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/scopes/impl/CfirTypeVariableReceiverMemberScopeTowerLevel.kt`.
- CFIR owner files changed: `cfir/checkers/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/analysis/checkers/expression/CfirMutabilityCheckers.kt`, `cfir/checkers/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/analysis/checkers/expression/CfirFunctionReferenceLegalityChecker.kt`, `cfir/checkers/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/analysis/checkers/expression/CfirExpressionSemanticsChecker.kt`, `cfir/checkers/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/analysis/checkers/ModifierCheckerTargets.kt`, and `cfir/checkers/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/analysis/checkers/declaration/CfirInheritanceDeepChecker.kt`.
- repair principle: keep normal resolved-symbol checks as the primary path, but add a shared checker-context-aware declared-upper-bound recovery path for invalid struct bounds so the root mutability owners still see official mut-member semantics without fixture-local fallbacks.
- fixtures covered: `CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt.function.FunctionGenerated.testMutErrRetStruct`, `testMutErrOp`, `testMutErrParen`, `testMutFunction00`, `testMutFunction02`, `testMutFunction07`, `testMutFunction10`, `testMutFunction12`, `CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest` counterparts, `CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt.record.mut.MutGenerated.testRecordMutInvalid8`, `testRecordMutInvalid9`, `testRecordMutInvalid12`, `testRecordMutInvalid17`, `testRecordMutInvalid18`, `testRecordMutInvalid10`, `testRecordExtendMutInvalid5`, `testRecordExtendMutInvalid6`, and the corresponding PSI methods / subdir generated `testMain` methods.
- fixture correction: record mutability fixtures now place immutable-access and standalone-mut-reference diagnostics on the selector token according to the repository diagnostic range policy; struct-extend and override-consistency expectations were corrected to match official struct/interface mut semantics.
- verification command: `.\gradlew.bat :cfir:analysis-tests:test --tests '*FunctionGenerated.testMutErrRetStruct' --tests '*FunctionGenerated.testMutErrOp' --tests '*FunctionGenerated.testMutErrParen' --tests '*FunctionGenerated.testMutFunction00' --tests '*FunctionGenerated.testMutFunction02' --tests '*FunctionGenerated.testMutFunction07' --tests '*FunctionGenerated.testMutFunction10' --tests '*FunctionGenerated.testMutFunction12' --tests '*MutGenerated.testRecordMutInvalid8' --tests '*MutGenerated.testRecordMutInvalid9' --tests '*MutGenerated.testRecordMutInvalid12' --tests '*MutGenerated.testRecordMutInvalid17' --tests '*MutGenerated.testRecordMutInvalid18' --tests '*MutGenerated.testRecordMutInvalid10' --tests '*MutGenerated.testRecordExtendMutInvalid5' --tests '*MutGenerated.testRecordExtendMutInvalid6' --no-daemon --max-workers=1 --console=plain --stacktrace`
- verification outcome: `BUILD SUCCESSFUL`; the focused mutability slice passed for PSI and non-PSI paths.
- additional verification: `.\gradlew.bat :cfir:analysis-tests:test --tests '*MutGenerated.testRecordMutInvalid17' --tests '*MutGenerated.testRecordMutInvalid18' --no-daemon --max-workers=1 --console=plain --stacktrace` timed out externally after 304 seconds, but completed XML showed `CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt.record.mut.MutGenerated` and `CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt.record.mut.MutGenerated` both had `tests=2 failures=0 errors=0`.
- regression command: `.\gradlew.bat :cfir:analysis-tests:test --no-daemon --max-workers=1 --console=plain --stacktrace`
- regression outcome: the outer full-regression command timed out while Gradle workers continued and exited later. Completed XML aggregate showed `8756 tests`, `2823 failures`, `0 errors`, `311 skipped`; all covered mutability methods and subdir generated `testMain` methods had no failure nodes in the XML.

## Primary constructor completion source and hidden constructor no-match classification

- problem type: primary-constructor completion diagnostics must underline the constructor declaration header, and hidden ordinary constructor calls must report `NO_CONSTRUCTOR` instead of ordinary function no-match.
- root cause: constructor completion reporting used the whole constructor source as an offsets-only range, so `CLASS_UNINITIALIZED_FIELD` covered constructor bodies. Separately, `ConeHiddenCandidateError` treated all hidden function-call candidates as `NO_MATCH_FUNCTION_DECLARATION_FOR_CALL`, so `C()` against an inaccessible constructor lost the official constructor-call error kind.
- official Cangjie evidence: `cjc --diagnostic-format json --error-count-limit all tmp/codex-primary-constructor-hidden-probe.cj` reported `sema_no_match_constructor` on the `C` callee for `class C { private C(var z!: Int64 = 3) {} } main() { C() }`; `external/cangjie_compiler/src/Sema/TypeCheckCall.cpp` maps `CALL_OBJECT_CREATION` and `CALL_STRUCT_CREATION` no-match paths to `sema_no_match_constructor`, and `external/cangjie_compiler/include/cangjie/Basic/DiagnosticSema.def` defines that diagnostic.
- Kotlin counterpart files consulted: `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/raw-fir/light-tree2fir/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/lightTree/converter/LightTreeRawFirDeclarationBuilder.kt`, `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/raw-fir/psi2fir/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/builder/PsiRawFirBuilder.kt`, `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/tree/gen/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/declarations/builder/FirPrimaryConstructorBuilder.kt`, `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/checkers/checkers-component-generator/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/checkers/generator/diagnostics/FirDiagnosticsList.kt`, and `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/checkers/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/analysis/diagnostics/coneDiagnosticToFirDiagnostic.kt`.
- CFIR owner files changed: `cfir/checkers/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/analysis/checkers/declaration/CfirDeclarationDiagnosticSources.kt`, `cfir/checkers/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/analysis/checkers/declaration/CfirInitializationCheckers.kt`, `cfir/semantics/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/semantics/AbstractCallInfo.kt`, `cfir/resolve/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/resolve/calls/candidate/CallInfo.kt`, and `cfir/checkers/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/analysis/diagnostics/coneDiagnosticToCfirDiagnostic.kt`.
- repair principle: keep constructor completion source selection in the shared declaration-source helper, and carry call-kind semantics through the semantics-layer call-info abstraction so diagnostic mapping can classify ordinary constructor creation without checkers depending on resolve internals or changing delegating/enum constructor behavior.
- fixtures covered: `cfir/analysis-tests/testData/llt/class/constructor/primaryConstructor/primaryConstructor2.cj`, `cfir/analysis-tests/testData/llt/class/constructor/primaryConstructor/primaryConstructor8.cj`, and `cfir/analysis-tests/testData/llt/record/primaryConstructor/primaryConstructor8.cj`.
- fixture correction: only the `CLASS_UNINITIALIZED_FIELD` ranges on constructor declarations were widened from the single constructor-name character to the constructor declaration header; diagnostic names and unrelated semantic markers were left unchanged.
- verification command: `.\gradlew.bat :cfir:analysis-tests:test --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt._class.constructor.primaryConstructor.PrimaryConstructorGenerated.testPrimaryConstructor2' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt._class.constructor.primaryConstructor.PrimaryConstructorGenerated.testPrimaryConstructor2' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt._class.constructor.primaryConstructor.PrimaryConstructorGenerated.testPrimaryConstructor8' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt._class.constructor.primaryConstructor.PrimaryConstructorGenerated.testPrimaryConstructor8' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt.record.primaryConstructor.PrimaryConstructorGenerated.testPrimaryConstructor8' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt.record.primaryConstructor.PrimaryConstructorGenerated.testPrimaryConstructor8' --no-daemon --max-workers=1 --console=plain --stacktrace`
- verification outcome: the outer command timed out after 304 seconds while the Gradle Java worker continued; completed XML showed all six targeted PSI/non-PSI methods passed with `failures=0 errors=0`.

## Static/global initialization lambda boundary and diagnostic ranges

- problem type: static/global definite-initialization must not treat nested lambda or anonymous-function bodies as immediately executed static initializer effects, while static/global field and constructor-completion diagnostics must use the repository's token/header range policy.
- root cause: the shared initialization-flow expression dispatcher entered `CfirAnonymousFunctionExpression` bodies, so assignments and reads inside lambda arguments affected the enclosing static initialization state. Immutable static `let` assignment classification also treated nested assignments in a constructor/static-init context too broadly. Separately, `TYPE_UNINITIALIZED_STATIC_FIELD` fixtures still expected whole declarations, and `CLASS_UNINITIALIZED_FIELD` on explicit constructors still included the body braces.
- official Cangjie evidence: `cjc --diagnostic-format json --error-count-limit all tmp/codex-static-let-lambda-assign-probe.cj` produced no diagnostics for a static `let` initialized only inside a lambda argument; `tmp/codex-static-let-lambda-reassign-probe.cj` reported `sema_cannot_assign_to_immutable` on the nested `CTX = 10` assignment after a prior direct static-init assignment. Official implementation was traced through `external/cangjie_compiler/src/Sema/LegalityOfUsage/InitializationChecker.cpp` `IsUsedInInitFunction`, `NotAssignableVariable`, `CheckLetFlag`, `CheckLetFlagInMemberAccess`, and `external/cangjie_compiler/src/Sema/Diags.cpp` `DiagCannotAssignToImmutable`, which uses the assignment expression as the main diagnostic range.
- Kotlin counterpart files consulted: `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/checkers/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/analysis/cfa/FirPropertyInitializationAnalyzer.kt` and `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/checkers/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/analysis/checkers/expression/FirReassignmentAndInvisibleSetterChecker.kt`.
- CFIR owner files changed: `cfir/checkers/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/analysis/checkers/declaration/CfirInitializationCheckers.kt` and `cfir/checkers/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/analysis/checkers/expression/CfirAssignmentLegalityChecker.kt`.
- repair principle: keep static/global initialization in the shared flow owner, but model anonymous functions as value creation rather than immediate execution, and classify immutable-field assignment through the same constructor/static-init ownership instead of fixture-local branches.
- fixtures covered: `cfir/analysis-tests/testData/llt/class/static_or_global_var/static_or_global_var3.cj`, `static_or_global_var9.cj`, `static_or_global_var10.cj`, `static_or_global_var11.cj`, `static_or_global_var12.cj`, `static_or_global_var14/test1.cj`, and `static_or_global_var14/test2.cj`.
- fixture correction: `TYPE_UNINITIALIZED_STATIC_FIELD` now underlines the field-name token (`b`, `ctx`, `CTX`); explicit-constructor `CLASS_UNINITIALIZED_FIELD` now underlines the declaration header `public init()` without the body; `CANNOT_ASSIGN_TO_IMMUTABLE` on `CTX = 10` now covers the assignment expression.
- verification command: `.\gradlew.bat :cfir:analysis-tests:test --tests '*StaticOrGlobalVar*' --no-daemon --max-workers=1 --console=plain --stacktrace`
- verification outcome: `BUILD SUCCESSFUL`; the StaticOrGlobalVar generated family passed for the current PSI/non-PSI slice.
- guard command: `.\gradlew.bat :cfir:analysis-tests:test --tests '*InitializationCheck*' --no-daemon --max-workers=1 --console=plain --stacktrace`
- guard outcome: `BUILD FAILED`; XML/console showed `100 tests completed, 46 failed`, concentrated in broader `InitializationCheckGenerated` PSI/non-PSI fixtures plus `global_variable_not_assignable_*` and `static_variable_use_before_init_05`. These remaining failures are outside the verified StaticOrGlobalVar problem type and were not changed in this entry.

## While-let expression raw build, pattern scope, OR diagnostics, recovery, and Option target typing

- problem type: `while-let-expr` LLT failures across PSI and light-tree paths: PSI raw CFIR did not build `let` conditions, let-pattern bindings had incorrect loop-condition/body scope and redeclaration behavior, `||` conditions could introduce pattern variables without `VAR_IN_OR_CONDITION`, malformed `while (let ... <- ... {}` recovery treated the loop body as a trailing lambda, and `None` inside assignment/tuple/call contexts lost the expected `Option<T>` target type.
- root cause: PSI `CjIfExpression` and `CjWhileExpression` looked for `LET_EXPRESSION` as a direct child instead of inside the `CONDITION` container. Condition pattern bindings were registered for expression resolution but the conflict checker did not model a non-do-while loop condition and body as one local declaration scope, so body redeclarations were missed while outer declarations could be treated as conflicts. The expression checker set did not walk `CfirBinaryOpKind.OR` for visible let-pattern introductions. The parser did not mark malformed while-let condition boundaries strongly enough, allowing the following block to be consumed as a trailing lambda. Finally, tuple element checking and call-completion result writing did not reuse the existing no-arg enum constructor expected-type rule, so `None` stayed unresolved or candidate-diagnostic-backed when its expected type was `Option<T>`.
- official Cangjie evidence: `cjc` on a minimized `while (false || let Some(b) <- a) { return b - 1 }` reports `sema_var_in_or_condition`; `external/cangjie_compiler/include/cangjie/Basic/DiagRefactor/DiagnosticSema.def` defines that diagnostic, and `external/cangjie_compiler/src/Sema/TypeCheckExpr/IfExpr.cpp` `CheckBinaryCondition` collects variables in `||` and diagnoses the first introduced variable. `cjc` on `while (let Some(b) <- a) { let b = b }` reports redefinition for the body declaration and unresolved use on the initializer side; `external/cangjie_compiler/src/Sema/TypeCheckPattern.cpp` explicitly documents that `while (let a <- 1) { let a = 1 }` conflicts with a definition in the while-let body. `external/cangjie_compiler/src/Sema/Collector.cpp` documents the official `while (let p <- i) { t }` scope shape.
- Kotlin counterpart files consulted: `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/raw-fir/psi2fir/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/builder/PsiRawFirBuilder.kt`, `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/raw-fir/light-tree2fir/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/lightTree/converter/LightTreeRawFirExpressionBuilder.kt`, `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/resolve/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/resolve/calls/stages/ArgumentCheckingProcessor.kt`, and `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/resolve/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/resolve/calls/ArgumentUtils.kt`.
- CFIR owner files changed: `psi/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/psi/CjIfExpression.kt`, `psi/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/psi/CjWhileExpression.kt`, `psi/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/psi/CjLetExpression.kt`, `psi/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/parsing/AbstractCangJieParsing.kt`, `psi/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/parsing/CangJieExpressionParsing.kt`, `cfir/raw-cfir/psi2cfir/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/builder/PsiRawCfirBuilder.kt`, `cfir/raw-cfir/light-tree2cfir/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/lightTree/LightTreeRawCfirExpressionBuilder.kt`, `cfir/resolve/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/resolve/body/CfirExpressionsResolveTransformer.kt`, `cfir/resolve/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/resolve/body/CfirDeclarationsResolveTransformer.kt`, `cfir/resolve/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/resolve/transformers/body/resolve/BodyResolveContext.kt`, `cfir/checkers/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/analysis/checkers/CommonExpressionCheckers.kt`, `cfir/checkers/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/analysis/checkers/expression/CfirPatternExpressionChecker.kt`, `cfir/checkers/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/analysis/checkers/expression/CfirExpressionSemanticsChecker.kt`, `cfir/checkers/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/analysis/checkers/declaration/CfirConflictsDeclarationChecker.kt`, `cfir/resolve/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/resolve/calls/EnumConstructorTargetTyping.kt`, `cfir/resolve/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/resolve/calls/stages/ArgumentCheckingProcessor.kt`, and `cfir/resolve/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/resolve/inference/CfirCallCompletionResultsWriterTransformer.kt`.
- repair principle: keep while-let construction, condition binding scope, OR-condition diagnostics, malformed-condition recovery, and enum-constructor expected typing in their shared PSI/parser/CFIR resolve/checker owners, so PSI and light-tree LLT consume the same framework behavior rather than fixture-specific output shaping.

## If-let enhanced condition scope, OR diagnostics, or-pattern legality, and libast quote typing

- problem type: current `if_let_expr.enhancedcondition` LLT failures across PSI and light-tree paths plus macro libast failures under `macro.llt.if_let_expr.enhancedcondition`: let-pattern condition bindings did not consistently reach the right-hand side of `&&` and the successful then branch, `||` let-pattern diagnostics used incomplete condition-level range/source rules, let-condition or-pattern legality was only checked in match patterns, and `quote(...)` in libast array literals had no resolved token-stream type.
- root cause: enhanced-condition body resolve was not fully aligned to official `CheckCondition` / `CheckBinaryCondition` control flow, the local redeclaration checker did not model if-let condition bindings and then-branch top-level declarations as one fresh branch scope, the existing or-pattern checker was match-only and reported variable binding diagnostics inappropriate for condition-level `||`, and `CfirQuoteExpression` was not dispatched through expression resolve; when synthesized manually, it used the wrong libast result type for array-element inference.
- official Cangjie evidence: `external/cangjie_compiler/src/Sema/TypeCheckExpr/IfExpr.cpp` `CheckCondition` / `CheckBinaryCondition` resolves `&&` left-to-right, diagnoses variables introduced under `||`, and still checks both sides; `external/cangjie_compiler/src/Sema/LoopExprs.cpp` routes while conditions through the same condition checker; `external/cangjie_compiler/src/Sema/Collector.cpp` documents condition and successful-branch scope collection; `external/cangjie_compiler/src/Sema/TypeCheckPattern.cpp` contains the shared or-pattern binding/kind legality checks; `external/cangjie_compiler/src/Sema/TypeCheckExpr/QuoteExpr.cpp` uses the desugared quote expression type; `external/cangjie_compiler/src/Sema/Desugar/DesugarMacro.cpp` desugars `quote(...)` to `std.ast.Tokens(...)`, and `analysis/stubs/testData/builtins/std.ast.cjo.decompiled.text.cj` confirms `class Tokens` and libast constructors consuming `std.ast.Tokens`.
- Kotlin counterpart files consulted: `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/resolve/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/resolve/transformers/body/resolve/FirAbstractBodyResolveTransformerDispatcher.kt`, `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/resolve/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/resolve/transformers/body/resolve/FirExpressionsResolveTransformer.kt`, `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/checkers/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/analysis/checkers/expression/FirExpressionChecker.kt`, and `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/checkers/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/analysis/checkers/declaration/FirConflictsDeclarationChecker.kt`.
- CFIR owner files changed: `cfir/resolve/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/resolve/body/CfirExpressionsResolveTransformer.kt`, `cfir/resolve/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/resolve/body/CfirAbstractBodyResolveTransformer.kt`, `cfir/checkers/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/analysis/checkers/CommonExpressionCheckers.kt`, `cfir/checkers/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/analysis/checkers/expression/CfirPatternExpressionChecker.kt`, and `cfir/checkers/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/analysis/checkers/declaration/CfirConflictsDeclarationChecker.kt`.
- repair principle: keep enhanced-condition binding propagation, branch redeclaration scope, `||` condition diagnostics, let-condition or-pattern legality, and quote typing in shared resolve/checker dispatch owners so if, while, PSI, light-tree, and macro libast paths consume the same CFIR behavior.
- fixtures covered: `CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt.if_let_expr.enhancedcondition.EnhancedconditionGenerated`, `CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt.if_let_expr.enhancedcondition.EnhancedconditionGenerated`, `CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt.if_let_expr.enhancedcondition.o2.O2Generated`, `CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt.if_let_expr.enhancedcondition.o2.O2Generated`, `CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt.if_let_expr.enhancedcondition.parse.ParseGenerated`, `CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt.if_let_expr.enhancedcondition.parse.ParseGenerated`, `CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt.if_let_expr.enhancedcondition.patterns.PatternsGenerated`, `CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt.if_let_expr.enhancedcondition.patterns.PatternsGenerated`, `CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt.if_let_expr.enhancedcondition.precdence.PrecdenceGenerated`, `CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt.if_let_expr.enhancedcondition.precdence.PrecdenceGenerated`, `CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt.if_let_expr.enhancedcondition.vars.VarsGenerated`, `CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt.if_let_expr.enhancedcondition.vars.VarsGenerated`, the corresponding `while_let_expr.enhancedcondition` regression slice, and `macro.llt.if_let_expr.enhancedcondition.libast.LibastGenerated`.

## Flow expression desugaring, function-value context, and constructor argument diagnostics

- problem type: flow expressions must resolve `|>` / `~>` through the shared call resolver, preserve function references as function values, recover from root operand errors without cascading unresolved calls, and zero-parameter constructor calls with supplied arguments must report shared argument-mapping diagnostics on the full call source.
- root cause: `transformFlowExpression` desugared pipeline uniformly as `right.invoke(left)`, so function references, callable values, overload filtering, and expected function-type resolution did not follow the official flow-call shape. Composition operands were not resolved in flow function-value context, and root operand errors could leave the composition initializer with a non-callable error type, causing `let h = f ~> g; h(-1)` to emit a secondary unresolved `h`. Constructor argument diagnostics also treated named arguments on zero-parameter constructors as missing names and used argument-only source ranges.
- official Cangjie evidence: `external/cangjie_compiler/src/Sema/Desugar/DesugarInTypeCheck.cpp` `TryDesugarFunctionCallExpr`, `DesugarPipelineExpr`, and `DesugarCompositionExpr` show that pipeline builds `right(left)`, composition builds `composition(TryDesugar(left), TryDesugar(right))`, and non-function operands are converted to `expr.()` only when needed. `external/cangjie_compiler/src/Sema/TypeCheckExpr/BinaryExpr.cpp` `ChkFlowExpr` resolves operands under diagnostic suppression, calls normal call checking on the desugared expression, and recovers root operand errors without cascading outer diagnostics.
- Kotlin counterpart files consulted: `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/resolve/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/resolve/transformers/body/resolve/FirExpressionsResolveTransformer.kt` for context-dependent callable references and synthetic outer-call resolution, plus the existing CFIR call-resolution counterparts `CfirCallResolver`, `PostponedArgumentsAnalyzer`, and `functionTypeForFunctionValueCandidate`.
- CFIR owner files changed: `cfir/resolve/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/resolve/body/CfirExpressionsResolveTransformer.kt`, `cfir/resolve/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/resolve/calls/candidate/Candidate.kt`, `cfir/resolve/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/resolve/calls/tower/TowerLevelHandler.kt`, `cfir/resolve/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/resolve/calls/stages/CfirMapArguments.kt`, `cfir/checkers/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/analysis/diagnostics/coneDiagnosticToCfirDiagnostic.kt`, and `cfir/checkers/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/analysis/collectors/components/ErrorNodeDiagnosticCollectorComponent.kt`.
- repair principle: express flow through the existing call/candidate/constraint framework and use `ConeErrorType.delegatedType` only as a recoverable callable type carrier, so unresolved operand roots remain the only diagnostics while later function-value calls still resolve through normal callable-value candidates.
- fixtures covered: `CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt.flow_expr.FlowExprGenerated` and `CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt.flow_expr.FlowExprGenerated`, including `testPipeline7`, `testComposition5`, `testComposition91`, `testErrPipeline00`, `testErroFlowLambda`, `testPipeline9`, and the exposed `testPipeline8` guard.
- fixture correction: none; no `cfir/analysis-tests/testData/llt/flow_expr/*.cj` fixture was changed.
- verification command: `.\gradlew.bat :cfir:analysis-tests:test --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt.flow_expr.FlowExprGenerated' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt.flow_expr.FlowExprGenerated' --no-daemon --max-workers=1 --console=plain --stacktrace`
- verification outcome: `BUILD SUCCESSFUL`; XML showed `TEST-org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt.flow_expr.FlowExprGenerated.xml` and `TEST-org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt.flow_expr.FlowExprGenerated.xml` both had `tests=19 failures=0 errors=0`.
- regression command: `.\gradlew.bat :cfir:analysis-tests:test --no-daemon --max-workers=1 --console=plain --stacktrace`
- regression outcome: `BUILD FAILED` in unrelated families reported by the console (`CfirAnalysisMacro*`, `TryGenerated`, and `ConstEvalGenerated`). The test-results directory retained the FlowExpr XML proof above with `failures=0 errors=0`; no FlowExpr failure was reported in the full-run console.

## Generic static member access through type-parameter upper bounds

- problem type: static member access through exposed generic type parameters must select the closest static implementation from class/interface upper-bound intersections, diagnose instance members on generic qualifiers as generic static-access errors, preserve private static access-control diagnostics, and report inherited upper-bound return-type conflicts separately from call-resolution ambiguity.
- root cause: type-parameter qualifier static scopes concatenated upper-bound static scopes without modeling the official closer-implementation merge, so inherited static functions from interface/class upper bounds could remain ambiguous. Generic qualifier instance-member access reused the ordinary type-name non-static diagnostic instead of the exposed-generic diagnostic. Declaration-side generic upper-bound checking did not run the official inherited-member conflict pass over `where T <: C1 & I`, so `INHERIT_MEMBER_TYPE_INCONSISTENT` was missing even when call resolution correctly chose the class implementation.
- official Cangjie evidence: `cjc --diagnostic-format json --error-count-limit all tmp\codex-generic-static-functions-probe.cj` reported `sema_invalid_field_expose_access` for `U.a` where `a` is a public instance upper-bound member, `sema_invalid_access_control` for a private static member accessed through `U`, accepted closest static implementation selection for class/interface upper-bound intersections, and reported both `sema_return_type_incompatible` and `sema_inherit_member_type_inconsistent` for the `T <: C1 & I` static return-type conflict. Official implementation was traced through `external/cangjie_compiler/src/Sema/TypeCheckExpr/NameReferenceExpr.cpp`, `external/cangjie_compiler/src/Sema/TypeCheckReference.cpp`, `external/cangjie_compiler/src/Sema/InheritanceChecker/GenericInheritanceChecker.cpp`, `external/cangjie_compiler/src/Sema/InheritanceChecker/MergeInheritedMemberHelper.cpp`, and `external/cangjie_compiler/src/Sema/InheritanceChecker/StructInheritanceChecker.cpp`.
- Kotlin counterpart files consulted: `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/providers/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/scopes/FirKotlinScopeProvider.kt`, `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/resolve/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/resolve/calls/QualifierReceiver.kt`, `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/providers/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/scopes/impl/FirTypeIntersectionScope.kt`, `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/providers/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/scopes/impl/FirTypeIntersectionScopeContext.kt`, `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/providers/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/scopes/impl/AbstractFirUseSiteMemberScope.kt`, `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/checkers/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/analysis/checkers/declaration/FirOverrideChecker.kt`, and `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/checkers/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/analysis/checkers/declaration/FirImplementationMismatchChecker.kt`.
- CFIR owner files changed: `cfir/providers/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/scopes/impl/CfirClassStaticScope.kt`, `cfir/checkers/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/analysis/diagnostics/coneDiagnosticToCfirDiagnostic.kt`, `cfir/checkers/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/analysis/checkers/declaration/CfirTypeParameterBoundsChecker.kt`, `cfir/checkers/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/analysis/checkers/declaration/CfirOverrideChecker.kt`, and `cfir/checkers/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/analysis/checkers/declaration/CfirInheritanceDeepChecker.kt`.
- repair principle: keep static lookup, generic qualifier diagnostic mapping, and declaration-side upper-bound inherited-member conflict checking in their shared CFIR owners, so call resolution can select the closest static implementation while declaration diagnostics still see all inherited upper-bound member types.
- fixtures covered: `CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt.generics.generic_static_functions.GenericStaticFunctionsGenerated.testAccessByParameterClass4`, `testAccessByParameterClass6`, `testAccessByParameterClass7`, `testAccessByParameterInterface4`, `testAccessByParameterInterface9`, `testAccessByParameterInterface10`, and the corresponding `CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest` methods.
- fixture correction: `cfir/analysis-tests/testData/llt/generics/generic_static_functions/access_by_parameter_class_6.cj` now expects `INVALID_ACCESS_CONTROL` on `U` for private static member access through an exposed generic parameter, matching official `sema_invalid_access_control`, instead of `INVISIBLE_MEMBER` on `a`.

## Generic enum constructor member access inference and variadic argument shape

- problem type: generic enum constructor member access must infer the enum owner type from member-call arguments when possible, keep ordinary `Array<T>` arguments out of the variadic element path, and still report a bare generic enum constructor when the owner type cannot be inferred.
- root cause: substituted enum member value parameters were copied from the symbol's cached resolved return type instead of the declaration parameter type, so `a16(x: Array<T>)` could degrade to an element-level expectation. The call argument checker also treated a single `Array<Int64>` argument mapped to a variadic-capable `Array<T>` parameter as a variadic element, fixing `T` to `Array<Int64>` and producing `Array<Array<Int64>>`. Finally, the bare-generic access checker handled class/typealias classifiers but not enum constructor symbols whose generic owner remained uninstantiated after call completion.
- official Cangjie evidence: `cjc --diagnostic-format json --error-count-limit all cfir/analysis-tests/testData/llt/type_infer/infer_member_access_with_enum_ctor_succ.cj` produced no errors; only the unused-variable warning for `v`. A marker-free probe for `infer_member_access_with_enum_ctor_fail.cj` produced `sema_unable_to_infer_generic_func` on `T1.a16` and `sema_generic_type_without_type_argument` on `T1`. `external/cangjie_compiler/src/Sema/DeclAttributeChecker.cpp` `CheckEnumAttribute` enables `PUBLIC` on enum constructors; `external/cangjie_compiler/src/Sema/TypeCheckCall.cpp` treats enum constructors as global decls and builds owner mappings for enum-constructor member access; `external/cangjie_compiler/src/Sema/TypeArgumentInference.cpp` / `TypeCheckUtil.cpp` contain the `IsEnumCtorWithoutTypeArgs` target-typing and inference rules.
- Kotlin counterpart files consulted: `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/resolve/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/resolve/calls/stages/CheckArguments.kt`, `CreateFreshTypeVariableSubstitutorStage.kt`, `ResolutionStages.kt`, and `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/providers/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/scopes/impl/FirClassSubstitutionScope.kt`.
- CFIR owner files changed: `cfir/providers/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/scopes/impl/CfirClassSubstitutionScope.kt`, `cfir/resolve/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/resolve/calls/stages/CfirCreateFreshTypeVariableSubstitutorStage.kt`, `cfir/resolve/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/resolve/calls/stages/CfirCheckDispatchReceiver.kt`, `cfir/resolve/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/resolve/calls/stages/CfirCheckArguments.kt`, `cfir/resolve/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/resolve/calls/stages/ArgumentCheckingProcessor.kt`, `cfir/resolve/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/resolve/inference/CfirCallCompletionResultsWriterTransformer.kt`, `cfir/resolve/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/resolve/calls/EnumConstructorTargetTyping.kt`, and `cfir/checkers/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/analysis/checkers/expression/CfirGenericBareClassifierAccessChecker.kt`.
- repair principle: keep enum-constructor owner target typing, member substitution, argument-shape selection, and bare-generic diagnostics in their shared resolve/checker owners, so both successful and failed owner inference follow official behavior without fixture-specific handling.
