## Lambda fold pipeline PSI type-argument ownership and function-value completion

- problem type: `LambdaGenerated.testTestFold` PSI path reported `GENERIC_ARGUMENT_NO_MATCH` / `NO_MATCHING_OPERATOR_INVOKE` on `fold { ... }(Nil)` while the light-tree path had already resolved the same fixture; the lambda receiver subtree also needed completed function-value types to prevent stale postponed-lambda diagnostics.
- root cause: PSI `CjCallExpression.typeArgumentList` recursively searched arbitrary callee syntax and could capture a type argument from a trailing lambda body, so the outer function-value `invoke` for `fold { ... }(Nil)` received a spurious explicit type argument. Completion writing for function-call receivers also had to write back the resolved receiver subtree for nested function-value invocations.
- official Cangjie evidence: `cjc --diagnostic-format json --error-count-limit all cfir/analysis-tests/testData/llt/lambda/test_fold.cj` exited `0` with no diagnostics. Official flow behavior for this fixture family follows the already traced `external/cangjie_compiler/src/Sema/Desugar/DesugarInTypeCheck.cpp` `TryDesugarFunctionCallExpr` / `DesugarPipelineExpr` rules: `a |> f` is checked as `f(a)` through normal call checking.
- Kotlin counterpart files consulted: `external/kotlin/compiler/psi/psi-api/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/psi/KtCallExpression.kt`, where `typeArgumentList` is the call's own `TYPE_ARGUMENT_LIST` child and `typeArguments` are not found by recursive search into lambda bodies.
- CFIR owner files changed: `psi/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/psi/CjCallExpression.kt`, `cfir/resolve/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/resolve/body/CfirCallResolver.kt`, `cfir/resolve/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/resolve/body/CfirExpressionsResolveTransformer.kt`, `cfir/resolve/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/resolve/inference/CfirCallCompletionResultsWriterTransformer.kt`, and `cfir/resolve/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/resolve/calls/stages/CfirMapTypeArguments.kt`.
- repair principle: keep type-argument ownership in the PSI call model and function-value receiver completion in the shared call completion writer, so synthetic `invoke` calls never inherit type arguments from receiver expressions or lambda bodies.
- fixtures covered: `CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt.lambda.LambdaGenerated.testTestFold`, `CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt.lambda.LambdaGenerated.testTestFold`, plus the full PSI/non-PSI `LambdaGenerated` suites as regression guards.
- fixture correction: none; no `.cj` fixture was changed for this family.
- focused verification command: `.\gradlew.bat :cfir:analysis-tests:test --tests "org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt.lambda.LambdaGenerated.testTestFold" --tests "org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt.lambda.LambdaGenerated.testTestFold" --no-daemon --max-workers=1 --no-build-cache --console=plain --stacktrace`
- focused verification outcome: `BUILD SUCCESSFUL`.
- full-family verification command: `.\gradlew.bat :cfir:analysis-tests:test --tests "org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt.lambda.LambdaGenerated" --tests "org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt.lambda.LambdaGenerated" --no-daemon --max-workers=1 --console=plain --stacktrace`
- full-family verification outcome: `BUILD SUCCESSFUL`; XML showed both `LambdaGenerated` suites with `tests=32 failures=0 errors=0`.

## Generic enum constructor bare-owner diagnostic after uninferred completion

- problem type: `infer_member_access_with_enum_ctor_fail.cj` must report both `UNABLE_TO_INFER_GENERIC_FUNC` on the member call and `GENERIC_TYPE_SHOULD_BE_USED_WITH_TYPE_ARGUMENT` on the bare enum constructor owner when member-call arguments cannot fix `Test<T>`.
- root cause: completion fixes unresolved owner type variables to `ConeErrorType(isUninferredParameter=true)`. `CfirGenericBareClassifierAccessChecker` recognized declaration type parameters and live type variables but not the official uninferred-type result, so it treated `Test<uninferred>` as no longer containing an unfixed owner parameter and skipped the bare generic diagnostic.
- official Cangjie evidence: same family evidence as the preceding generic enum constructor entry: marker-free official `cjc` output for `infer_member_access_with_enum_ctor_fail.cj` reports `sema_unable_to_infer_generic_func` on `T1.a16` and `sema_generic_type_without_type_argument` on `T1`.

## Non-exhaustive enum declaration flag and known-subject match narrowing

- problem type: `nonExhaustiveEnum.bad.BadGenerated` must keep enum-body `...` as declaration-level semantics, report `NON_EXHAUSTIVE_MATCH` when declared enum constructors are covered but future constructors remain possible, and avoid `UNREACHABLE_PATTERN` false positives in known-subject and nested enum paths.
- root cause: the shared match model needs the official `NonExhaustiveEnum` virtual constructor for declaration-side `...`; after that owner is present, the remaining `BadGenerated` false positive was the unreachable-pattern checker narrowing a non-exhaustive enum subject to the currently known constructor set before the shared Maranget model could account for future constructors.
- official Cangjie evidence: `external/cangjie_compiler/src/Parse/ParseDecl.cpp` records enum-body `...` as `EnumDecl.hasEllipsis`; `external/cangjie_compiler/src/AST/Types.cpp` exposes it through `EnumTy::IsNonExhaustive()`; `external/cangjie_compiler/src/Sema/PatternUsefulness.cpp` adds `Constructor::NonExhaustiveEnum()` to enum constructor universes and renders that virtual constructor as `_`.
- Kotlin counterpart files consulted: `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/resolve/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/resolve/transformers/FirWhenExhaustivenessComputer.kt`, `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/resolve/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/resolve/transformers/body/resolve/FirControlFlowStatementsResolveTransformer.kt`, and `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/tree/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/expressions/ExhaustivenessStatus.kt`; Kotlin is used only for the shared exhaustiveness-owner shape because Cangjie non-exhaustive enum semantics are language-specific.
- CFIR owner files changed: `cfir/checkers/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/analysis/checkers/expression/CfirMatchUnreachablePatternChecker.kt`.
- CFIR owner files verified: `cfir/cfir-tree/tree-generator/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/tree/generator/CfirTree.kt`, generated `CfirEnum` tree files, PSI and LightTree raw builders, stub/flatbuffer deserialization, `CfirRenderer`, and the active shared match owner under `cfir/semantics/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/resolve/match`.
- repair principle: keep `...` on the enum declaration and let the shared Maranget constructor universe own future-constructor semantics; local known-subject optimizations must step aside whenever the subject type contains a non-exhaustive enum.
- fixtures covered: `CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt.nonExhaustiveEnum.bad.BadGenerated` and `CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt.nonExhaustiveEnum.bad.BadGenerated`.
- fixture correction: none; no `.cj` LLT fixture was changed for this problem type.
- focused verification command: `.\gradlew.bat :cfir:analysis-tests:test --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt.nonExhaustiveEnum.bad.BadGenerated' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt.nonExhaustiveEnum.bad.BadGenerated' --no-daemon --max-workers=1 --console=plain --stacktrace --no-build-cache --no-configuration-cache`
- focused verification outcome: `BUILD SUCCESSFUL`; XML showed both `BadGenerated` suites with `tests=6 failures=0 errors=0`.
- broader nonExhaustiveEnum command: `.\gradlew.bat :cfir:analysis-tests:test --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt.nonExhaustiveEnum.NonExhaustiveEnumGenerated' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt.nonExhaustiveEnum.NonExhaustiveEnumGenerated' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt.nonExhaustiveEnum.bad.BadGenerated' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt.nonExhaustiveEnum.bad.BadGenerated' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt.nonExhaustiveEnum.binaryCompat.BinaryCompatGenerated' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt.nonExhaustiveEnum.binaryCompat.BinaryCompatGenerated' --no-daemon --max-workers=1 --console=plain --stacktrace --no-build-cache --no-configuration-cache`

## Overload-by-lambda target-typed array literal candidate short-circuit

- problem type: `OverloadGenerated.testOverloadResolution3` spent unbounded time in overload-by-lambda candidate trials when a wrong candidate target-typed a deeply nested array literal and kept resolving all remaining elements after the first incompatible element.
- root cause: rollback analysis for overload-by-lambda candidates used the normal array-literal `transformChildren` path even when the expected array element type was already known. The wrong `(Int) -> Array<Component>` candidate failed on the first `Button(flag)` element but still recursively resolved later nested `Box { ... }` elements, repeatedly re-entering the same candidate-reduction path.
- official Cangjie evidence: `external/cangjie_compiler/src/Sema/TypeCheckBuiltinExpr.cpp` `ChkArrayLit` stops target-element checking after a failed element check; `external/cangjie_compiler/src/Sema/TypeCheckCall.cpp` returns candidate mismatch immediately on incompatible arguments and only invokes `ReInferCallArgs` for selected candidates.
- Kotlin counterpart files consulted: `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/resolve/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/resolve/calls/overloads/FirOverloadByLambdaReturnTypeResolver.kt`, `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/resolve/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/resolve/inference/FirPCLAInferenceSession.kt`, and `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/resolve/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/resolve/inference/PostponedArgumentsAnalyzer.kt`.
- CFIR owner files changed: `cfir/resolve/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/resolve/transformers/body/resolve/BodyResolveContext.kt`, `cfir/resolve/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/resolve/calls/overloads/CfirOverloadByLambdaBodyResolver.kt`, and `cfir/resolve/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/resolve/body/CfirExpressionsResolveTransformer.kt`.
- repair principle: keep speculative overload-by-lambda short-circuiting in the shared candidate-trial context and target-typed array resolver, while final selected-candidate analysis still uses the complete body path and preserves normal diagnostics.
- fixtures covered: `CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt.overload.OverloadGenerated.testOverloadResolution3`, `CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt.overload.OverloadGenerated.testOverloadResolution3`, and the PSI/non-PSI `overload_resolution_1..5` methods in `OverloadGenerated`.
- fixture correction: none; `cfir/analysis-tests/testData/llt/overload/overload_resolution_3.cj` was not changed.
- focused non-PSI command: `.\gradlew.bat :cfir:analysis-tests:test --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt.overload.OverloadGenerated.testOverloadResolution3' --no-daemon --max-workers=1 --console=plain --stacktrace --no-build-cache --no-configuration-cache`
- focused non-PSI outcome: `BUILD SUCCESSFUL`; the target no longer hung.
- focused PSI command: `.\gradlew.bat :cfir:analysis-tests:test --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt.overload.OverloadGenerated.testOverloadResolution3' --no-daemon --max-workers=1 --console=plain --stacktrace --no-build-cache --no-configuration-cache`
- focused PSI outcome: `BUILD SUCCESSFUL`; the target no longer hung.

All `testOverloadResolution1` through `testOverloadResolution5` methods passed in both PSI and non-PSI suites; the remaining failures are the unrelated `class_extends_*` and `class_impl_interface*` inheritance/interface fixtures.

## Overload inherited static/member kind diagnostic source

- problem type: `OverloadGenerated` class/interface inheritance fixtures reported `INHERIT_MEMBER_KIND_INCONSISTENT` correctly, but the diagnostic range was anchored to the first character of the declaration source (`public`) instead of the conflicting function name token.
- root cause: the shared `checkInheritedMemberKindConsistency` static/non-static conflict branch reused `ownInfo.source.firstCharacterDiagnosticSource()` for both extend-specific `STATIC_AND_NON_STATIC_MEMBER_CANNOT_HAVE_SAME_NAME` and class-like `INHERIT_MEMBER_KIND_INCONSISTENT`; class-like inherited-member-kind diagnostics need the member-name source already carried in `InheritedMemberInfo.nameSource`.
- official Cangjie evidence: range-only issue; the diagnostic already fired with the expected project diagnostic name and semantics, so the `cangjie-cfir-llt-repair` diagnostic range policy is the evidence source. No `.cj` fixture was changed.
- Kotlin counterpart files consulted: `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/checkers/gen/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/analysis/diagnostics/FirErrors.kt`, where `VIRTUAL_MEMBER_HIDDEN` uses `SourceElementPositioningStrategies.DECLARATION_NAME`, and `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/checkers/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/analysis/checkers/declaration/FirOverrideChecker.kt`, where the override checker reports on the callable declaration source while the diagnostic factory selects the declaration name.
- CFIR owner files changed: `cfir/checkers/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/analysis/checkers/declaration/CfirInheritanceDeepChecker.kt`.
- repair principle: keep the semantic conflict detection in the shared inheritance checker, but select the source per diagnostic owner: extend static/non-static shadowing keeps its existing declaration-start range, while class-like inherited member-kind inconsistency uses the inherited member model's member-name source.
- fixtures covered: `CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt.overload.OverloadGenerated` and `CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt.overload.OverloadGenerated`, including `testClassExtendsClass1`, `testClassExtendsClass2`, `testClassExtendsClass4`, `testClassExtendsClass5`, `testClassImplInterface1`, `testClassImplInterface2`, `testClassImplInterface3`, and `testClassImplInterface4`.
- fixture correction: none; no `.cj` fixture was changed for this problem type.
- verification command: `.\gradlew.bat :cfir:analysis-tests:test --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt.overload.OverloadGenerated' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt.overload.OverloadGenerated' --no-daemon --max-workers=1 --console=plain --stacktrace --no-build-cache --no-configuration-cache`
- verification outcome: `BUILD SUCCESSFUL`; XML showed both `OverloadGenerated` suites with `tests=14 failures=0 errors=0`.

## Generic static interface member through implicit extend receiver

- problem type: `O2_part_gi.param_desugarDecl` resolved inherited static interface function `foo<T>` through the extend body's implicit receiver and then reported `ARGUMENT_TYPE_MISMATCH` on the whole `extend<T> A<T> <: I<T>` declaration.
- root cause: `CfirCheckDispatchReceiver` treated the implicit receiver used to expose a static member scope as a runtime dispatch receiver and added a receiver subtype constraint. For `extend<T> A<T> <: I<T>`, that made the static interface member candidate `foo` compare type parameters from different declaration owners and surface a false receiver `ConstraintMismatch`.
- official Cangjie evidence: `cjc --diagnostic-format json --error-count-limit all cfir/analysis-tests/testData/llt/generics/instantiation/O2_part_gi/param_desugarDecl.cj` exited `0` with no diagnostics. A probe using `func foo<T>() { 1 }` as an `Int64` value also exited `0`, confirming the fixture's implicit function return was valid. Official implementation evidence: `external/cangjie_compiler/src/Sema/TypeCheckReference.cpp` filters object member access to static members as an explicit object-access error, while `external/cangjie_compiler/src/Sema/TypeCheckCall.cpp` `GetAllGenericTys` treats static member access as owner-generic inference input rather than an instance receiver subtype check.
- Kotlin counterpart files consulted: `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/resolve/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/resolve/calls/tower/FirTowerResolveTask.kt`, especially `toScopeBasedTowerLevelForStaticWithImplicitDispatchReceiver`, plus `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/resolve/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/resolve/calls/stages/ResolutionStages.kt` for dispatch receiver checking placement.
- CFIR owner files changed: `cfir/resolve/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/resolve/calls/stages/CfirCheckDispatchReceiver.kt`.
- repair principle: keep owner generic substitution in the existing candidate fresh-variable initialization, but skip dispatch receiver subtype checking when a static callable is reached only through an implicit receiver used for scope lookup.
- fixtures covered: `CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt.generics.instantiation.O2_part_gi.O2PartGiGenerated.testParamDesugarDecl`, `CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt.generics.instantiation.O2_part_gi.O2PartGiGenerated.testParamDesugarDecl`, and the full PSI/non-PSI `O2PartGiGenerated` class slice.
- fixture correction: none; no `.cj` fixture was changed for this problem type.
- focused verification command: `.\gradlew.bat :cfir:analysis-tests:test --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt.generics.instantiation.O2_part_gi.O2PartGiGenerated.testParamDesugarDecl' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt.generics.instantiation.O2_part_gi.O2PartGiGenerated.testParamDesugarDecl' --no-daemon --max-workers=1 --console=plain --stacktrace --no-build-cache --no-configuration-cache`
- focused verification outcome: `BUILD SUCCESSFUL`; both PSI and non-PSI triggering methods passed.
- full-slice verification command: `.\gradlew.bat :cfir:analysis-tests:test --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt.generics.instantiation.O2_part_gi.O2PartGiGenerated' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt.generics.instantiation.O2_part_gi.O2PartGiGenerated' --no-daemon --max-workers=1 --console=plain --stacktrace --no-build-cache --no-configuration-cache`

## LLT companion ancestor aggregate scanning canonicalization

- problem type: full `cfir:analysis-tests` regression could spend unbounded time in `LltCompanionSourceFilesProvider.collectAncestorAggregateFiles` while resolving repeated ancestor companion-file scans for LLT fixtures.
- root cause: the companion provider recomputed parent aggregate `.cj` files for every test file and repeatedly used `File.canonicalFile` / `File.getCanonicalPath` for identity checks. In the full LLT flow this made directory traversal and canonicalization dominate before the current generated test could reach semantic assertions.
- official Cangjie evidence: infrastructure-only failure; no Cangjie source semantics or fixture expectation changed. The observed evidence was the timed-out full LLT run with the test executor stack inside `LltCompanionSourceFilesProvider.collectAncestorAggregateFiles` and repeated `File.getCanonicalPath` calls.
- Kotlin counterpart files consulted: not applicable; this is the repository's LLT fixture-loading infrastructure, not compiler semantic behavior.
- CFIR owner files changed: `cfir/analysis-tests/testFixtures/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/analysis/tests/services/LltCompanionSourceFilesProvider.kt`.
- repair principle: cache ancestor aggregate-file scans by normalized parent directory path and use normalized absolute paths for test-data identity, so LLT companion discovery remains deterministic without repeated filesystem canonicalization.

## Same-project macro package import usage through construction registry

- problem type: same-project macro package imports used only by construction-stage macro calls were falsely reported as `UNUSED_IMPORT` after macro expansion removed or replaced the original `@Macro` surface from final CFIR.
- root cause: `CfirImportsChecker` collected ordinary referenced names from final CFIR and tried to recover macro surface usage by reverse-mapping each surface source element to the current file path. That was path-stable for PSI but not for the light-tree raw builder, so non-PSI same-project macro imports lost their construction-only usage record.
- official Cangjie evidence: `external/cangjie_compiler/src/Sema/CheckUnusedImportImpl.cpp` `IsImportContentUsedInMacro` checks macro package imports against `importManager.GetUsedMacroDecls(*importSpec.curFile)`; `external/cangjie_compiler/src/Macro/MacroEvaluation.cpp` `SaveUsedMacros` records used macro declarations with `AddUsedMacroDecls`; `external/cangjie_compiler/src/Modules/ImportManager.cpp` owns `AddUsedMacroDecls` / `GetUsedMacroDecls`. This proves used macro calls are file-level import usage facts, not ordinary AST references after expansion.
- Kotlin counterpart files consulted: `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/semantics/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/FirImportTrackerComponent.kt` and `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/entrypoint/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/session/IncrementalPassThroughImportTrackerComponent.kt` as the session-component/import-tracking architecture reference; Kotlin has no Cangjie macro equivalent.
- CFIR owner files changed: `cfir/providers/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/resolve/providers/macro/MacroConstructionApi.kt`, `compiler/frontend/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/frontend/pipeline/MacroExpandPhase.kt`, and `cfir/checkers/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/analysis/checkers/declaration/CfirImportsChecker.kt`.
- repair principle: record construction-consumed macro names in the session registry keyed by the same stable file identity used by macro cache keys, then let the shared imports checker query that file-level fact instead of depending on PSI/light-tree source reverse mapping.
- fixtures covered: `CfirAnalysisMacroTestGenerated` and `CfirAnalysisMacroPsiTestGenerated` same-project macro package methods: `testSameProjectMacroPackageDeclarationReference`, `testSameProjectMacroPackageExpressionRootPackage`, `testSameProjectMacroPackageExpressionAliasImport`, `testSameProjectMacroPackageImportOnly`, `testSameProjectMacroPackageExpressionAllUnderImport`, `testSameProjectMacroPackageExpression`, `testSameProjectMacroPackagePublicImportExpression`, and `testSameProjectMacroPackageExpressionAutoCompileDisabled`.
- fixture correction: none; no `.cj` fixture was changed for this problem type.
- focused verification command: `.\gradlew.bat :cfir:analysis-tests:test --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisMacroTestGenerated' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisMacroPsiTestGenerated' --no-daemon --max-workers=1 --console=plain --stacktrace --no-build-cache --no-configuration-cache`
- focused verification outcome: `BUILD SUCCESSFUL`; XML showed both generated macro suites with `tests=10 failures=0 errors=0`.
- guard command: `.\gradlew.bat :cfir:analysis-tests:test --tests '*UnusedImport*' --no-daemon --max-workers=1 --console=plain --stacktrace --no-build-cache --no-configuration-cache`
- guard outcome: `BUILD FAILED` with 98 tests completed and 42 failed; failures are the next ordinary unused-import/import-resolution family, not the same-project macro package generated suites.
- full LLT command: `.\gradlew.bat :cfir:analysis-tests:test --no-daemon --max-workers=1 --console=plain --stacktrace --no-build-cache --no-configuration-cache`
- full LLT outcome: `BUILD FAILED` with `8471 tests completed, 2470 failed, 310 skipped`; the previous same-project macro package `UNUSED_IMPORT` failures are no longer present.

## Ordinary unused-import import target and light-tree grouped import regression

- problem type: the already-closed `UnusedImportGenerated` family regressed across PSI and non-PSI paths: unused all-under imports were skipped, `internal import` fixtures were treated as reexporting source imports, inherited/extension member usage did not always mark imported declaration targets as used, and light-tree grouped imports such as `std.collection.{ArrayList, Map}` lost the directive prefix and became unresolved bare names.
- root cause: `CfirImportsChecker` still used name-only unused-import accounting for several paths and skipped all-under imports before checking resolved usage facts. Source import reexport detection treated `internal import` like public/protected reexport at checker time. The light-tree raw builder also built each `IMPORT_ITEM` from the item-local expression only, unlike PSI `CjImportItem.importedFqName`, which composes the directive base path with grouped import items.
- official Cangjie evidence: `external/cangjie_compiler/src/Sema/CheckUnusedImportImpl.cpp` records used declaration targets and packages through `AddUsedTarget`, `AddUsedPackage`, and `CollectUsedPackages`, filters imports in `CollectNeedCheckImports`, and checks star and named imports through `IsImportContentUsed`; `external/cangjie_compiler/src/Sema/TypeChecker.cpp` calls `CheckUnusedImportSpec`. A direct `cjc --diagnostic-format json --error-count-limit all` probe for `internal import std.collection.ArrayList` exited `0` with no diagnostics, so the `internal import` branch here follows this repository's previously verified `UnusedImportGenerated` LLT surface rather than changing fixtures in the middle of the regression repair.
- Kotlin counterpart files consulted: `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/checkers/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/analysis/checkers/declaration/FirImportsChecker.kt`, `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/resolve/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/resolve/transformers/FirImportResolveTransformer.kt`, and `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/resolve/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/scopes/ImportingScopes.kt`; PSI grouping parity was checked against `psi/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/psi/CjImportDirectiveItem.kt` and `cfir/raw-cfir/psi2cfir/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/builder/PsiRawCfirBuilder.kt`.
- CFIR owner files changed: `cfir/checkers/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/analysis/checkers/declaration/CfirImportsChecker.kt`, `cfir/providers/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/resolve/providers/CfirImportReexportInfo.kt`, and `cfir/raw-cfir/light-tree2cfir/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/lightTree/LightTreeRawCfirDeclarationBuilder.kt`.
- repair principle: keep unused-import decisions in the shared import checker using resolved target/package facts, keep source reexport classification in the provider helper, and make light-tree import construction match the PSI import model before import resolution instead of adding checker-side recovery for malformed FQNs.
- fixtures covered: PSI and non-PSI `UnusedImportGenerated`, including ordinary `unusedImport001`, `unusedImport002`, `unusedImport003`, macro unused-import slices selected by the same generated-class filter, all-under import cases, internal import cases, inherited/extension member usage cases, and grouped import cases.
- fixture correction: none; no `.cj` fixture was changed for this problem type.
- verification command: `.\gradlew.bat :cfir:analysis-tests:test --tests '*UnusedImportGenerated' --no-daemon --max-workers=1 --console=plain --stacktrace --no-build-cache --no-configuration-cache`
- verification outcome: `BUILD SUCCESSFUL`; the focused PSI/non-PSI unused-import family completed with no failures.

## Qualified declaration macro import usage in PSI and light-tree macro LLT

- problem type: `macro.llt.unusedImport` package imports used only by qualified declaration macros such as `@macro_definition.ModifyFunc` and `@a.Derive[...]` were still reported as `UNUSED_IMPORT` on PSI, while the same construction-stage usage must keep the import live after macro expansion.
- root cause: the macro construction registry did not expose construction-consumed macro names as a file-level import usage fact, and the PSI top-level parser consumed only the first identifier after `@`, so qualified declaration macro surfaces never reached the PSI raw builder as the same `MACRO_EXPRESSION` shape that light-tree already used.
- official Cangjie evidence: `external/cangjie_compiler/src/Sema/CheckUnusedImportImpl.cpp` checks macro imports through `IsImportContentUsedInMacro` against `importManager.GetUsedMacroDecls(*importSpec.curFile)`; `external/cangjie_compiler/src/Macro/MacroCallResolve.cpp` resolves qualified macro names through imported package declarations; `external/cangjie_compiler/src/Modules/ImportManager.cpp` owns the used-macro declaration table. This makes macro usage a per-file import fact, not an ordinary final-AST reference.
- Kotlin counterpart files consulted: `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/semantics/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/FirImportTrackerComponent.kt`, `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/resolve/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/resolve/transformers/FirImportResolveTransformer.kt`, `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/resolve/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/scopes/ImportingScopes.kt`, and `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/checkers/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/analysis/checkers/declaration/FirImportsChecker.kt`; Kotlin is used only for import tracking/checker ownership because Cangjie macro semantics come from the official compiler.
- CFIR owner files changed: `cfir/providers/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/resolve/providers/macro/MacroConstructionApi.kt`, `compiler/frontend/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/frontend/pipeline/MacroExpandPhase.kt`, `cfir/checkers/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/analysis/checkers/declaration/CfirImportsChecker.kt`, `cfir/checkers/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/analysis/collectors/components/ErrorNodeDiagnosticCollectorComponent.kt`, `cfir/raw-cfir/psi2cfir/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/builder/PsiRawCfirBuilder.kt`, `cfir/raw-cfir/light-tree2cfir/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/lightTree/LightTreeRawCfirDeclarationBuilder.kt`, `psi/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/parsing/CangJieParsing.kt`, and `psi/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/parsing/CangJieExpressionParsing.kt`.
- repair principle: record macro construction usage in the shared session registry and make both PSI and light-tree produce/preserve qualified declaration macro surfaces before unused-import checking, instead of teaching the checker fixture-specific names or relying on final expanded CFIR to still contain macro call syntax.
- fixtures covered: PSI and non-PSI `CfirAnalysisMacroTest.macro.llt.unusedImport.*` and `CfirAnalysisMacroPsiTest.macro.llt.unusedImport.*`, including `unusedImport001.unused014` and `unusedImport005.unused006`.
- fixture correction: none; no `.cj` fixture was changed for this problem type.
- focused verification command: `.\gradlew.bat :cfir:analysis-tests:test --tests '*macro.llt.unusedImport.unusedImport001.unused014.Unused014Generated' --tests '*macro.llt.unusedImport.unusedImport005.unused006.Unused006Generated' --no-daemon --max-workers=1 --console=plain --stacktrace --no-build-cache --no-configuration-cache`
- focused verification outcome: `BUILD SUCCESSFUL`; XML showed both PSI and non-PSI generated suites with `tests=2 failures=0 errors=0`.
- full macro unused-import slice command: `.\gradlew.bat :cfir:analysis-tests:test --tests '*macro.llt.unusedImport*' --no-daemon --max-workers=1 --console=plain --stacktrace --no-build-cache --no-configuration-cache`
- full macro unused-import slice outcome: `BUILD SUCCESSFUL`; XML showed all generated `macro.llt.unusedImport` PSI and non-PSI suites with `failures=0 errors=0`.

## Catch parameter redeclaration scope in diagnostics2 try

- problem type: `diagnostics2/try` fixtures with repeated catch parameter names across separate catch clauses falsely reported `REDECLARATION` on the later catch parameter.
- root cause: the local redeclaration visitor did not open a separate local declaration scope for each `CfirCatch`, so catch pattern binding variables leaked into the enclosing function/block scope and conflicted with parameters from sibling catch clauses.
- official Cangjie evidence: `external/cangjie_compiler/include/cangjie/AST/Node.h` models `TryExpr` with parallel `catchPatterns` and `catchBlocks`; `external/cangjie_compiler/src/Sema/Collector.cpp` states that each catch block has the same scope as its catch pattern and calculates that scope per catch block/pattern pair.
- Kotlin counterpart files consulted: `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/tree/gen/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/expressions/FirCatch.kt` and `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/resolve/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/resolve/transformers/body/resolve/FirControlFlowStatementsResolveTransformer.kt`, where `transformCatch` enters the catch clause and resolves the parameter plus block inside a block context.
- CFIR owner files changed: `cfir/checkers/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/analysis/checkers/declaration/CfirConflictsDeclarationChecker.kt`.
- repair principle: model each catch clause as its own local declaration scope while sharing that scope between the catch parameter and catch body, instead of suppressing `REDECLARATION` for selected names or fixtures.
- fixtures covered: `diagnostics2/try/catchOrderingAndDuplicate.cj`, `diagnostics2/try/duplicateCatchPlaceholder.cj`, `diagnostics2/try/multiCatchJoinMismatch.cj`, `diagnostics2/try/tryMultipleCatchJoinMatrix.cj`, and `diagnostics2/try/unreachableCatchPlaceholder.cj` in PSI and non-PSI `TryGenerated`; `WithoutAliasExpansion` was also included in the focused run.
- fixture correction: none; no `.cj` fixture was changed for this problem type.
- verification command: `.\gradlew.bat :cfir:analysis-tests:test --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisDiagnostics2Test.diagnostics2._try.TryGenerated' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisDiagnostics2PsiTest.diagnostics2._try.TryGenerated' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisDiagnostics2WithoutAliasExpansionTest.diagnostics2._try.TryGenerated' --no-daemon --max-workers=1 --console=plain --stacktrace --no-build-cache --no-configuration-cache`
- verification outcome: `BUILD SUCCESSFUL`; XML showed `CfirAnalysisDiagnostics2Test.diagnostics2._try.TryGenerated` with `tests=11 failures=0 errors=0 skipped=0`, `CfirAnalysisDiagnostics2PsiTest.diagnostics2._try.TryGenerated` with `tests=11 failures=0 errors=0 skipped=0`, and `CfirAnalysisDiagnostics2WithoutAliasExpansionTest.diagnostics2._try.TryGenerated` with `tests=11 failures=0 errors=0 skipped=10`.

## Const-eval integer multiplication overflow and divisor-zero diagnostics

- problem type: `diagnostics/const-eval` and `diagnostics2/const-eval` failed to report `CONST_EVAL_ARITHMETIC_OVERFLOW` for integer multiplication and failed to report `CONST_EVAL_DIVIDE_BY_ZERO` for literal `/ 0` and `% 0` expressions.
- root cause: `CfirConstEvalArithmeticChecker` excluded `TIMES` from the shared overflow-reporting operator set, and the divisor-zero branch skipped primitive literal calls whose callee reference was not a resolved named reference even though the shared primitive operator predicate could already prove the call was an integer primitive operator.
- official Cangjie evidence: `cjc --diagnostic-format json --error-count-limit all cfir/analysis-tests/build/codex-probes/constEvalProbe.cj` reported `chir_arithmetic_operator_overflow` for both `9223372036854775807 + 1` and `9223372036854775807 * 2`, and `chir_divisor_is_zero` for both `10 / 0` and `10 % 0`. `external/cangjie_compiler/include/cangjie/CHIR/Analysis/ConstAnalysis.h` handles integer `ADD`, `SUB`, `MUL`, `DIV`, and `MOD` through `HandleArithmeticOpOfInt`, raises `RaiseDivByZeroError` when `DIV`/`MOD` right operand is zero, and maps `MUL`, `DIV`, and `MOD` in `RaiseArithmeticOverflowError`.
- Kotlin counterpart files consulted: `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/checkers/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/analysis/checkers/expression/FirDivisionByZeroChecker.kt`, `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/providers/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/expressions/FirExpressionEvaluator.kt`, and `external/kotlin/compiler/frontend/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/resolve/constants/evaluate/ConstantExpressionEvaluator.kt`; Kotlin keeps division-by-zero and integer-overflow checks in constant-expression/operator evaluation owners.
- CFIR owner files changed: `cfir/checkers/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/analysis/checkers/expression/CfirConstEvalArithmeticChecker.kt`.
- repair principle: keep constant integer arithmetic diagnostics in the shared const-eval arithmetic checker by using the same primitive-operator predicate for divisor-zero diagnostics and applying overflow range checks uniformly to multiplication as well as other integer arithmetic operators.
- fixtures covered: PSI and non-PSI `CfirAnalysisDiagnosticsTest.diagnostics.const_eval.ConstEvalGenerated`, `CfirAnalysisDiagnosticsPsiTest.diagnostics.const_eval.ConstEvalGenerated`, `CfirAnalysisDiagnostics2Test.diagnostics2.const_eval.ConstEvalGenerated`, `CfirAnalysisDiagnostics2PsiTest.diagnostics2.const_eval.ConstEvalGenerated`, plus both `WithoutAliasExpansion` generated classes in the same const-eval slices.
- fixture correction: none; no `.cj` fixture was changed for this problem type.
- verification command: `.\gradlew.bat :cfir:analysis-tests:test --tests '*diagnostics.const_eval.ConstEvalGenerated' --tests '*diagnostics2.const_eval.ConstEvalGenerated' --no-daemon --max-workers=1 --console=plain --stacktrace --no-build-cache --no-configuration-cache`
- verification outcome: `BUILD SUCCESSFUL`; XML showed all six `ConstEvalGenerated` suites with `failures=0 errors=0`, and the two `WithoutAliasExpansion` suites had `skipped=3` as expected.

- root cause: several old inline expectations still anchored diagnostics on the first character of modifiers such as `public`, on whole assignments/declarations, or on only a callee name where this project's Diagnostic Range Policy expects the full relevant token or call expression.
- official Cangjie evidence: range-only correction governed by this skill's Diagnostic Range Policy; no semantic diagnostic firing condition changed, and `cjc` narrow CLI anchors are intentionally not used as the IDE-facing range authority.

- CFIR owner files changed: none.

## ClassGenerated constructor/member/static-global initialization flow

- problem type: constructor and member initialization flow in PSI and non-PSI `ClassGenerated`, including nested function/lambda capture before initialization, member calls before all instance fields are initialized, delegated constructor state, struct/member-initializer implicit receiver checks, same-file forward global reads from instance initializers, and binding-variable type projection needed by initializer diagnostics.
- root cause: `CfirInitializationCheckers` skipped anonymous/nested function bodies for capture-before-initialization, treated assignments inside nested functions as if they initialized the outer constructor state, did not model `this(...)` delegation as initializing instance fields after its arguments, over-skipped implicit struct member access in field initializers, and originally scanned all file-level static/global symbols from instance initializers instead of only top-level globals. `ReturnTypeCalculatorWithJump` could also fail when a `CfirPatternBindingVariable` requested its type before the owning pattern variable had projected binding types.
- official Cangjie evidence: `external/cangjie_compiler/src/CHIR/Checker/VarInitCheck.cpp` checks member function calls before all fields are initialized through `CheckMemberFuncCall`, `UseBeforeInitCheck`, and `RaiseIllegalMemberFunCallError`; `external/cangjie_compiler/src/Sema/LegalityOfUsage/InitializationChecker.cpp` reports `sema_undefined_variable`, `sema_capture_before_initialization`, `sema_class_uninitialized_field`, and `sema_illegal_usage_of_member`, and `IsVarUsedBeforeDefinition` distinguishes ordinary forward global references from class-like/static contexts. `external/cangjie_compiler/src/Sema/LegalityOfUsage/GlobalVarChecker.cpp` owns global/static initialization dependency collection. `cjc --diagnostic-format json --error-count-limit all cfir/analysis-tests/build/codex-probes/class_static_global_forward_static_member_probe.cj` accepted `A.a1 = B.b`, while `class_static_global_forward_global_probe.cj` reported `sema_undefined_variable` on `c` in `A.a = c`.
- Kotlin counterpart files consulted: `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/checkers/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/analysis/collectors/components/ControlFlowAnalysisDiagnosticComponent.kt`; Kotlin's FIR control-flow diagnostic component keeps initialization diagnostics attached to graph-owner/control-flow traversal rather than fixture-specific declaration checks.
- CFIR owner files changed: `cfir/checkers/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/analysis/checkers/declaration/CfirInitializationCheckers.kt`, `cfir/resolve/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/resolve/body/ReturnTypeCalculatorWithJump.kt`, and fixture range correction in `cfir/analysis-tests/testData/llt/class/class_no_member_func_use_before_init4.cj`.
- repair principle: keep initialization state, nested capture handling, static/global order checks, and binding type projection in their shared CFIR owners so both raw-builder and PSI paths observe the same official initialization semantics.
- fixtures covered: PSI and non-PSI `CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt._class.ClassGenerated` / `CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt._class.ClassGenerated` methods `testClassNoMemberFuncUseBeforeInit0`, `testClassNoMemberFuncUseBeforeInit3`, `testClassNoMemberFuncUseBeforeInit4`, `testClassInitWithNestedFunc0`, `testClassInitWithNestedFunc1`, `testClassInitDefaultConstructor1`, and `testClassInitialization5`.
- fixture correction: narrowed the stale range in `class_no_member_func_use_before_init4.cj` for `CANNOT_ASSIGN_TO_IMMUTABLE`; no semantic fixture expectation was changed.
- focused verification command: `.\gradlew.bat :cfir:analysis-tests:test --tests '*ClassGenerated.testClassNoMemberFuncUseBeforeInit0' --tests '*ClassGenerated.testClassNoMemberFuncUseBeforeInit3' --tests '*ClassGenerated.testClassNoMemberFuncUseBeforeInit4' --tests '*ClassGenerated.testClassInitWithNestedFunc0' --tests '*ClassGenerated.testClassInitWithNestedFunc1' --tests '*ClassGenerated.testClassInitDefaultConstructor1' --tests '*ClassGenerated.testClassInitialization5' --no-daemon --max-workers=1 --console=plain --stacktrace --no-build-cache --no-configuration-cache`
- focused verification outcome: `BUILD SUCCESSFUL`.
- regression command: `.\gradlew.bat :cfir:analysis-tests:test --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt._class.ClassGenerated' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt._class.ClassGenerated' --no-daemon --max-workers=1 --console=plain --stacktrace --no-build-cache --no-configuration-cache`
- regression outcome: `464 tests completed, 52 failed`; the covered initialization methods no longer appear in the remaining failure list.

## ClassGenerated main entry signature diagnostics

- problem type: PSI and non-PSI `ClassGenerated` missed official `main` entry signature diagnostics for invalid entry parameters and had stale narrow inline range for `class_main1.cj`.
- root cause: CFIR only checked missing-entry and common-package entry constraints; it had no generated diagnostics or declaration checker for official `CheckEntryFunc` parameter/return signature rules on `CfirMainFunction`, and `main` source selection reused no token-level `MAIN_KEYWORD` path.
- official Cangjie evidence: `external/cangjie_compiler/src/Sema/TypeCheckDecl.cpp` `TypeChecker::TypeCheckerImpl::CheckEntryFunc` reports `sema_unexpected_return_type_for_entry` when an explicit return type is neither integer nor `Unit`, and reports `sema_unexpected_param_for_entry` when entry parameters are more than one or not `Array<String>`; `external/cangjie_compiler/include/cangjie/Basic/DiagnosticSema.def` defines both messages. Probe `cfir/analysis-tests/build/codex-probes/class_main_entry_signature_probe.cj` with `main(a: Int32): Int64` reported `sema_unexpected_param_for_entry`.
- Kotlin counterpart files consulted: `external/kotlin/compiler/frontend/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/idea/MainFunctionDetector.kt`, which keeps main-function parameter and return type validation in a dedicated main-function detector/signature owner and checks the resolved descriptor types rather than fixture text.
- CFIR owner files changed: `cfir/checkers/checkers-component-generator/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/checkers/generator/diagnostics/CfirDiagnosticsList.kt`, `cfir/checkers/gen/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/analysis/diagnostics/CfirErrors.kt`, `cfir/checkers/gen/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/analysis/diagnostics/CfirNonSuppressibleErrorNames.kt`, `cfir/checkers/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/analysis/diagnostics/CfirErrorsDefaultMessages.kt`, `cfir/checkers/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/analysis/checkers/CommonDeclarationCheckers.kt`, `cfir/checkers/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/analysis/checkers/declaration/CfirMainFunctionSignatureChecker.kt`, and `cfir/checkers/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/analysis/checkers/declaration/CfirDeclarationDiagnosticSources.kt`.
- repair principle: attach official entry signature validation to the shared `CfirMainFunctionChecker` pipeline and select the `MAIN_KEYWORD` token as the diagnostic source for both PSI and light-tree paths, instead of adapting one fixture or reporting from whole declarations.
- fixtures covered: PSI and non-PSI `CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt._class.ClassGenerated.testClassMain1` / `CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt._class.ClassGenerated.testClassMain1`, plus the full PSI/non-PSI `ClassGenerated` slice.
