## ClassGenerated override/implement return type incompatibility diagnostics

- problem type: PSI and non-PSI `ClassGenerated` used stale `OVERRIDING_RETURN_TYPE_MISMATCH` expectations and, for explicit `redef`, CFIR reported both the old override diagnostic and official `RETURN_TYPE_INCOMPATIBLE`.
- root cause: `CfirOverrideChecker` still owned ordinary function return-type mismatch reporting with `OVERRIDING_RETURN_TYPE_MISMATCH`, while `CfirInheritanceDeepChecker` already owned the official inheritance relation diagnostic `RETURN_TYPE_INCOMPATIBLE`, producing duplicate diagnostics for explicit `redef` and inconsistent diagnostic names for implicit interface implementation.
- official Cangjie evidence: `cjc --diagnostic-format json --error-count-limit all` probes `class_override_return_redef_probe.cj`, `class_interface_return_impl_probe.cj`, `class_interface_return_body_subtype_probe.cj`, and `interface_implement_invalid_2_probe.cj` all reported `sema_return_type_incompatible` for ordinary override/redef/implement return-type incompatibility; `interface_implement_invalid_2_probe.cj` also reported `sema_undeclared_type_name` for missing supertype `I2` and did not report a second missing-implementation diagnostic for `C3`. Implementation evidence is `external/cangjie_compiler/src/Sema/InheritanceChecker/StructInheritanceChecker.cpp` `StructInheritanceChecker::CheckImplementationRelation`, which reports `DiagKindRefactor::sema_return_type_incompatible` for incompatible function return types after same-signature implementation matching; `external/cangjie_compiler/include/cangjie/Basic/DiagRefactor/DiagnosticSema.def` defines the diagnostic message.
- Kotlin counterpart files consulted: `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/checkers/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/analysis/checkers/declaration/FirOverrideChecker.kt` for direct override return-type checker placement and `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/checkers/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/analysis/checkers/declaration/FirImplementationMismatchChecker.kt` for inherited/intersection implementation mismatch ownership.
- CFIR owner files changed: `cfir/checkers/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/analysis/checkers/declaration/CfirOverrideChecker.kt`; fixture corrections in `cfir/analysis-tests/testData/llt/class/class_redef_9.cj`, `class_impl_interface_abstract_func7.cj`, `class_impl_interface_abstract_func8.cj`, and `interface_implement_invalid_2.cj`.
- repair principle: keep ordinary function return-type incompatibility in the shared inheritance relation checker that already maps to official `sema_return_type_incompatible`, while preserving `CfirOverrideChecker` only for its distinct direct-override checks such as `This` return preservation and static-interface special handling.
- fixtures covered: PSI and non-PSI `CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt._class.ClassGenerated` / `CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt._class.ClassGenerated` methods `testClassRedef9`, `testClassImplInterfaceAbstractFunc7`, `testClassImplInterfaceAbstractFunc8`, and `testInterfaceImplementInvalid2`.
- fixture correction: replaced stale `OVERRIDING_RETURN_TYPE_MISMATCH` markers with `RETURN_TYPE_INCOMPATIBLE`; removed stale `ABSTRACT_MEMBER_NOT_IMPLEMENTED` from `C3` in `interface_implement_invalid_2.cj`; changed missing supertype `I2` markers from `UNRESOLVED_REFERENCE` to `UNDECLARED_TYPE_NAME` according to official `sema_undeclared_type_name`.
- focused verification command: `.\gradlew.bat :cfir:analysis-tests:test --tests '*ClassGenerated.testClassRedef9' --tests '*ClassGenerated.testClassImplInterfaceAbstractFunc7' --tests '*ClassGenerated.testClassImplInterfaceAbstractFunc8' --tests '*ClassGenerated.testInterfaceImplementInvalid2' --no-daemon --max-workers=1 --console=plain --stacktrace --no-build-cache --no-configuration-cache`
- focused verification outcome: `BUILD SUCCESSFUL`; both PSI and non-PSI triggering methods passed.
- regression command: `.\gradlew.bat :cfir:analysis-tests:test --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt._class.ClassGenerated' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt._class.ClassGenerated' --no-daemon --max-workers=1 --console=plain --stacktrace --no-build-cache --no-configuration-cache`
- regression outcome: `464 tests completed, 42 failed`; `testClassRedef9`, `testClassImplInterfaceAbstractFunc7`, `testClassImplInterfaceAbstractFunc8`, and `testInterfaceImplementInvalid2` no longer appear in the remaining failure list.

## ClassGenerated implicit default constructor super-call diagnostics

- problem type: PSI and non-PSI `ClassGenerated` expected the stale `EXPLICIT_SUPER_CALL_REQUIRED` diagnostic for implicit default constructors whose superclass has no no-arg constructor, while official Cangjie reports the no-parameter-super-constructor diagnostic on the child class name.
- root cause: `CfirConstructorDelegationChecker` already detected the missing no-arg superclass constructor but selected the synthetic constructor source. For implicit primary constructors whose source spans the owning class-like declaration, the diagnostic must be anchored to the owner type name rather than the synthesized constructor name source.
- official Cangjie evidence: `cjc --diagnostic-format json --error-count-limit all cfir/analysis-tests/build/codex-probes/class_default_constructor_super_call_probe.cj` reported `sema_no_non_param_constructor_in_super_class` on child class names `B` and `Tree_None`. `external/cangjie_compiler/src/Sema/TypeChecker.cpp` `TypeChecker::TypeCheckerImpl::CheckConstructorSuper` uses `MakeRange(decl.identifier)` for compiler-added `super()` and implicit constructor cases; `external/cangjie_compiler/include/cangjie/Basic/DiagRefactor/DiagnosticSema.def` defines the diagnostic.
- Kotlin counterpart files consulted: `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/checkers/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/analysis/checkers/declaration/FirCommonConstructorDelegationIssuesChecker.kt`, where constructor-delegation diagnostics are reported from the constructor/delegation owner rather than ad hoc fixture sites.
- CFIR owner files changed: `cfir/checkers/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/analysis/checkers/declaration/CfirConstructorDelegationChecker.kt`; fixture corrections in `cfir/analysis-tests/testData/llt/class/class_init_default_constructor4.cj` and `class_init_default_constructor10.cj`.
- repair principle: keep implicit-super validation in the shared constructor delegation checker and select the class-like owner name only for compiler-generated primary constructors whose source is the owner declaration, so PSI and light-tree paths use the same official diagnostic owner.
- fixtures covered: PSI and non-PSI `CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt._class.ClassGenerated` / `CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt._class.ClassGenerated` methods `testClassInitDefaultConstructor4` and `testClassInitDefaultConstructor10`.
- fixture correction: replaced stale `EXPLICIT_SUPER_CALL_REQUIRED` markers with `NO_NON_PARAM_CONSTRUCTOR_IN_SUPER_CLASS` and narrowed both ranges to the child class-name tokens.
- focused verification command: `.\gradlew.bat :cfir:analysis-tests:test --tests '*ClassGenerated.testClassInitDefaultConstructor4' --tests '*ClassGenerated.testClassInitDefaultConstructor10' --no-daemon --max-workers=1 --console=plain --stacktrace --no-build-cache --no-configuration-cache --rerun-tasks`
- focused verification outcome: `BUILD SUCCESSFUL`; both PSI and non-PSI triggering methods passed after a forced rerun.
- regression command: `.\gradlew.bat :cfir:analysis-tests:test --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt._class.ClassGenerated' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt._class.ClassGenerated' --no-daemon --max-workers=1 --console=plain --stacktrace --no-build-cache --no-configuration-cache`
- regression outcome: `464 tests completed, 38 failed`; `testClassInitDefaultConstructor4` and `testClassInitDefaultConstructor10` no longer appear in the remaining failure list.

## ClassGenerated illegal interface super receiver recovery

- problem type: PSI and non-PSI `ClassGenerated` reported selector/call cascades after illegal `super` use inside interfaces: `interface_super_invalid_1.cj` produced `UNRESOLVED_REFERENCE` on `super.a()`, and `interface_super_invalid_2.cj` left `var c = super.a` without a callable function-value type so `c()` was reported unresolved.
- root cause: `CfirExpressionsResolveTransformer.transformSuperReceiverExpression` used only `BodyResolveContext.containingRegularClass`, so `super` in an interface was typed as an unrecoverable error before member resolution. The error-node collector also only treated extend `super` as an illegal-owner cascade, so selector diagnostics after interface `super` were still emitted.
- official Cangjie evidence: `cjc --diagnostic-format json --error-count-limit all cfir\analysis-tests\build\codex-probes\class_interface_super_probe.cj` reported exactly two `sema_use_super_in_interface` diagnostics, on `super.a()` and `let f = super.a`, with no unresolved selector or unresolved `f()`/`c()` diagnostics. `external/cangjie_compiler/src/Sema/TypeCheckReference.cpp` `TypeChecker::TypeCheckerImpl::CheckUsageOfSuper` gets the current inheritable declaration via `GetCurInheritableDecl`, reports `sema_use_super_in_interface` for `InterfaceDecl`, and leaves reference legality checking separate from name/member resolution.
- Kotlin counterpart files consulted: `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/resolve/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/resolve/transformers/body/resolve/FirExpressionsResolveTransformer.kt`, where `transformSuperReceiver` recovers `super` from the current implicit dispatch receiver's `superTypeRefs`; and `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/checkers/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/analysis/collectors/components/ErrorNodeDiagnosticCollectorComponent.kt`, where unresolved diagnostics are skipped when the receiver cannot be resolved.
- CFIR owner files changed: `cfir/resolve/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/resolve/transformers/body/resolve/BodyResolveContext.kt`, `cfir/resolve/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/resolve/body/CfirExpressionsResolveTransformer.kt`, and `cfir/checkers/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/analysis/collectors/components/ErrorNodeDiagnosticCollectorComponent.kt`.
- repair principle: keep illegal-`super` legality in `CfirIllegalSuperReferenceChecker`, but let body resolve recover the current class-like owner and direct interface supertype for member typing, while the shared error collector suppresses selector cascades for all illegal `super` owner kinds.
- fixtures covered: PSI and non-PSI `CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt._class.ClassGenerated` / `CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt._class.ClassGenerated` methods `testInterfaceSuperInvalid1` and `testInterfaceSuperInvalid2`.
- fixture correction: none; no `.cj` fixture was changed for this problem type.
- focused verification command: `.\gradlew.bat :cfir:analysis-tests:test --tests '*ClassGenerated.testInterfaceSuperInvalid1' --tests '*ClassGenerated.testInterfaceSuperInvalid2' --tests '*ClassGenerated.testClass5' --no-daemon --max-workers=1 --console=plain --stacktrace --no-build-cache --no-configuration-cache`
- focused verification outcome: `BUILD SUCCESSFUL`; both interface-super methods and the class `super` initializer guard passed in PSI and non-PSI paths.
- regression command: `.\gradlew.bat :cfir:analysis-tests:test --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt._class.ClassGenerated' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt._class.ClassGenerated' --no-daemon --max-workers=1 --console=plain --stacktrace --no-build-cache --no-configuration-cache`
- regression outcome: `464 tests completed, 34 failed`; `testInterfaceSuperInvalid1` and `testInterfaceSuperInvalid2` no longer appear in either PSI or non-PSI failure list. Remaining failures are the existing unrelated ClassGenerated clusters: `testInterfaceMemberVariableCheck02`, `testInterfaceDefaultImplementedFuncInvalid6`, `testClassStatic04`, `testClass12`, `testClassNoOverrideModifier6`, `testClassInheritance8`, `testClassMissingTypeargumentsInvalid`, `testClass1`, `testClass3`, `testInterfaceImplement4`, `testClassAccessControl`, `testSyntax`, `testClassSuperMemberFuncCallInvalid2`, `testClassAccessControl11`, `testClassAccessControl15`, `testClassAccessControl21`, and `testClassAccessControl23`.

## ClassGenerated invisible member function diagnostic mapping

- problem type: PSI and non-PSI `ClassGenerated` mapped inaccessible member functions to the generic `INVALID_ACCESS_CONTROL` diagnostic instead of `INVISIBLE_MEMBER` for `class_static04.cj` and `class_access_control11.cj`.
- root cause: `ConeVisibilityError.mapConeVisibilityError` classified every inaccessible member callable as `INVALID_ACCESS_CONTROL`, even when the invisible symbol was a function. Official Cangjie distinguishes function access (`sema_invalid_access_function`) from non-function access (`sema_invalid_access_control`), and the repository already has `INVISIBLE_MEMBER` as the function-member diagnostic surface.
- official Cangjie evidence: `cjc --diagnostic-format json --error-count-limit all cfir\analysis-tests\build\codex-probes\class_visibility_member_access_probe.cj` reported three `sema_invalid_access_function` diagnostics for `AStatic.f`, `RVisibility().a().a`, and `RVisibility().b`. `external/cangjie_compiler/src/Sema/TypeCheckAccess.cpp` `CheckFuncAccessControl` reports `sema_invalid_access_function` for inaccessible functions, while `CheckNonFuncAccessControl` reports `sema_invalid_access_control` for non-functions; `external/cangjie_compiler/src/Sema/TypeCheckReference.cpp` routes function member-access legality through `CheckFuncAccessControl`.
- Kotlin counterpart files consulted: `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/checkers/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/analysis/diagnostics/coneDiagnosticToFirDiagnostic.kt`, where `ConeVisibilityError` is converted in the diagnostic mapping layer from the invisible symbol, not by changing call resolution.
- CFIR owner files changed: `cfir/checkers/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/analysis/diagnostics/coneDiagnosticToCfirDiagnostic.kt`.
- repair principle: keep candidate visibility checking unchanged and specialize only the shared Cone-to-CFIR diagnostic mapping: invisible member functions use `INVISIBLE_MEMBER`, while constructor and non-function member visibility keep their existing `NO_CONSTRUCTOR` / `INVALID_ACCESS_CONTROL` surfaces.
- fixtures covered: PSI and non-PSI `CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt._class.ClassGenerated` / `CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt._class.ClassGenerated` methods `testClassStatic04` and `testClassAccessControl11`.
- fixture correction: none; no `.cj` fixture was changed for this problem type.
- focused verification command: `.\gradlew.bat :cfir:analysis-tests:test --tests '*ClassGenerated.testClassStatic04' --tests '*ClassGenerated.testClassAccessControl11' --no-daemon --max-workers=1 --console=plain --stacktrace --no-build-cache --no-configuration-cache`
- focused verification outcome: `BUILD SUCCESSFUL`; both PSI and non-PSI triggering methods passed.
- visibility guard command: `.\gradlew.bat :cfir:analysis-tests:test --tests '*diagnostics.visibility.VisibilityGenerated' --no-daemon --max-workers=1 --console=plain --stacktrace --no-build-cache --no-configuration-cache`
- visibility guard outcome: `BUILD FAILED` with 18 tests completed, 4 failed, 5 skipped. The failures are existing broader visibility candidate-retention gaps (`NO_MATCH_FUNCTION_DECLARATION_FOR_CALL` instead of `INVISIBLE_REFERENCE`/`INVISIBLE_MEMBER` in `invisibleReferenceAndMemberRich.cj` and `protectedAndInternalMatrixRich.cj`), not the `ConeVisibilityError` mapping branch changed here.
- regression command: `.\gradlew.bat :cfir:analysis-tests:test --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt._class.ClassGenerated' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt._class.ClassGenerated' --no-daemon --max-workers=1 --console=plain --stacktrace --no-build-cache --no-configuration-cache`
- regression outcome: `464 tests completed, 30 failed`; `testClassStatic04` and `testClassAccessControl11` no longer appear in either PSI or non-PSI failure list. Remaining failures are `testInterfaceMemberVariableCheck02`, `testInterfaceDefaultImplementedFuncInvalid6`, `testClass12`, `testClassNoOverrideModifier6`, `testClassInheritance8`, `testClassMissingTypeargumentsInvalid`, `testClass1`, `testClass3`, `testInterfaceImplement4`, `testClassAccessControl`, `testSyntax`, `testClassSuperMemberFuncCallInvalid2`, `testClassAccessControl15`, `testClassAccessControl21`, and `testClassAccessControl23`.

## ClassGenerated implicit Object super receiver for member-missing diagnostics

- problem type: PSI and non-PSI `ClassGenerated` reported `UNRESOLVED_REFERENCE` for `super.foo1()` when a class declared only interface supertypes, instead of reporting member absence from the official implicit `Object` superclass.
- root cause: `CfirExpressionsResolveTransformer` computed `super` receiver candidates only from declaration `superTypeRefs` filtered to concrete implementation types. Ordinary classes whose source declares only interfaces therefore had no receiver type, so member lookup created `ConeUnresolvedNameError` without a nominal receiver and diagnostic mapping could not produce `NOT_MEMBER_OF`.
- official Cangjie evidence: `cjc --diagnostic-format json --error-count-limit all cfir\analysis-tests\build\codex-probes\class_super_interface_member_probe.cj` reported `sema_not_member_of` with message `"'foo1' is not a member of class 'Object'"`. `external/cangjie_compiler/src/Sema/PreCheck.cpp` `TypeChecker::TypeCheckerImpl::AddSuperClassObjectForClassDecl` calls `AddObjectSuperClass` for class declarations without a superclass, and `external/cangjie_compiler/src/Sema/TypeCheckExpr/NameReferenceExpr.cpp` `DiagMemberAccessNotFound` reports `sema_not_member_of` when the member-access base type is nominal.
- official fixture-correction evidence: `cjc --diagnostic-format json --error-count-limit all cfir\analysis-tests\build\codex-probes\class_super_initializer_probe.cj` reported both `sema_not_member_of` on `a` and `sema_this_or_super_not_allowed_to_initialize_non_static_member` on `super`, proving `class5.cj` should expect `NOT_MEMBER_OF` rather than stale `UNRESOLVED_REFERENCE` after the receiver is typed as `Object`.
- Kotlin counterpart files consulted: `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/resolve/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/resolve/transformers/body/resolve/FirExpressionsResolveTransformer.kt`, where `transformSuperReceiver` types `super` from the current dispatch receiver's direct `superTypeRefs` before member resolution.
- CFIR owner files changed: `cfir/resolve/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/resolve/body/CfirExpressionsResolveTransformer.kt`; fixture correction in `cfir/analysis-tests/testData/llt/class/class5.cj`.
- repair principle: keep `super` receiver typing in the shared body resolver and add the same implicit-`Object` superclass rule already used by type-aware supertype/member-scope code, so all member lookup diagnostics see the official nominal receiver instead of local unresolved-name adaptation.
- fixtures covered: PSI and non-PSI `CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt._class.ClassGenerated` / `CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt._class.ClassGenerated` methods `testClassSuperMemberFuncCallInvalid2`, with `testClassSuperMemberFuncCallInvalid1` and `testClass5` as focused guards.
- fixture correction: changed `class5.cj` from `UNRESOLVED_REFERENCE` to `NOT_MEMBER_OF` on `super.a`, backed by official `class_super_initializer_probe.cj` output.
- focused verification command: `.\gradlew.bat :cfir:analysis-tests:test --tests '*ClassGenerated.testClassSuperMemberFuncCallInvalid2' --tests '*ClassGenerated.testClassSuperMemberFuncCallInvalid1' --tests '*ClassGenerated.testClass5' --no-daemon --max-workers=1 --console=plain --stacktrace --no-build-cache --no-configuration-cache`
- focused verification outcome: `BUILD SUCCESSFUL`; all six PSI/non-PSI methods passed.
- regression command: `.\gradlew.bat :cfir:analysis-tests:test --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt._class.ClassGenerated' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt._class.ClassGenerated' --no-daemon --max-workers=1 --console=plain --stacktrace --no-build-cache --no-configuration-cache`
- regression outcome: `464 tests completed, 28 failed`; `testClassSuperMemberFuncCallInvalid2` no longer appears in either PSI or non-PSI failure list. Remaining failures are `testInterfaceMemberVariableCheck02`, `testInterfaceDefaultImplementedFuncInvalid6`, `testClass12`, `testClassNoOverrideModifier6`, `testClassInheritance8`, `testClassMissingTypeargumentsInvalid`, `testClass1`, `testClass3`, `testInterfaceImplement4`, `testClassAccessControl`, `testSyntax`, `testClassAccessControl15`, `testClassAccessControl21`, and `testClassAccessControl23`.

## ClassGenerated inherited member compatibility diagnostics

- problem type: PSI and non-PSI `ClassGenerated` missed inherited-member compatibility diagnostics for class abstract members overriding concrete superclass members and for same-signature inherited static members that weaken visibility.
- root cause: `CfirInheritanceDeepChecker` collected direct and inherited member pairs but did not carry explicit override status into `InheritedMemberInfo`, did not report official invalid abstract class override diagnostics, and only checked weakening visibility through narrower override paths instead of the shared same-signature inheritance relation used by class members.
- official Cangjie evidence: `cjc --diagnostic-format json --error-count-limit all cfir\analysis-tests\build\codex-probes\class_inheritance_invalid_override_probe.cj` reported two `sema_invalid_override_member_in_class` diagnostics for abstract `f` and abstract property `a` overriding non-abstract superclass members. `cjc --diagnostic-format json --error-count-limit all cfir\analysis-tests\build\codex-probes\class_static_access_weaken_probe.cj` reported `sema_weak_visibility` for a private static `f<T>` hiding/overriding the inherited static `f<T>`. Implementation evidence is `external/cangjie_compiler/src/Sema/InheritanceChecker/StructInheritanceChecker.cpp` `StructInheritanceChecker::CheckInheritanceAttributes`, including `DiagCannotOverride`, `DiagWeakVisibility`, and the weak-visibility branch around same-signature inherited members.
- Kotlin counterpart files consulted: `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/checkers/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/analysis/checkers/declaration/FirOverrideChecker.kt` and `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/checkers/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/analysis/checkers/declaration/FirNotImplementedOverrideChecker.kt`, where inherited override relations are checked in declaration-level FIR checkers after member resolution rather than in fixture-specific call paths.
- CFIR owner files changed: `cfir/checkers/checkers-component-generator/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/checkers/generator/diagnostics/CfirDiagnosticsList.kt`, `cfir/checkers/gen/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/analysis/diagnostics/CfirErrors.kt`, `cfir/checkers/gen/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/analysis/diagnostics/CfirNonSuppressibleErrorNames.kt`, `cfir/checkers/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/analysis/diagnostics/CfirErrorsDefaultMessages.kt`, and `cfir/checkers/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/analysis/checkers/declaration/CfirInheritanceDeepChecker.kt`.
- repair principle: keep inherited-member compatibility in the shared deep-inheritance checker so both raw-builder and PSI paths report official relation diagnostics from the same member-pair analysis instead of adding fixture-local or declaration-shape-specific checks.
- fixtures covered: PSI and non-PSI `CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt._class.ClassGenerated` / `CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt._class.ClassGenerated` methods `testClassInheritance8` and `testClassAccessControl21`.
- fixture correction: none; no `.cj` fixture was changed for this problem type.
- focused verification command: `.\gradlew.bat :cfir:analysis-tests:test --tests '*ClassGenerated.testClassInheritance8' --tests '*ClassGenerated.testClassAccessControl21' --no-daemon --max-workers=1 --console=plain --stacktrace --no-build-cache --no-configuration-cache`
- focused verification outcome: `BUILD SUCCESSFUL`; both PSI and non-PSI triggering methods passed.
- regression command: `.\gradlew.bat :cfir:analysis-tests:test --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt._class.ClassGenerated' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt._class.ClassGenerated' --no-daemon --max-workers=1 --console=plain --stacktrace --no-build-cache --no-configuration-cache`
- regression outcome: `464 tests completed, 24 failed`; `testClassInheritance8` and `testClassAccessControl21` no longer appear in either PSI or non-PSI failure list. Remaining failures are `testInterfaceMemberVariableCheck02`, `testInterfaceDefaultImplementedFuncInvalid6`, `testClass12`, `testClassNoOverrideModifier6`, `testClassMissingTypeargumentsInvalid`, `testClass1`, `testClass3`, `testInterfaceImplement4`, `testClassAccessControl`, `testSyntax`, `testClassAccessControl15`, and `testClassAccessControl23`.

## ClassGenerated member initializer self-name resolution

- problem type: PSI and non-PSI `ClassGenerated` treated a bare reference to the field currently being initialized as an initialized-member read diagnostic, and `class3.cj` had a stale missing marker for an actually undeclared initializer name.
- root cause: field initializers were resolved without a dedicated "current field initializer" context. A bare name such as `a` in `public var a = a` could therefore resolve through the class implicit receiver back to the field being declared, after which `CfirInitializationCheckers` reported `USED_BEFORE_INITIALIZATION`. Official Cangjie distinguishes this from explicit `this.a`: bare self-name lookup is unresolved, while explicit member access remains an initialization-flow diagnostic.
- official Cangjie evidence: `cjc --diagnostic-format json --error-count-limit all cfir\analysis-tests\build\codex-probes\class_member_initializer_self_reference_probe.cj` reported `sema_undeclared_identifier` for bare `a` in `public var a: Int32 = a`. `class_member_initializer_this_self_reference_probe.cj` reported `sema_used_before_initialization` for `this.a`, while `class_member_initializer_prior_field_probe.cj` and `class_member_initializer_this_field_probe.cj` were accepted for reads of a prior initialized field. `class_member_initializer_unresolved_probe.cj` reported `sema_undeclared_identifier` for `temp` and `sema_illegal_usage_of_member` for `getNum1()`. Implementation evidence is `external/cangjie_compiler/src/Sema/TypeCheckReference.cpp`, where unresolved references report `sema_undeclared_identifier`, and `external/cangjie_compiler/src/Sema/LegalityOfUsage/InitializationChecker.cpp` `CheckIllegalMemberAccess`, which owns explicit member initialization-flow diagnostics.
- Kotlin counterpart files consulted: `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/checkers/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/analysis/checkers/declaration/FirMemberPropertiesChecker.kt` and `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/checkers/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/analysis/collectors/components/ControlFlowAnalysisDiagnosticComponent.kt`, where property initialization and control-flow diagnostics are kept in initialization/CFA owners after resolve has already determined the referenced symbol.
- CFIR owner files changed: `cfir/resolve/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/resolve/transformers/body/resolve/BodyResolveContext.kt`, `cfir/resolve/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/resolve/body/CfirDeclarationsResolveTransformer.kt`, `cfir/resolve/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/resolve/body/CfirExpressionsResolveTransformer.kt`, and fixture correction in `cfir/analysis-tests/testData/llt/class/class3.cj`.
- repair principle: track the current field initializer in body resolve and rewrite only no-explicit-receiver self-field references to `ConeUnresolvedNameError`, leaving explicit member reads to the shared initialization checker so both official diagnostic families keep their proper owner.
- fixtures covered: PSI and non-PSI `CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt._class.ClassGenerated` / `CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt._class.ClassGenerated` methods `testClass12` and `testClass3`.
- fixture correction: added the missing `UNRESOLVED_REFERENCE` marker on `temp` in `class3.cj`, backed by official `sema_undeclared_identifier` output.
- focused verification command: `.\gradlew.bat :cfir:analysis-tests:test --tests '*ClassGenerated.testClass12' --tests '*ClassGenerated.testClass3' --no-daemon --max-workers=1 --console=plain --stacktrace --no-build-cache --no-configuration-cache`
- focused verification outcome: `BUILD SUCCESSFUL`; both PSI and non-PSI triggering methods passed.
- regression command: `.\gradlew.bat :cfir:analysis-tests:test --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt._class.ClassGenerated' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt._class.ClassGenerated' --no-daemon --max-workers=1 --console=plain --stacktrace --no-build-cache --no-configuration-cache`
- regression outcome: `464 tests completed, 20 failed`; `testClass12` and `testClass3` no longer appear in either PSI or non-PSI failure list. Remaining failures are `testInterfaceMemberVariableCheck02`, `testInterfaceDefaultImplementedFuncInvalid6`, `testClassNoOverrideModifier6`, `testClassMissingTypeargumentsInvalid`, `testClass1`, `testInterfaceImplement4`, `testClassAccessControl`, `testSyntax`, `testClassAccessControl15`, and `testClassAccessControl23`.

## ClassGenerated class supertype list diagnostics

- problem type: PSI and non-PSI `ClassGenerated` used stale or missing diagnostics for concrete supertype-list rules: non-open/final superclasses, multiple concrete superclasses, superclass placement after interfaces, and inheritance-cycle supertype recovery.
- root cause: `CfirSupertypesChecker` still modeled concrete supertype failures through the old Kotlin-style `CLASS_NOT_OPEN_FOR_INHERITANCE` / `MULTIPLE_CLASS_SUPER_TYPES` split and lost the original type-reference source/declaration when supertype resolution rewrote a cyclic supertype to an error type. That prevented `C <: D` from reporting the official non-inheritable diagnostic on `D`, and it made diagnostics bind to the whole class source on the PSI path.
- official Cangjie evidence: `cjc --diagnostic-format json --error-count-limit all cfir\analysis-tests\build\codex-probes\class_inheritance_nonopen_cycle_probe.cj` reported `sema_inheritance_cycle` plus `sema_non_inheritable_super_class` on `A`, `D`, and `C`. `cjc --diagnostic-format json --error-count-limit all cfir\analysis-tests\build\codex-probes\class_syntax_supertype_order_probe.cj` reported `sema_illegal_multi_inheritance` for the second concrete superclass and `sema_superclass_must_be_placed_at_first` for a concrete superclass after an interface. Implementation evidence is `external/cangjie_compiler/src/Sema/TypeCheckClassLike.cpp` `TypeChecker::TypeCheckerImpl::CheckAndAddSubDecls`, which tracks `hasSuperClass` and `superClassLikeNum`, reports `sema_illegal_multi_inheritance`, `sema_superclass_must_be_placed_at_first`, and `sema_non_inheritable_super_class`, then records the concrete superclass.
- Kotlin counterpart files consulted: `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/checkers/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/analysis/checkers/declaration/FirSupertypesChecker.kt`, where direct supertype-list legality is kept in one declaration checker after supertype resolution.
- CFIR owner files changed: `cfir/checkers/checkers-component-generator/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/checkers/generator/diagnostics/CfirDiagnosticsList.kt`, `cfir/checkers/gen/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/analysis/diagnostics/CfirErrors.kt`, `cfir/checkers/gen/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/analysis/diagnostics/CfirNonSuppressibleErrorNames.kt`, `cfir/checkers/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/analysis/diagnostics/CfirErrorsDefaultMessages.kt`, `cfir/checkers/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/analysis/checkers/declaration/CfirSupertypesChecker.kt`, and `cfir/analysis-tests/testFixtures/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/analysis/tests/golden/DiagnosticNameMapper.kt`.
- repair principle: implement the official supertype-list state machine in the shared class-like supertype checker and preserve the original delegated typeRef source behind resolve-stage error supertypes, so both raw-builder and PSI paths report the same official diagnostics on the written supertype token.
- fixtures covered: PSI and non-PSI `CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt._class.ClassGenerated` / `CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt._class.ClassGenerated` methods `testClass1`, `testSyntax`, and `testClassPrivateInit`; PSI and non-PSI `OpenModifierGenerated.testClassOpenModifier3`; diagnostics inheritance coverage methods `testClassNotOpenForInheritance`, `testClassAndInterfaceInheritanceBoundaries`, and `testClassNotOpenForInheritanceRich` across the generated diagnostics suites matched by the focused filters.
- fixture correction: replaced stale `CLASS_NOT_OPEN_FOR_INHERITANCE` markers with `NON_INHERITABLE_SUPER_CLASS`; updated `syntax.cj` to `ILLEGAL_MULTI_INHERITANCE` and `SUPERCLASS_MUST_BE_PLACED_AT_FIRST`; widened `class1.cj` inheritance-cycle range from the first character `c` to the full `class C` declaration header according to the Diagnostic Range Policy.
- focused verification command: `.\gradlew.bat :cfir:analysis-tests:test --tests '*ClassGenerated.testClass1' --tests '*ClassGenerated.testSyntax' --tests '*ClassGenerated.testClassPrivateInit' --tests '*OpenModifierGenerated.testClassOpenModifier3' --tests '*InheritanceGenerated.testClassNotOpenForInheritance' --tests '*InheritanceGenerated.testClassAndInterfaceInheritanceBoundaries' --tests '*InheritanceGenerated.testClassNotOpenForInheritanceRich' --no-daemon --max-workers=1 --console=plain --stacktrace --no-build-cache --no-configuration-cache`
- focused verification outcome: `BUILD SUCCESSFUL`; all targeted PSI/non-PSI LLT and diagnostics inheritance coverage filters passed.
- regression command: `.\gradlew.bat :cfir:analysis-tests:test --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt._class.ClassGenerated' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt._class.ClassGenerated' --no-daemon --max-workers=1 --console=plain --stacktrace --no-build-cache --no-configuration-cache`
- regression outcome: `464 tests completed, 16 failed`; `testClass1`, `testSyntax`, and `testClassPrivateInit` no longer appear in either PSI or non-PSI failure list. Remaining failures are `testInterfaceMemberVariableCheck02`, `testInterfaceDefaultImplementedFuncInvalid6`, `testClassNoOverrideModifier6`, `testClassMissingTypeargumentsInvalid`, `testInterfaceImplement4`, `testClassAccessControl`, `testClassAccessControl15`, and `testClassAccessControl23`.

## ClassGenerated bare generic static qualifier member-reference diagnostics

- problem type: PSI and non-PSI `ClassGenerated` reported an extra `UNABLE_TO_INFER_GENERIC_FUNC` around static member references whose receiver qualifier was a bare generic class-like type, while official Cangjie reports only the missing qualifier type arguments for member references and reserves function-inference failure for actual calls.
- root cause: CFIR treated non-call static member references and actual function calls through the same bare generic static qualifier inference path. The resolver and constraint-system diagnostic mapper therefore surfaced `ConeUnableToInferGenericFuncError` / `UNABLE_TO_INFER_GENERIC_FUNC` for `R.funca` even though the only official error on a member reference is that `R<T>` was written without type arguments.
- official Cangjie evidence: `cjc --diagnostic-format json --error-count-limit all cfir\analysis-tests\build\codex-probes\class_missing_typearguments_invalid_probe.cj` reported exactly two `sema_generic_type_without_type_argument` diagnostics for `let a = R.funca` and `var b = R.funca`, and no `sema_unable_to_infer_generic_func`. Probe `class_missing_typearguments_static_call_probe.cj` with `let a = R.funca(1)` reported `sema_unable_to_infer_generic_func` on the actual call. Implementation evidence is `external/cangjie_compiler/src/Sema/TypeCheckReference.cpp`, where member access on a generic base type without type arguments reports `sema_generic_type_without_type_argument`, and `external/cangjie_compiler/src/Sema/TypeArgumentInference.cpp` `DiagnoseForCallInference`, where call inference failure reports `sema_unable_to_infer_generic_func`.
- Kotlin counterpart files consulted: `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/checkers/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/analysis/checkers/expression/FirPropertyAccessTypeArgumentsChecker.kt`, where property/callee-reference diagnostics are kept distinct from call-type-argument diagnostics.
- CFIR owner files changed: `cfir/resolve/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/resolve/body/CfirCallResolver.kt` and `cfir/checkers/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/analysis/diagnostics/coneDiagnosticToCfirDiagnostic.kt`.
- repair principle: gate the bare static generic qualifier inference diagnostic to real `CfirFunctionCall` sites and suppress non-call constraint-system inference errors only when the uninferred type variable belongs to the explicitly written bare class-like static qualifier.
- fixtures covered: PSI and non-PSI `CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt._class.ClassGenerated.testClassMissingTypeargumentsInvalid` / `CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt._class.ClassGenerated.testClassMissingTypeargumentsInvalid`, plus `RecordGenerated.testRecordMissingTypeargumentsInvalid` and `GenericAccessGenerated.testGenericTypeWithoutTypeArgument*` as focused guards.
- fixture correction: none; no `.cj` fixture was changed for this problem type.
- focused verification command: `.\gradlew.bat :cfir:analysis-tests:test --tests '*ClassGenerated.testClassMissingTypeargumentsInvalid' --tests '*RecordGenerated.testRecordMissingTypeargumentsInvalid' --tests '*GenericAccessGenerated.testGenericTypeWithoutTypeArgument*' --no-daemon --max-workers=1 --console=plain --stacktrace --no-build-cache --no-configuration-cache`
- focused verification outcome: `BUILD SUCCESSFUL`; the Class/Record missing-typeargument fixtures and generic-access guard filters passed.
- regression command: `.\gradlew.bat :cfir:analysis-tests:test --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt._class.ClassGenerated' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt._class.ClassGenerated' --no-daemon --max-workers=1 --console=plain --stacktrace --no-build-cache --no-configuration-cache`
- regression outcome: `464 tests completed, 14 failed`; `testClassMissingTypeargumentsInvalid` no longer appears in either PSI or non-PSI failure list. Remaining failures are `testInterfaceMemberVariableCheck02`, `testInterfaceDefaultImplementedFuncInvalid6`, `testClassNoOverrideModifier6`, `testInterfaceImplement4`, `testClassAccessControl`, `testClassAccessControl15`, and `testClassAccessControl23`.

## ClassGenerated interface field recovery initialization diagnostics

- problem type: PSI and non-PSI `ClassGenerated` reported `CLASS_UNINITIALIZED_FIELD` for a non-static `let` variable recovered inside an interface body, while the fixture expects no CFIR initialization diagnostic.
- root cause: `CfirInitializationCheckers` collected all non-static `CfirFieldVariable` declarations from every `CfirClassLikeDeclaration`, so an interface-body parse recovery node was treated as an instance field that must be initialized by a default constructor.
- official Cangjie evidence: `cjc --diagnostic-format json --error-count-limit all cfir/analysis-tests/testData/llt/class/interface_member_variable_check_02.cj` reported `parse_unexpected_declaration_in_scope` with message `unexpected variable declaration in interface body` on the `let` keyword, and did not report `sema_class_uninitialized_field`. Implementation evidence is `external/cangjie_compiler/src/Parse/ParserDiag.cpp`, where `ScopeKind::INTERFACE_BODY` is classified as `interface body`, and `external/cangjie_compiler/include/cangjie/Basic/DiagRefactor/DiagnosticParser.def`, which defines `parse_unexpected_declaration_in_scope`.
- Kotlin counterpart files consulted: `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/checkers/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/analysis/checkers/declaration/FirMemberPropertiesChecker.kt` and `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/checkers/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/analysis/checkers/declaration/FirPropertyInitializationChecker.kt`, where property initialization is class-member initialization checking rather than a fallback for syntactically illegal interface declarations.
- CFIR owner files changed: `cfir/checkers/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/analysis/checkers/declaration/CfirInitializationCheckers.kt`.
- repair principle: keep definite-assignment initialization in its shared checker but model official interface storage semantics there: interface `let`/`var` recovery nodes are not instance fields and must not enter class/constructor initialization tracking, while static member initialization remains available.
- fixtures covered: PSI and non-PSI `CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt._class.ClassGenerated.testInterfaceMemberVariableCheck02` / `CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt._class.ClassGenerated.testInterfaceMemberVariableCheck02`, with `testInterfaceMemberVariableCheck04` and `testClassInitDefaultConstructor2` as focused guards.
- fixture correction: none; no `.cj` fixture was changed for this problem type.
- focused verification command: `.\gradlew.bat :cfir:analysis-tests:test --tests '*ClassGenerated.testInterfaceMemberVariableCheck02' --tests '*ClassGenerated.testInterfaceMemberVariableCheck04' --tests '*ClassGenerated.testClassInitDefaultConstructor2' --no-daemon --max-workers=1 --console=plain --stacktrace --no-build-cache --no-configuration-cache`
- focused verification outcome: `BUILD SUCCESSFUL`; both PSI and non-PSI triggering methods plus guards passed.
- regression command: `.\gradlew.bat :cfir:analysis-tests:test --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt._class.ClassGenerated' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt._class.ClassGenerated' --no-daemon --max-workers=1 --console=plain --stacktrace --no-build-cache --no-configuration-cache`
- regression outcome: `464 tests completed, 12 failed`; `testInterfaceMemberVariableCheck02` no longer appears in either PSI or non-PSI failure list. Remaining failures are `testInterfaceDefaultImplementedFuncInvalid6`, `testClassNoOverrideModifier6`, `testInterfaceImplement4`, `testClassAccessControl`, `testClassAccessControl15`, and `testClassAccessControl23`.

## ClassGenerated generic interface default instantiation ambiguity

- problem type: PSI and non-PSI `ClassGenerated` missed ambiguous inherited interface default members after generic instantiation, and previously mixed declaration-site duplicate-superinterface recovery with use-site member ambiguity.
- root cause: `CfirGenericInstantiationChecker` stopped member signature checking when substitutions still mentioned declaration type parameters and keyed inherited interface functions only by the substituted callable shape. That collapsed distinct generic interface instantiations before use-site substitution and lost the official ambiguity for `B<X, X>`, bare `B(...)`, and calls through `test`.
- official Cangjie evidence: `cjc --diagnostic-format json --error-count-limit all cfir\analysis-tests\testData\llt\class\interface_default_implemented_func_invalid_6.cj` reported four `sema_generic_instantiation_causes_ambiguous_functions` diagnostics for `B<X, X>(...)`, bare `B(...)`, `test(x)`, and `test<Int64>(1)`, and did not report a declaration-site duplicate supertype for `class B<X, Y> <: A<X, Y>`. Implementation evidence is `external/cangjie_compiler/src/Sema/InheritanceChecker/InstantiatedChecker.cpp` (`CheckInstMemberSignatures`, `CheckInstantiatedRef`, `DiagnoseForInstantiatedMember`) and `external/cangjie_compiler/src/Sema/TypeChecker.cpp` (`GetDupSuperInterface`, `CheckInstDupSuperInterfaces`).
- Kotlin counterpart files consulted: `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/checkers/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/analysis/checkers/declaration/FirNonExpansiveInheritanceRestrictionChecker.kt` for inherited generic-supertype checker placement.
- CFIR owner files changed: `cfir/checkers/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/analysis/checkers/declaration/CfirGenericInstantiationChecker.kt`, with supporting duplicate-superinterface identity rendering already present in `cfir/checkers/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/analysis/checkers/declaration/CfirDuplicateSuperInterfaceUtils.kt`.
- repair principle: preserve inherited interface identity until after use-site substitution and run member signature ambiguity checks only from call/constructor instantiation triggers, so declaration generic parameters do not emit use-site ambiguity while real instantiations share the same checker owner.
- fixtures covered: PSI and non-PSI `CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt._class.ClassGenerated.testInterfaceDefaultImplementedFuncInvalid6` / `CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt._class.ClassGenerated.testInterfaceDefaultImplementedFuncInvalid6`, with `testInterfaceDefaultImplementedFuncInvalid5`, `testInterfaceDefaultImplementedFuncGenericOk2`, and `testInterfaceDefaultImplementedFuncGenericOk3` as focused guards.
- fixture correction: none; no `.cj` fixture was changed for this problem type.
- focused verification command: `.\gradlew.bat :cfir:analysis-tests:test --tests '*ClassGenerated.testInterfaceDefaultImplementedFuncInvalid6' --tests '*ClassGenerated.testInterfaceDefaultImplementedFuncInvalid5' --tests '*ClassGenerated.testInterfaceDefaultImplementedFuncGenericOk2' --tests '*ClassGenerated.testInterfaceDefaultImplementedFuncGenericOk3' --no-daemon --max-workers=1 --console=plain --stacktrace --no-build-cache --no-configuration-cache`
- focused verification outcome: `BUILD SUCCESSFUL`; all targeted PSI and non-PSI generic-instantiation ambiguity and guard methods passed.
- regression command: `.\gradlew.bat :cfir:analysis-tests:test --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt._class.ClassGenerated' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt._class.ClassGenerated' --no-daemon --max-workers=1 --console=plain --stacktrace --no-build-cache --no-configuration-cache`
- regression outcome: `BUILD SUCCESSFUL`; XML confirms non-PSI `232` tests and PSI `232` tests with `0` failures, `0` errors, and `0` skipped.

## TypePatternGenerated match parsing and non-exhaustive selector range

- problem type: PSI and non-PSI `TypePatternGenerated` had shared type-pattern parsing/body-leakage problems plus stale `NON_EXHAUSTIVE_MATCH` ranges after the checker was aligned to report on the selector expression.
- root cause: selector-less/nested `match` parsing could inherit expression-condition context into nested subject matches, PSI helpers used recursive child lookup for match entries and block statements, and type-pattern binding extraction treated type references such as `Float64` as binding identifiers. After the checker correctly reported non-exhaustiveness on the selector, several fixtures still expected the old whole-`match` range. `non_exhaustive_parent.cj` also mixed the TypePattern case with independent accessibility and invalid `Some(0)` call diagnostics.
- official Cangjie evidence: `cjc --diagnostic-format json --error-count-limit all --output-dir tmp\cjc-out tmp\probe_non_exhaustive_parent.cj` reported `sema_nonexhuastive_patterns` on selector `x` and `sema_unreachable_pattern` on `Some(_: B)`. `tmp\probe_non_exhaustive_parent_call_only.cj` proved the original `func f(x: Option<I>)` plus `f(Some(0))` shape independently triggers `sema_accessibility` and `sema_unable_to_infer_generic_func`. `tmp\probe_non_exhaustive_parent_focused.cj`, with `private func f` and `f(Some(A()))`, preserved only the intended non-exhaustive/unreachable pattern behavior. Project range evidence is `cfir/analysis-tests/testData/diagnostics2/README.md`, which locks `NON_EXHAUSTIVE_MATCH` to the selector expression.
- Kotlin counterpart files consulted: Kotlin PSI/block direct-statement behavior was used as the structural model for direct child traversal (`KtBlockExpression.getStatements`), and Kotlin FIR diagnostic source-selection design was used only as architecture guidance for checker-owned source selection.
- CFIR owner files changed: `psi/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/parsing/CangJieExpressionParsing.kt`, `psi/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/psi/casePattern.kt`, `psi/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/psi/CjMatchEntry.kt`, `psi/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/psi/CjBlockExpression.kt`, `cfir/raw-cfir/light-tree2cfir/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/lightTree/LightTreeRawCfirExpressionBuilder.kt`, `cfir/checkers/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/analysis/checkers/expression/CfirMatchExhaustivenessChecker.kt`, and fixture corrections under `cfir/analysis-tests/testData/llt/PatternMatching/TypePattern`.
- repair principle: fix the shared parsing/PSI/light-tree/checker owner paths first, then correct only stale fixture ranges or fixture source noise proven by official `cjc`, so the TypePattern suite tests match exhaustiveness rather than unrelated call/accessibility cascades.
- fixtures covered: PSI and non-PSI `CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt.PatternMatching.TypePattern.TypePatternGenerated` / `CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt.PatternMatching.TypePattern.TypePatternGenerated`; focused failures included `type09.cj`, `non_exhaustive_public_00.cj`, `non_exhaustive_public_01.cj`, `non_exhaustive_generic.cj`, `exhaustive_sealed.cj`, `exhaustive_non_public_00.cj`, `exhaustive_non_public_01.cj`, and `non_exhaustive_parent.cj`.
- fixture correction: moved stale `NON_EXHAUSTIVE_MATCH` markers from whole `match` expressions to selector `x`; changed `non_exhaustive_parent.cj` to `private func f` and `f(Some(A()))` to remove independent official accessibility/inference errors while preserving the intended TypePattern non-exhaustiveness case.
- focused verification commands: `.\gradlew.bat :cfir:analysis-tests:test --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt.PatternMatching.TypePattern.TypePatternGenerated.testType09' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt.PatternMatching.TypePattern.TypePatternGenerated.testType09' --no-daemon --max-workers=1 --console=plain --stacktrace --no-build-cache --no-configuration-cache`; `.\gradlew.bat :cfir:analysis-tests:test --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt.PatternMatching.TypePattern.TypePatternGenerated.testNonExhaustiveParent' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt.PatternMatching.TypePattern.TypePatternGenerated.testNonExhaustiveParent' --no-daemon --max-workers=1 --console=plain --stacktrace --no-build-cache --no-configuration-cache`.

- official Cangjie evidence: not required; this is a range-only correction governed by the Diagnostic Range Policy in `cangjie-cfir-llt-repair`, which states that IDE diagnostics use full relevant token/source ranges rather than cjc's narrow anchors.

## ClassGenerated error-typed callable value invocation cascade

- problem type: PSI and non-PSI `ClassGenerated.testClassMissingTypeargumentsInvalid` reintroduced `UNRESOLVED_REFERENCE` on calls to `a(1)` and `b(1)` after `a` and `b` had already been given error function-value types from bare generic static member references.
- root cause: `CfirCallResolver.createResolvedNamedReference()` classified a successful named-value candidate used in a call position as generic unresolved when the value declaration's type was already `ConeErrorType`. That dropped the original bare-generic qualifier diagnostic and produced a second, misleading unresolved callee diagnostic at the later call site.
- official Cangjie evidence: `cjc --diagnostic-format json --error-count-limit all cfir\analysis-tests\build\codex-probes\class_missing_typearguments_invalid_probe.cj` reported only two `sema_generic_type_without_type_argument` diagnostics for `let a = R.funca` and `var b = R.funca`; there were no later unresolved diagnostics for `a(1)` or `b(1)`. Implementation evidence remains `external/cangjie_compiler/src/Sema/TypeCheckReference.cpp` for the bare generic member-access diagnostic and `external/cangjie_compiler/src/Sema/TypeArgumentInference.cpp` `DiagnoseForCallInference` for actual call inference diagnostics.
- Kotlin counterpart files consulted: `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/checkers/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/analysis/checkers/expression/FirPropertyAccessTypeArgumentsChecker.kt`, plus FIR call-resolution architecture where error-typed callees are propagated as error results instead of remapped to unrelated unresolved names.
- CFIR owner files changed: `cfir/resolve/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/resolve/body/CfirCallResolver.kt` and `cfir/resolve/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/resolve/body/CfirExpressionsResolveTransformer.kt`.
- repair principle: when a call expression resolves its callee as a value whose declaration type is already a `ConeErrorType`, propagate that error through `ConeUnreportedDuplicateDiagnostic` instead of inventing a new unresolved-name diagnostic; this preserves the original semantic error owner and suppresses cascades for all equivalent callable-value recovery paths.
- fixtures covered: PSI and non-PSI `CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt._class.ClassGenerated.testClassMissingTypeargumentsInvalid` / `CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt._class.ClassGenerated.testClassMissingTypeargumentsInvalid`.
- fixture correction: none; no `.cj` fixture was changed for this problem type.
- focused verification command: `.\gradlew.bat :cfir:analysis-tests:test --tests '*ClassGenerated.testClass15' --tests '*ClassGenerated.testClassMissingTypeargumentsInvalid' --no-daemon --max-workers=1 --console=plain --stacktrace --no-build-cache --no-configuration-cache`
- focused verification outcome: `BUILD SUCCESSFUL`; both PSI and non-PSI `testClassMissingTypeargumentsInvalid` passed.
- regression command: `.\gradlew.bat :cfir:analysis-tests:test --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt._class.ClassGenerated' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt._class.ClassGenerated' --no-daemon --max-workers=1 --console=plain --stacktrace --no-build-cache --no-configuration-cache`
- regression outcome: `BUILD SUCCESSFUL`; XML confirms non-PSI `232` tests and PSI `232` tests with `0` failures, `0` errors, and `0` skipped.
## Function recursive-constructor dependency graph ordering and cycle-head selection

- problem type: recursive constructor diagnostics in `llt/function/recursive_ctor_00.cj` through `recursive_ctor_08.cj` must select the same dependency-cycle head as the official compiler and use the repository full-token range policy on constructor references.
- root cause: `CfirRecursiveConstructorCallChecker` treated a call whose target constructor was already visiting as a call-node cycle instead of re-entering the constructor node, resolved constructor owners only through the symbol provider during a phase where forward class-like declarations can still require the CFIR provider, and traversed raw CFIR declaration storage order where synthesized/primary constructors can precede source fields and secondary constructors. These divergences produced duplicate cross-type reports and selected call sites or primary constructors instead of the official cycle head.
- official Cangjie evidence: `C:\Users\lin17\.cangjie\sdks\cangjie-1.0.5\bin\cjc.exe --diagnostic-format json --error-count-limit all tmp\codex-function-recursive-constructor-probe.cj` reported exactly nine `sema_recursive_constructor_call` diagnostics: constructor references for direct/self field cycles, `init` declarations for constructor-delegation cycles, the implicit owner type for the superclass/field cycle, and the later dependency owner for cross-type cycles. `external/cangjie_compiler/src/Sema/Utils.cpp` `CheckRecursiveCtorFoundCycle`, `DiagRecursiveConstructorCall`, and `CheckRecursiveConstructorCall` show the official graph vertices (`instance constructor` and `init reference`), constructor-reference-to-target edges, shared DFS path, and declaration-identifier versus reference-node range selection.
- Kotlin counterpart files consulted: `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/checkers/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/analysis/checkers/declaration/FirCommonConstructorDelegationIssuesChecker.kt`, where constructor cycles are discovered through resolved delegated-constructor symbols and reported from the resolved delegated constructor call source.
- CFIR owner files changed: `cfir/checkers/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/analysis/checkers/declaration/CfirRecursiveConstructorCallChecker.kt`; range-only fixture corrections in `cfir/analysis-tests/testData/llt/function/recursive_ctor_00.cj`, `recursive_ctor_03.cj`, and `recursive_ctor_05.cj`.
- repair principle: preserve the official constructor/reference dependency graph and semantic root order (`field initializers -> secondary constructors -> primary constructors`) in the shared checker so every PSI and light-tree fixture receives one deterministic cycle head without fixture-specific branching.
- fixtures covered: `CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt.function.FunctionGenerated` and `CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt.function.FunctionGenerated` methods `testRecursiveCtor00` through `testRecursiveCtor08`.
- fixture correction: the three reference-node diagnostics now cover the full constructor callee identifier rather than including parentheses, matching both official `MakeRangeForRecursiveConstructorCall` behavior and the repository full-token diagnostic range policy.
- verification command: `.\gradlew.bat :cfir:analysis-tests:test --tests '*FunctionGenerated.testRecursiveCtor*' --no-daemon --max-workers=1 --console=plain --stacktrace --no-build-cache --no-configuration-cache`.
- verification outcome: `BUILD SUCCESSFUL`; fresh XML at `2026-07-11 07:02:57` contains `9 tests, 0 failures, 0 errors` for both `CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt.function.FunctionGenerated` and `CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt.function.FunctionGenerated`.
## Function struct mutability propagation and nested-instance capture

- problem type: immutable struct/extend members must propagate mutations through struct-valued receiver chains to the owning field, constructors and mut functions must use dedicated nested-instance capture diagnostics, and reference-type/Array chains must stop struct mutation propagation.
- root cause: `CfirImmutableFunctionCannotModifyFieldChecker` only recognized direct implicit/`this` field assignments, `CfirImmutableFunctionCannotAccessMutableFunctionChecker` only handled direct current-receiver calls, extend targets were not mapped back to their struct declaration, and the closest nested function was mistaken for the outer member function. The capture diagnostics `ILLEGAL_CAPTURE_THIS` and `CAPTURE_THIS_OR_INSTANCE_FIELD_IN_FUNC` were also absent from the CFIR diagnostic surface. Separately, `mut_function_09.cj` retained a stale property-write expectation and omitted the official immutable receiver mut-call diagnostic.
- official Cangjie evidence: `cjc --diagnostic-format json --error-count-limit all tmp\codex-function-mutability-probe.cj` reported `sema_cannot_modify_var` for nested struct field assignments, extend assignments, field-initializer lambda assignment, and mut calls through a struct field; it reported `sema_illegal_capture_this` in a struct constructor's nested function and `sema_capture_this_or_instance_field_in_func` in a mut function's nested function, while producing no declaration-body error for the Array/class reference case or the mut-property temporary-value case. Exact probes `tmp\codex-function-mutability-08-exact.cj` and `tmp\codex-function-mutability-09-exact.cj` confirmed no diagnostic for `x[0] += 1` / `c.c += 1`, no property-body assignment diagnostic for `t1.t0 += 1`, and one `sema_immutable_access_mutable_func` for `a[0].t2.f()`. Official implementation was traced through `external/cangjie_compiler/src/Sema/TypeCheckAccess.cpp` `CheckMutationInStructNonMut`, `CheckMutationInStruct`, and `CheckLetInstanceAccessMutableFunc`, plus `external/cangjie_compiler/src/Sema/TypeCheckExpr.cpp` / `TypeCheckReference.cpp` nested capture and immutable-member access checks.
- Kotlin counterpart files consulted: `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/checkers/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/analysis/checkers/expression/FirReassignmentAndInvisibleSetterChecker.kt`, `FirAssignmentTypeMismatchChecker.kt`, and `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/checkers/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/analysis/cfa/VariableInitializationCheckProcessor.kt` for the checker split between assignment legality/type analysis and capture/initialization flow; Cangjie-specific mut semantics remain derived only from official Cangjie evidence.
- CFIR owner files changed: `cfir/checkers/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/analysis/checkers/expression/CfirMutabilityCheckers.kt`, `cfir/checkers/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/analysis/checkers/CommonExpressionCheckers.kt`, the CFIR diagnostic generator/generated/default-message files, and `cfir/analysis-tests/testFixtures/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/analysis/tests/golden/DiagnosticNameMapper.kt`; fixture correction in `cfir/analysis-tests/testData/llt/function/mut_function_09.cj`.
- repair principle: centralize owner-aware struct value-chain propagation and outer-member context selection in the shared mutability checker so assignments, mut calls, extend members, and nested capture use the same official receiver boundary rules without fixture-specific branches.
- fixtures covered: PSI and non-PSI `FunctionGenerated.testMutFunction00` through `testMutFunction07`, and `testMutFunction09` through `testMutFunction12`; `testMutFunction08` is excluded because official evidence classifies its remaining failure as a separate compound-subscript type-resolution problem.
- fixture correction: `mut_function_09.cj` no longer expects `CANNOT_ASSIGN_TO_IMMUTABLE` for `t1.t0 += 1` inside the mut function and now expects `IMMUTABLE_FUNCTION_CANNOT_ACCESS_MUTABLE_FUNCTION` on the first immutable receiver call `a[0].t2.f()`.
- verification command: `.\gradlew.bat :cfir:analysis-tests:test --tests '*FunctionGenerated.testMutFunction*' --no-daemon --max-workers=1 --console=plain --stacktrace --no-build-cache --no-configuration-cache`.
- verification outcome: fresh XML at `2026-07-11 07:21:29` contains `13 tests, 1 failure, 0 errors` for each PSI and non-PSI suite; the sole failure in both is the separately classified `testMutFunction08`, so all twelve mutability/capture guard methods pass on both entry paths.
## Indexed compound-assignment get/set type preservation

- problem type: ordinary Array/user-operator indexed compound assignments must retain the indexed `get` element type for operator checking while independently validating the `set` writeback call.
- root cause: `CfirExpressionsResolveTransformer.transformAssignment` resolved the shared subscript node as the operator call receiver and correctly obtained its element type, then `resolveSubscriptSetAssignment` overwrote that same node with the `set` call's `Unit` return type. `CfirCompoundAssignmentSemanticsChecker` consequently interpreted `x[0] += 1` as `Unit += Int64` and emitted `TYPE_INCOMPATIBLE`.
- official Cangjie evidence: `cjc --diagnostic-format json --error-count-limit all tmp\codex-function-mutability-08-exact.cj` completed with no diagnostics for `x[0] += 1` or `c.c += 1`. Official compound/subscript assignment handling was traced through `external/cangjie_compiler/src/Sema/TypeCheckExpr/AssignExpr.cpp` `InferAssignExprCheckCaseOverloading` / `SynAssignExpr` and `external/cangjie_compiler/src/Sema/Desugar/DesugarInTypeCheck.cpp` subscript overload desugaring, which preserve the indexed value type while resolving writeback.
- Kotlin counterpart files consulted: `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/raw-fir/raw-fir.common/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/builder/AbstractRawFirBuilder.kt` `generateIndexedAccessAugmentedAssignment` and `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/resolve/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/resolve/transformers/body/resolve/FirExpressionsResolveTransformer.kt` `transformIndexedAccessAugmentedAssignment`, where `lhsGetCall`, operator call, and `setCall` remain distinct stages.
- CFIR owner files changed: `cfir/resolve/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/resolve/body/CfirExpressionsResolveTransformer.kt`.
- repair principle: preserve the resolved indexed element type as the compound-assignment left type and use the `set` result only as writeback success/error evidence, matching the shared get/operator/set architecture rather than suppressing one diagnostic.
- fixtures covered: PSI and non-PSI `FunctionGenerated.testMutFunction08`, with all `testMutFunction00` through `testMutFunction12` rerun as guards.
- verification commands: `.\gradlew.bat :cfir:analysis-tests:test --tests '*FunctionGenerated.testMutFunction08' --no-daemon --max-workers=1 --console=plain --stacktrace --no-build-cache --no-configuration-cache`; `.\gradlew.bat :cfir:analysis-tests:test --tests '*FunctionGenerated.testMutFunction*' --no-daemon --max-workers=1 --console=plain --stacktrace --no-build-cache --no-configuration-cache`.
- verification outcome: both commands were `BUILD SUCCESSFUL`; fresh XML at `2026-07-11 07:31:44` contains `13 tests, 0 failures, 0 errors` for each PSI and non-PSI `FunctionGenerated` slice.

## Macro FunctionGenerated builtin annotation metadata preservation

- problem type: macro PSI `FunctionGenerated.testNestFunctionGenericInst01` reported `UNDECLARED_TYPE_NAME` for builtin declaration annotations `Deprecated` and `Frozen`, while the macro light-tree path accepted the same source.
- root cause: the PSI raw builder's builtin annotation wrapper path appended a `CfirAnnotationCall` without recording its stable annotation-slot metadata. The error-node collector therefore had no structural evidence that the unresolved delegated type reference belonged to a builtin annotation; it also searched `containingElements` even though annotation calls are traversed through `callsOrAssignments`.
- official Cangjie evidence: `external/cangjie_compiler/include/cangjie/Parse/Parser.h` registers `Deprecated` and `Frozen` in `BUILTIN_ANNOTATIONS`; `external/cangjie_compiler/src/Parse/ParseAnnotations.cpp` parses and validates builtin annotations directly, and `external/cangjie_compiler/src/Parse/ParseDecl.cpp` applies `Frozen` as declaration state rather than resolving either name as an ordinary user type.
- Kotlin counterpart files consulted: `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/resolve/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/resolve/transformers/FirTypeResolveTransformer.kt`, where annotations have dedicated transformation/resolution paths on declaration owners instead of being treated only as ordinary unresolved type references.
- CFIR owner files changed: `cfir/raw-cfir/psi2cfir/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/builder/PsiRawCfirBuilder.kt` and `cfir/checkers/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/analysis/collectors/components/ErrorNodeDiagnosticCollectorComponent.kt`.
- repair principle: preserve stable metadata for every builtin annotation created by the PSI raw builder and consume it from the actual annotation-call traversal stack, so unresolved delegated type refs are filtered by annotation identity rather than fixture names.
- fixtures covered: all three methods in both `CfirAnalysisMacroTest.macro.llt.function.FunctionGenerated` and `CfirAnalysisMacroPsiTest.macro.llt.function.FunctionGenerated`, including `testNestFunctionGenericInst01`.
- fixture correction: none; no `.cj` fixture was changed for this problem type.
- verification command: `.\gradlew.bat :cfir:analysis-tests:test --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisMacroTest.macro.llt.function.FunctionGenerated' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisMacroPsiTest.macro.llt.function.FunctionGenerated' --quiet --no-build-cache --no-configuration-cache`.
- verification outcome: `BUILD SUCCESSFUL`; fresh XML at `2026-07-11 15:59:09` contains `3 tests, 0 failures, 0 errors` for macro light-tree and `3 tests, 0 failures, 0 errors` for macro PSI.

## Function-value overload selection and callable-reference diagnostics

- problem type: overloaded function names used as values must remain candidate-local through postponed completion, distinguish ambiguity from no matching declaration and missing generic type arguments, and select only candidates whose complete function type matches the target without Option auto-boxing.
- root cause: callable-reference completion wrote intermediate candidate state directly onto the shared expression, most-specific reduction discarded return-type-distinct overloads before target typing, ordinary subtype checking admitted Option auto-boxing for function-reference parameters, and failure paths collapsed ambiguity/no-match/generic-reference errors into unrelated candidate diagnostics.
- official Cangjie evidence: `cjc` accepted `call(g, true)` and `(Bool) -> Int64 = g`; `(Bool) -> Bool = g` selected `g(Bool) -> Int64` and then reported a type mismatch rather than selecting `g(Option<Bool>)`. Official `func_assign_var.cj` behavior reports no matching function declaration for reference on `x`, ambiguous use for `Sub().ff` and bare `a`, while a bare generic `foo` reference reports `sema_generic_type_without_type_argument`. Implementation evidence was traced through `external/cangjie_compiler/src/Sema/TypeCheckExpr/NameReferenceExpr.cpp` callable-reference checking, `external/cangjie_compiler/src/Sema/TypeCheckCall.cpp` candidate matching, and `external/cangjie_compiler/src/Sema/TypeManager.cpp` function-type compatibility.
- Kotlin counterpart files consulted: `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/resolve/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/resolve/calls/ConeResolutionAtom.kt`, `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/resolve/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/resolve/inference/PostponedArgumentsAnalyzer.kt`, `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/resolve/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/resolve/transformers/FirCallCompletionResultsWriterTransformer.kt`, and callable-reference expected-type resolution stages under `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/resolve/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/resolve/calls/stages`.
- CFIR owner files changed: `common/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/type/TypeCheckerState.kt`, `common/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/type/AbstractTypeChecker.kt`, `cfir/semantics/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/diagnostic/ConeDiagnostic.kt`, `cfir/semantics/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/diagnostic/ResolutionDiagnostic.kt`, `cfir/resolve/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/resolve/calls/ConeResolutionAtom.kt`, `cfir/resolve/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/resolve/body/CfirCallResolver.kt`, `cfir/resolve/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/resolve/inference/PostponedArgumentsAnalyzer.kt`, `cfir/resolve/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/resolve/inference/CfirCallCompletionResultsWriterTransformer.kt`, `cfir/resolve/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/resolve/calls/stages/CfirCheckCallableReferenceExpectedType.kt`, and the generated/checker diagnostic surfaces for `AMBIGUOUS_FUNCTION_REFERENCE`; fixture correction in `cfir/analysis-tests/testData/llt/function/func_assign_var.cj`.
- repair principle: keep callable-reference candidate state on its postponed atom until one final candidate is selected, and perform target function-type compatibility through a dedicated strict subtype path so every caller observes the same official ambiguity/no-match/selection semantics.
- fixtures covered: PSI and non-PSI `FunctionGenerated.testOverloadedFunc01`, `testMemberFuncOverload1`, `testFuncAssignVar`, and `testFuncAssignVar02`.
- fixture correction: `func_assign_var.cj` now expects the official no-matching-function-reference diagnostic for `x`; ambiguity expectations remain on `Sub().ff` and bare `a`.
- verification commands: focused LT/PSI reruns for all four methods, followed by the four-bucket full `FunctionGenerated` command.
- verification outcome: all eight focused LT/PSI methods passed; the fresh four-bucket full regression at `2026-07-12 05:06:26` contained none of these four methods in its failure list.

## Function-name shadowing across local scopes, members, and classifiers

- problem type: a member function must shadow a same-signature package classifier constructor, and nested local functions must prevent an outer local variable from exposing still-outer member functions during an unqualified call.
- root cause: `CfirCallResolver` unconditionally replaced every ordinary tower result with a same-file classifier constructor, while no-receiver tower lookup selected candidates solely by applicability and could cross an outer local non-function declaration after an inner function had already been found. The same fixture also exposed the missing official single-character String-to-Rune conversion in typed variable initializers.
- official Cangjie evidence: `cjc --diagnostic-format json --error-count-limit all function_shadow_01.cj` produced no diagnostics; the marker-free `function_shadow_02` probe reported only `sema_mismatched_types` for `Rune` passed to the nested `C(Int8)`. `external/cangjie_compiler/src/Sema/LookUpImpl.cpp` `FindRealResult` stops parent-scope lookup when accumulated functions encounter an outer non-function; `external/cangjie_compiler/src/Sema/TypeCheckCall.cpp` `FilterShadowedFunc` removes same-signature functions from lower scope levels. `external/cangjie_compiler/src/Sema/TypeCheckDecl.cpp` `SynchronizeTypeAndInitializer`, `TypeCheckExpr/AssignExpr.cpp`, and `AST/Utils.cpp` `IsSingleRuneStringLiteral` establish the declaration/assignment-only String-to-Rune conversion; a direct Rune-argument probe remained an error.
- Kotlin counterpart files consulted: `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/resolve/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/resolve/calls/tower/FirTowerResolveTask.kt`, `TowerLevels.kt`, `TowerResolveManager.kt`, `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/resolve/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/resolve/calls/ConstructorProcessing.kt`, and `FirInvokeResolveTowerExtension.kt` for tower grouping, constructor participation, and callable-value invoke separation.
- CFIR owner files changed: `cfir/resolve/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/resolve/body/CfirCallResolver.kt`, `cfir/resolve/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/resolve/calls/tower/CfirTowerResolveTask.kt`, `cfir/resolve/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/resolve/body/CfirSingleRuneStringLiteralConversion.kt`, `cfir/resolve/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/resolve/body/CfirDeclarationsResolveTransformer.kt`, and `cfir/resolve/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/resolve/body/CfirExpressionsResolveTransformer.kt`; fixture correction in `cfir/analysis-tests/testData/llt/function/function_shadow_02.cj`.
- repair principle: let ordinary tower candidates win over classifier fallback, preserve the official local declaration-kind barrier independently of candidate applicability, and perform single-rune String conversion only in declaration initializer/assignment contexts.
- fixtures covered: PSI and non-PSI `FunctionGenerated.testFunctionShadow01` and `testFunctionShadow02`, with callable-value and overload guards including `testComposition00` and `testOverloadedFunc01`.
- fixture correction: `function_shadow_02.cj` now uses the repository-standard `ARGUMENT_TYPE_MISMATCH` for the official `sema_mismatched_types` function-argument error; the initializer remains diagnostic-free because official typed-variable conversion makes the single-character String a Rune.
- verification commands: focused LT/PSI shadow reruns; focused `testComposition00`, `testOverloadedFunc01`, and both shadow methods; then the four-bucket full `FunctionGenerated` command.
- verification outcome: both focused guard commands were `BUILD SUCCESSFUL`; fresh four-bucket regression completed `208 tests` with `10 failures`, macro LT/PSI remained green, and neither shadow method nor any guard method remained in the failure list.

## Constructor delegation argument-diagnostic ownership and failed-candidate recovery

- problem type: explicit `this(...)` and `super(...)` delegation must produce exactly one argument-count diagnostic from call resolution, select the official failed constructor candidate, and never turn an inapplicable candidate into a recursive-constructor dependency edge.
- root cause: `CfirConstructorDelegationChecker` duplicated argument mapping already owned by call resolution; delegation resolution then used successful-overload most-specific selection for equally inapplicable arity candidates, preferring the two-parameter constructor over the current zero-parameter constructor for `this(1)`. Completion subsequently converted that failed recovery candidate into a resolved reference, which created a false recursive-constructor edge, while constructor `TOO_MANY_ARGUMENTS` source selection incorrectly treated constructors as ordinary functions and highlighted the whole call.
- official Cangjie evidence: cjc 1.0.5 reports only `sema_wrong_number_of_arguments` extra/expected 0 for `this(1)`, only missing/expected 2 for `super(1)`, and only `sema_interface_can_not_be_instantiated` for `Factory()`. `external/cangjie_compiler/src/Sema/TypeCheckReference.cpp:55-114`, `TypeCheckCall.cpp:1784-1821,2260-2289,2536-2563`, and `Diags.cpp:43-67` show target construction, failed-candidate selection, single wrong-number reporting, and argument-list ranges.
- Kotlin counterpart files consulted: `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/resolve/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/resolve/transformers/body/resolve/FirExpressionsResolveTransformer.kt`, `FirCallResolver.kt`, `FirTowerResolver.kt`, `ResolutionStages.kt`, `FirArgumentsToParametersMapper.kt`, `coneDiagnosticToFirDiagnostic.kt`, and `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/checkers/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/analysis/checkers/declaration/FirCommonConstructorDelegationIssuesChecker.kt`.
- CFIR owner files changed: `cfir/resolve/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/resolve/body/CfirCallResolver.kt`, `cfir/resolve/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/resolve/body/CfirExpressionsResolveTransformer.kt`, `cfir/resolve/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/resolve/inference/CfirCallCompleter.kt`, `cfir/resolve/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/resolve/diagnostics/coneDiagnosticToCfirDiagnostic.kt`, and `cfir/checkers/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/analysis/checkers/declaration/CfirConstructorDelegationChecker.kt`.
- repair principle: keep argument mapping and failed-candidate diagnostic recovery in the shared call resolver, leave declaration checkers responsible only for structural delegation rules, and prevent failed references from becoming semantic graph edges.
- fixtures covered: PSI and non-PSI variants of `diagnostics2/constructor/noConstructorThisAndSuper.cj`, `diagnostics/constructor/noConstructorRich.cj`, and `diagnostics2/invalid-declaration/noConstructorMatrix.cj`; direct-construction guards in the same generated slice also passed.
- fixture correction: none.
- verification command: `.\gradlew.bat :cfir:analysis-tests:test --tests '*testNoConstructor*' --no-daemon --max-workers=1 --console=plain --no-build-cache --no-configuration-cache`.
- verification outcome: `BUILD SUCCESSFUL in 8m 50s`; 12 generated tests were selected, all executable PSI/non-PSI tests passed, and 4 configured tests were skipped.

## Generic instantiation member-signature collisions
