## Standalone function values filter accessibility and generic candidates before ambiguity

- problem type: an uninvoked member function used as a value must first discard inaccessible overloads, then classify the remaining no-target candidates as one concrete function value, a non-generic ambiguity, or a generic function whose type arguments cannot be inferred.
- root cause: ordinary `NamedValueAccess` reduction retained private candidates and committed every multi-candidate set as `ConeAmbiguityError`. This made `_01` ambiguous despite one accessible overload and made `_03` ambiguous before applying the official generic-candidate rule. Function-value ambiguity mapping also widened the diagnostic to the whole qualified access. An initial shared `ConeUnableToInferGenericFuncError` mapping then exposed why standalone generic references and ordinary generic calls require distinct Cone diagnostic ownership for stable ranges.
- official Cangjie evidence: cjc 1.0.5 accepts `_01`; `_02` reports one `sema_ambiguous_use` from the two accessible non-generic overloads; `_03` reports one `sema_generic_func_without_type_arg` after all accessible candidates are classified as generic. `external/cangjie_compiler/src/Sema/TypeCheckReference.cpp:376-395,500-539` performs accessibility filtering, removes generic functions when no expected type or explicit type arguments exist, reports generic-type-argument failure if that removal empties the set, and only then reports ordinary ambiguity. `TypeCheckAccess.cpp:93-107` supplies per-candidate access checks. `Diags.cpp:314-325,452-470` distinguishes generic-reference and ambiguity anchors.
- Kotlin counterpart files consulted: `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/resolve/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/resolve/transformers/body/resolve/FirExpressionsResolveTransformer.kt`; `FirSyntheticCallGenerator.kt`; `calls/stages/ArgumentCheckingProcessor.kt`; `calls/FirCallResolver.kt`; `ConeResolvedCallableReferenceAtom.kt`; `EagerResolveOfCallableReferences.kt`; and `PostponedArgumentsAnalyzer.kt`. Kotlin's callable-reference diagnostics use the callee reference source rather than the receiver-qualified access source.
- CFIR owner files changed: `cfir/resolve/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/resolve/body/CfirCallResolver.kt`; `cfir/semantics/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/diagnostic/ConeDiagnostic.kt`; and `cfir/checkers/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/analysis/diagnostics/coneDiagnosticToCfirDiagnostic.kt`.
- repair principle: restrict standalone function-value filtering to `NamedValueAccess` nodes that are not function-call callee probes, filter unsuccessful/inaccessible candidates before generic classification, and represent generic function references with a dedicated Cone diagnostic that maps to the project `UNABLE_TO_INFER_GENERIC_FUNC` on the selector token without changing ordinary call-level inference ranges.
- fixtures covered: PSI and non-PSI `call_reference_inference_01.cj`, `_02.cj`, and `_03.cj`; `call_inference_01.cj` is the ordinary generic-call range guard.
- fixture correction: none.
- focused verification command: `.\gradlew.bat :cfir:analysis-tests:test --tests '*.llt.call.CallGenerated.testCallReferenceInference01' --tests '*.llt.call.CallGenerated.testCallReferenceInference02' --tests '*.llt.call.CallGenerated.testCallReferenceInference03' --tests '*.llt.call.CallGenerated.testCallInference01' -x :cfir:analysis-tests:generateTestGeneratorForCfirAnalysisTestsTests --no-configuration-cache --no-daemon --max-workers=1 --console=plain`.
- focused verification outcome: `BUILD SUCCESSFUL in 8m 53s`; all 8 PSI/LightTree tests passed after replacing source-shape heuristics with the dedicated diagnostic model.
- class verification command: `.\gradlew.bat :cfir:analysis-tests:test --tests '*.llt.call.CallGenerated' -x :cfir:analysis-tests:generateTestGeneratorForCfirAnalysisTestsTests --no-configuration-cache --no-daemon --max-workers=1 --console=plain`.
- class verification outcome: 172 tests completed in 1m50s; failures dropped from 43 to 35, removing all six function-reference failures with no new failures. PSI/LightTree `call27` also became green through the same accessible-candidate filtering and is retained as neighboring regression evidence.

## Same-classifier completion never injects foreign type variables

- problem type: nested generic enum-constructor arguments such as `None`, `Option<Int64>.None`, and `Option<Nothing>.None` must participate in bidirectional call inference without leaking a child candidate's fresh type variable into the outer candidate constraint system.
- root cause: the CFIR-specific `CfirCallCompleter.addSameClassifierTypeArgumentConstraints` recursively decomposed matching classifier types and injected equality constraints whenever the expected side contained a current not-fixed variable. The actual side could still contain a foreign/subcandidate `ConeTypeVariableType`; `ConstraintInjector` then treated that foreign constructor as writable in the current storage and crashed because it was absent from `callTypeVariables`.
- official Cangjie evidence: cjc 1.0.5 accepts all four calls in `call_error_report_04.cj` with no semantic diagnostics. `external/cangjie_compiler/src/Sema/TypeArgumentInference.cpp:297-312,513-522` postpones untyped enum constructors until a parameter target is available and only diagnoses after final inference failure; `LocalTypeArgumentSynthesis.cpp:265-276,301-319,827-832` unifies nested `Option` arguments and accepts `Nothing` as a real solution.
- Kotlin counterpart files consulted: Kotlin FIR `FirCallCompleter`, `CheckArguments`, and `ArgumentCheckingProcessor`; Kotlin adds argument constraints after atom subsystems are merged and does not perform a global same-classifier recursive equality pass over arbitrary foreign type-variable trees.
- CFIR owner files changed: `cfir/resolve/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/resolve/inference/CfirCallCompleter.kt`.
- repair principle: normalize both classifier trees with the current candidate substitutor and inject recursive equality only when every participating fresh constructor belongs to the current storage; foreign variables remain owned by their atom subsystem and final substitution path.
- fixtures covered: PSI and non-PSI `call_error_report_04.cj`, covering bare and qualified `None` with `Int64` and `Nothing`.
- fixture correction: none.
- focused verification command: `.\gradlew.bat :cfir:analysis-tests:test --tests '*.llt.call.CallGenerated.testCallErrorReport04' -x :cfir:analysis-tests:generateTestGeneratorForCfirAnalysisTestsTests --no-configuration-cache --no-daemon --max-workers=1 --console=plain`.
- focused verification outcome: `BUILD SUCCESSFUL in 5m 53s`; both PSI and LightTree tests passed with no exception and no diagnostics.
- family verification command: `.\gradlew.bat :cfir:analysis-tests:test --tests '*.llt.call.CallGenerated.testCallErrorReport01' --tests '*.llt.call.CallGenerated.testCallErrorReport02' --tests '*.llt.call.CallGenerated.testCallErrorReport03' --tests '*.llt.call.CallGenerated.testCallErrorReport04' -x :cfir:analysis-tests:generateTestGeneratorForCfirAnalysisTestsTests --no-configuration-cache --no-daemon --max-workers=1 --console=plain`.
- family verification outcome: 8 tests completed in 1m28s; only the six independently classified `_01/_02/_03` failures remained, while both `_04` entries passed.
- class verification command: `.\gradlew.bat :cfir:analysis-tests:test --tests '*.llt.call.CallGenerated' -x :cfir:analysis-tests:generateTestGeneratorForCfirAnalysisTestsTests --no-configuration-cache --no-daemon --max-workers=1 --console=plain`.
- class verification outcome: 172 tests completed in 1m38s; failures dropped from 35 to 33 with no new Call failures.

## Nested argument root errors dominate constructor ambiguity

- problem type: an unresolved expression nested through scalar construction and an array literal must remain the sole root diagnostic when the array is passed to an overloaded constructor; outer array and constructor recovery must not manufacture mismatch or ambiguity diagnostics.
- root cause: array element type aggregation discarded the nested error carrier after `Int64(UndeclaredType())` recovered to a normal-looking scalar type, so the array appeared as ordinary `Array<Int64>`. After restoring a structured array error, the overloaded `ArrayList<Int64>` constructor candidates still remained nominally successful and `createResolvedNamedReference` committed `AMBIGUOUS_CONSTRUCTOR_CALL`, because ambiguity aggregation only considered candidate applicability and nested ambiguity replacements, not a shared structured error argument.
- official Cangjie evidence: cjc 1.0.5 reports exactly one `sema_undeclared_identifier` on `UndeclaredType` and no diagnostics on the enclosing `Int64` call, array literal, or `ArrayList` construction. `external/cangjie_compiler/src/Sema/TypeCheckReference.cpp:332-340` reports the missing identifier; `TypeCheckUtil.cpp:228-231` classifies invalid child types for diagnostic suppression; `TypeCheckCall.cpp:1165-1179,2611-2623` skips argument/call diagnostics when a child argument already carries an invalid root.
- Kotlin counterpart files consulted: Kotlin FIR `ArgumentCheckingProcessor`, error-type argument handling, `ErrorTypeInArguments` mapping, and ambiguity/none-applicable diagnostic aggregation; shared nested root diagnostics dominate outer overload diagnostics while the original error node remains reportable.
- CFIR owner files changed: `cfir/resolve/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/resolve/body/CfirExpressionsResolveTransformer.kt`; `CfirCallResolver.kt`; `cfir/semantics/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/diagnostic/ConeDiagnostic.kt`; and `cfir/checkers/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/analysis/diagnostics/coneDiagnosticToCfirDiagnostic.kt`.
- repair principle: preserve a nested root error as a `ConeErrorType` at aggregate expression boundaries while retaining a delegated recovery shape, and mark constructor ambiguity as an error-argument cascade only when every competing symbol is a constructor and a shared top-level argument structurally wraps a nested root error; suppress only that derived ambiguity, never the inner diagnostic or a real constructor ambiguity.
- fixtures covered: PSI and non-PSI `call_error_report_01.cj`; the rule applies to nested scalar calls, array/VArray recovery shapes, and overloaded constructor calls receiving structured error arguments.
- fixture correction: none.
- diagnostic probe: a temporary test-framework print (removed immediately after use) proved the pre-final output was `AMBIGUOUS_CONSTRUCTOR_CALL` on `ArrayList<Int64>` plus the correct inner `UNRESOLVED_REFERENCE`, locating the remaining aggregation seam without modifying test data.
- focused verification command: `.\gradlew.bat :cfir:analysis-tests:test --tests '*.llt.call.CallGenerated.testCallErrorReport01' -x :cfir:analysis-tests:generateTestGeneratorForCfirAnalysisTestsTests --no-configuration-cache --no-daemon --max-workers=1 --console=plain`.
- focused verification outcome: `BUILD SUCCESSFUL in 6m 48s`; both PSI and LightTree tests passed with only the expected inner unresolved diagnostic.
- family verification command: `.\gradlew.bat :cfir:analysis-tests:test --tests '*.llt.call.CallGenerated.testCallErrorReport01' --tests '*.llt.call.CallGenerated.testCallErrorReport02' --tests '*.llt.call.CallGenerated.testCallErrorReport03' --tests '*.llt.call.CallGenerated.testCallErrorReport04' -x :cfir:analysis-tests:generateTestGeneratorForCfirAnalysisTestsTests --no-configuration-cache --no-daemon --max-workers=1 --console=plain`.
- family verification outcome: 8 tests completed in 2m4s; `_01` and `_04` passed on both entry paths, leaving only the independently classified `_02/_03` failures.
- class verification command: `.\gradlew.bat :cfir:analysis-tests:test --tests '*.llt.call.CallGenerated' -x :cfir:analysis-tests:generateTestGeneratorForCfirAnalysisTestsTests --no-configuration-cache --no-daemon --max-workers=1 --console=plain`.
- class verification outcome: 172 tests completed in 2m25s; failures dropped from 33 to 29 with no new Call failures.

## Overload mismatch aggregation selects the argument shared by every candidate

- problem type: when overload candidates disagree on earlier parameter shapes but all fail on the same later argument with the same expected type, the shared concrete argument mismatch is the primary diagnostic; candidate iteration order must not select a mismatch that belongs to only part of the overload set.
- root cause: each candidate correctly recorded its own `ArgumentTypeMismatch`, but ambiguity mapping first coalesced each candidate to one list entry and then effectively trusted insertion order. For `String.indexOf`, some overloads rejected the first argument while every relevant overload rejected the second string argument where `Int64` was required, so CFIR sometimes reported the first argument and used the generic `ARGUMENT_TYPE_MISMATCH` surface.
- official Cangjie evidence: cjc 1.0.5 reports exactly two `sema_mismatched_types` diagnostics in `call_error_report_02.cj`, both on the second argument literal `"12"`, with expected `Int64` and found `String`; it reports no first-argument or whole-call diagnostic. `external/cangjie_compiler/src/Sema/TypeCheckCall.cpp:1165-1180` checks instantiated candidate parameters and diagnoses the valid mismatching argument; `Diags.cpp:195-245` anchors the mismatch on that argument and renders expected/found types.
- Kotlin counterpart files consulted: Kotlin FIR candidate-local argument diagnostics and ambiguity/none-applicable diagnostic aggregation; the shared root is derived across the best candidate set rather than selected from one candidate's first diagnostic.
- CFIR owner files changed: `cfir/checkers/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/analysis/diagnostics/coneDiagnosticToCfirDiagnostic.kt`.
- repair principle: intersect `ArgumentTypeMismatch.argument` identities across all call candidates in original argument order, require the substituted expected types to be semantically equal, and only then map that shared overload root to the project `TYPE_MISMATCH`; leave single-candidate and non-shared argument diagnostics unchanged.
- fixtures covered: PSI and non-PSI `call_error_report_02.cj`, including overload sets whose first parameter accepts different concrete types while a later parameter shares one required type.
- fixture correction: none.
- focused verification command: `.\gradlew.bat :cfir:analysis-tests:test --tests '*.llt.call.CallGenerated.testCallErrorReport02' -x :cfir:analysis-tests:generateTestGeneratorForCfirAnalysisTestsTests --no-configuration-cache --no-daemon --max-workers=1 --console=plain`.
- focused verification outcome: `BUILD SUCCESSFUL in 5m 20s`; both PSI and LightTree tests passed with one `TYPE_MISMATCH` on each second argument.
- family verification command: `.\gradlew.bat :cfir:analysis-tests:test --tests '*.llt.call.CallGenerated.testCallErrorReport01' --tests '*.llt.call.CallGenerated.testCallErrorReport02' --tests '*.llt.call.CallGenerated.testCallErrorReport03' --tests '*.llt.call.CallGenerated.testCallErrorReport04' -x :cfir:analysis-tests:generateTestGeneratorForCfirAnalysisTestsTests --no-configuration-cache --no-daemon --max-workers=1 --console=plain`.
- family verification outcome: 8 tests completed in 1m22s; `_01`, `_02`, and `_04` passed on both entry paths, leaving only the independent `_03` function-body return mismatch.
- class verification command: `.\gradlew.bat :cfir:analysis-tests:test --tests '*.llt.call.CallGenerated' -x :cfir:analysis-tests:generateTestGeneratorForCfirAnalysisTestsTests --no-configuration-cache --no-daemon --max-workers=1 --console=plain`.

## Extend specialization conflicts preserve instantiated target ownership

- problem type: duplicate-interface checks must distinguish disjoint instantiated extend targets and report a specialization conflict only when a generic target pattern can be consistently instantiated to the specialized target and its inherited interface becomes exactly equal.
- root cause: `CfirExtendTargetKey.ClassLike` correctly grouped declarations by nominal `ClassId` for provider candidate recall, but the rule query flattened every interface in that broad bucket and discarded its owner target pattern. `CfirExtendDuplicateInterfaceChecker` and `CfirExtendSpecializationConflictChecker` then treated the nominal bucket as conflict identity and used `semanticKey.contains("__EXT_TP_")` as a generic-pattern heuristic, producing false duplicates for disjoint tuple/function, nested `A`/`B`, inconsistent repeated-parameter, and distinct concrete instantiations.
- official Cangjie evidence: cjc 1.0.5 accepts `duplicate_impl_6.cj`, `duplicate_impl_6_1.cj`, `duplicate_impl_7.cj`, and `same_sign_default_in_multi_extend_impl01.cj` with no diagnostics. `external/cangjie_compiler/src/Sema/TypeCheckExtend.cpp:183-267` builds the generic-target-to-specialized-target mapping, rejects inconsistent repeated bindings such as `T -> Int64` and `T -> String`, and reports `sema_extend_duplicate_interface` only when the substituted generic interface exactly equals the specialized interface. `external/cangjie_compiler/src/Sema/MultiTypeSubstUtils.cpp:342-361` recursively requires matching type kinds and declaration identity before producing a mapping.
- Kotlin counterpart files consulted: `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/providers/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/resolve/TypeExpansionUtils.kt`; `external/kotlin/core/compiler.common/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/types/AbstractStrictEqualityTypeChecker.kt`; `external/kotlin/core/compiler.common/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/types/AbstractTypeChecker.kt`; `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/providers/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/types/TypeUnification.kt`; and `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/providers/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/scopes/FirKotlinScopeProvider.kt`.
- CFIR owner files changed: `cfir/cfir-common/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/session/services/CfirExtendRuleQueryService.kt`; `cfir/resolve/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/resolve/services/CfirExtendSemanticModel.kt`; `CfirExtendIndexStore.kt`; `CfirExtendRuleQueryService.kt`; and `cfir/checkers/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/analysis/checkers/declaration/CfirExtendCheckers.kt`.
- repair principle: retain the nominal target key only as a broad candidate index, preserve complete instantiated target identity in the semantic model, and perform specialization comparison by structurally matching the generic target pattern before substituting and strictly comparing the inherited interface type.
- fixtures covered: PSI and non-PSI `duplicate_impl_1.cj`, `duplicate_impl_2.cj`, `duplicate_impl_3.cj`, `duplicate_impl_3_1.cj`, `duplicate_impl_4.cj`, `duplicate_impl_5.cj`, `duplicate_impl_5_1.cj`, `duplicate_impl_5_2.cj`, `duplicate_impl_5_3.cj`, `duplicate_impl_6.cj`, `duplicate_impl_6_1.cj`, `duplicate_impl_7.cj`, `duplicate_impl_builtin.cj`, `same_sign_default_in_multi_extend_impl01.cj`, `same_sign_default_in_multi_extend_impl02.cj`, and `generics_00038.cj`.
- fixture correction: none.
- focused verification command: `.\gradlew.bat :cfir:analysis-tests:test --tests '*SpecializationDuplicateCheckGenerated.testDuplicateImpl6' --tests '*SpecializationDuplicateCheckGenerated.testDuplicateImpl7' --tests '*SpecializationDuplicateCheckGenerated.testDuplicateImpl61' --tests '*GenericParamDeclCallInMemberFuncGenerated.testGenerics00038' --tests '*GenericParamDeclCallInMemberFuncGenerated.testSameSignDefaultInMultiExtendImpl01' --tests '*GenericParamDeclCallInMemberFuncGenerated.testSameSignDefaultInMultiExtendImpl02' -x :cfir:analysis-tests:generateTestGeneratorForCfirAnalysisTestsTests --no-configuration-cache --no-daemon --max-workers=1 --console=plain --quiet`.
- focused verification outcome: all false `EXTEND_DUPLICATE_INTERFACE` diagnostics disappeared. The six specialization negative methods passed on both PSI/LightTree paths; `generics_00038` and `same_sign_default_in_multi_extend_impl02` retained only their independent member-shadow/return-invariance differences.
- family verification command: `.\gradlew.bat :cfir:analysis-tests:test --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt.Extend.specialization_duplicate_check.SpecializationDuplicateCheckGenerated' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt.Extend.specialization_duplicate_check.SpecializationDuplicateCheckGenerated' -x :cfir:analysis-tests:generateTestGeneratorForCfirAnalysisTestsTests --no-configuration-cache --no-daemon --max-workers=1 --console=plain --quiet`.

## Function-body tail calls resolve before declared-return mismatch reporting

- problem type: a valid overloaded/generic call used as the final expression of a function with an incompatible explicit return type must resolve by its arguments first, then report the body mismatch on the complete tail expression.
- root cause: `CfirCheckExpectedReturnTypeBeforeArguments` rejected every concrete-`Unit` `std.core.print` overload against `main(): Int64` before argument checking; after that gate was opened, `CfirCallCompleter` still injected `Unit <: Int64` into the candidate constraint system and converted the valid generic call into `NEW_INFERENCE_ERROR`. The body checker also classified ordinary non-empty implicit tail-expression mismatches as `RETURN_TYPE_MISMATCH`, although official Cangjie reports the expression-local mismatched-types diagnostic and reserves the body-level “because of return type” path for empty bodies.
- official Cangjie evidence: cjc 1.0.5 reports one `sema_mismatched_types` over the complete `print(a)`, expected `Int64` and found `Unit`; `external/cangjie_runtime/stdlib/libs/std/core/print.cj:80-83` defines `print<T>(arg: T, flush!: Bool = false): Unit where T <: ToString`; `external/cangjie_compiler/src/Sema/TypeChecker.cpp:92-164`, `TypeCheckExpr/Block.cpp:43-92`, `TypeCheckCall.cpp:1395-1405`, `TypeCheckExpr/NameReferenceExpr.cpp:68-82`, `TypeCheckExpr/LoopExprs.cpp:36-44`, and `Diags.cpp:195-265` show that non-empty bodies target-check their real tail expression, while only an empty body is diagnosed at the function-body layer with the return-type “because” diagnostic.
- Kotlin counterpart files consulted: `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/providers/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/scopes/impl/FirExplicitStarImportingScope.kt`; `FirAbstractStarImportingScope.kt`; `FirAbstractImportingScope.kt`; `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/fir-deserialization/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/deserialization/AbstractFirDeserializedSymbolProvider.kt`; `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/entrypoint/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/session/environment/MetadataSymbolProvider.kt`; and the existing CFIR expected-return refinement architecture derived from Kotlin FIR call-resolution stages.
- CFIR owner files changed: `cfir/resolve/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/resolve/calls/stages/CfirCheckExpectedReturnTypeBeforeArguments.kt`; `cfir/resolve/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/resolve/inference/CfirCallCompleter.kt`; `cfir/checkers/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/analysis/checkers/expression/CfirFunctionBodyTypeMismatchChecker.kt`.
- repair principle: keep declared-return expected types available for unresolved result variables and nested overload refinement, but never let a proper concrete tail-call result be rejected or made contradictory before the legal call is completed; diagnose non-empty implicit tails on the actual expression and use the body-level return mismatch only when no tail expression exists.
- fixtures covered: PSI and LightTree `call_error_report_01.cj` through `call_error_report_04.cj`; neighboring body-diagnostic guards `return/return1.cj`, `while-let-expr/while_let8.cj`, and `join_meet/join_05_2.cj`.
- fixture correction: official cjc proves the non-empty bare-reference and while-loop tails in `return1.cj` and `while_let8.cj` are ordinary `sema_mismatched_types`, so their inline names were corrected from `RETURN_TYPE_MISMATCH` to `TYPE_MISMATCH`; the empty bodies in `join_05_2.cj` retain `RETURN_TYPE_MISMATCH`.
- focused verification command: `.\gradlew.bat :cfir:analysis-tests:test --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt.call.CallGenerated.testCallErrorReport03' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt.call.CallGenerated.testCallErrorReport03' -x :cfir:analysis-tests:generateTestGeneratorForCfirAnalysisTestsTests --console=plain`.
- focused verification outcome: `BUILD SUCCESSFUL in 4m 47s`; PSI and LightTree `testCallErrorReport03` both passed.
- family verification command: `.\gradlew.bat :cfir:analysis-tests:test --tests '*CallGenerated.testCallErrorReport01' --tests '*CallGenerated.testCallErrorReport02' --tests '*CallGenerated.testCallErrorReport03' --tests '*CallGenerated.testCallErrorReport04' --tests '*ReturnGenerated.testReturn1' --tests '*WhileLetExprGenerated.testWhileLet8' --tests '*JoinMeetGenerated.testJoin052' -x :cfir:analysis-tests:generateTestGeneratorForCfirAnalysisTestsTests --console=plain`.
- family verification outcome: `BUILD SUCCESSFUL in 7m 23s`; all 14 PSI/LightTree tests passed.
- complete Call verification command: `.\gradlew.bat :cfir:analysis-tests:test --tests '*llt.call.CallGenerated*' -x :cfir:analysis-tests:generateTestGeneratorForCfirAnalysisTestsTests --console=plain`.

## Explicit-constructor completion diagnostics retain the declaration header

- problem type: `CLASS_UNINITIALIZED_FIELD` on an explicit constructor must underline the complete constructor declaration header from its modifiers through the parameter-list right parenthesis, not only the `init` keyword.
- root cause: commit `9b2b4d88f` changed the shared constructor-completeness report source in `CfirInitializationCheckers` from `constructorDeclarationHeaderDiagnosticSource()` back to `constructorNameDiagnosticSource()`. Both PSI and LightTree therefore pre-narrowed the source to the `init` token before the diagnostic factory positioning strategy ran, reverting an already verified range contract.
- official Cangjie evidence: this is a range-only problem governed by the repository Diagnostic Range Policy rather than `cjc`'s CLI anchor. Existing verified range entries at lines 1030-1058 require explicit and primary constructor completion diagnostics to cover the declaration header and exclude the body.
- Kotlin counterpart files consulted: `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/checkers/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/analysis/checkers/declaration/FirMemberPropertiesChecker.kt`; `FirTopLevelPropertiesChecker.kt`; `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/checkers/checkers-component-generator/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/checkers/generator/diagnostics/FirDiagnosticsList.kt`; `external/kotlin/compiler/frontend.common-psi/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/diagnostics/PositioningStrategies.kt`; and `LightTreePositioningStrategies.kt`. Kotlin models declaration-signature ranges symmetrically in PSI and LightTree, while Cangjie keeps its language-specific per-constructor completion diagnostic owner.
- CFIR owner files changed: `cfir/checkers/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/analysis/checkers/declaration/CfirInitializationCheckers.kt` and `CfirDeclarationDiagnosticSources.kt`.
- repair principle: constructor-completion reporting stays in the shared initialization checker, but both source frontends must produce the exact constructor header or expose a violated source invariant; the checker must never degrade the diagnostic to the constructor name or whole declaration.
- fixtures covered: PSI and LightTree `class_init_constructor3.cj`, `class_init_constructor4.cj`, `class_init_constructor5.cj`, `class_init_with_nested_func0.cj`, and `class_init_with_nested_func1.cj`; guards include `static_or_global_var3.cj`, class/record `primaryConstructor2.cj`, and class/record `primaryConstructor8.cj`.
- fixture correction: none.

## Immutable assignment diagnostics retain the final selector token

- problem type: ordinary immutable assignments through qualified accesses used inconsistent declaration-wide, whole-assignment, and whole-left-value ranges; the current checker regression reported `this.b` where the Class fixtures require the final selector token `b`.
- root cause: `CfirAssignmentLegalityChecker` reported `CANNOT_ASSIGN_TO_IMMUTABLE` from `access.source`, so PSI and LightTree both exposed the complete qualified access. Historical fixtures also retained several incompatible whole-assignment and whole-left-value ranges for the same shared diagnostic owner.
- official Cangjie evidence: this is a range-only problem governed by the repository Diagnostic Range Policy. For a qualified assignment the relevant IDE token is the final selector; semantic firing and diagnostic-name multisets were unchanged in the triggering Class failures.
- Kotlin counterpart files consulted: `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/checkers/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/analysis/checkers/expression/FirReassignmentAndInvisibleSetterChecker.kt`; `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/checkers/checkers-component-generator/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/checkers/generator/diagnostics/FirDiagnosticsList.kt`; `external/kotlin/compiler/frontend.common-psi/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/diagnostics/PositioningStrategies.kt`; and `LightTreePositioningStrategies.kt`. Kotlin reports reassignment from `expression.lValue.source` with `SELECTOR_BY_QUALIFIED`, producing the final selector for `this.b` and equivalent qualified accesses.
- CFIR owner files changed: `cfir/checkers/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/analysis/checkers/expression/CfirAssignmentLegalityChecker.kt`.
- repair principle: all ordinary qualified immutable assignments use the callee selector source from the shared assignment-legality checker, while subscript, VArray-size, and increment/decrement diagnostics retain their separate expression contracts; missing assignment or selector sources now expose violated CFIR source invariants instead of degrading through fallback chains.
- fixtures covered: PSI and LightTree `class_no_member_func_use_before_init4.cj`, `invalid_init_call_1.cj`, `constructor/primaryConstructor/primaryConstructor8.cj`, `let_in_init2.cj`, `let_in_init3.cj`, `let_in_init4.cj`, `let_in_init6.cj`, `let_in_init7.cj`, `let_in_init8.cj`, `let_in_init9.cj`, `let_in_init10-1.cj`, `let_in_init10-2.cj`, `let_in_init11-1.cj`, `let_in_init11-2.cj`, `let_in_init13-1.cj`, `let_in_init14-1.cj`, `static_or_global_var/static_or_global_var14/test1.cj`, and `super_this/this_error_01.cj`; the family contains 35 `CANNOT_ASSIGN_TO_IMMUTABLE` expectations.
- fixture correction: qualified-access expectations were normalized to the final selector token and unqualified assignments to the complete identifier token; the same-range `CANNOT_ASSIGN_TO_IMMUTABLE` / `ILLEGAL_MEMBER_USED_IN_OPEN_CONSTRUCTOR` pair is rendered as one combined inline marker. Diagnostic names and source programs were not changed.
- focused verification command: `.\gradlew.bat :cfir:analysis-tests:test --tests '*ClassGenerated.testInvalidInitCall1' --tests '*ClassGenerated.testClassNoMemberFuncUseBeforeInit4' --tests '*PrimaryConstructorGenerated.testPrimaryConstructor8' --tests '*LetInInitGenerated' --tests '*StaticOrGlobalVar14Generated.testTest1' --tests '*SuperThisGenerated.testThisError01' -x :cfir:analysis-tests:generateTestGeneratorForCfirAnalysisTestsTests --no-configuration-cache --no-daemon --max-workers=1 --console=plain --no-build-cache`.
- focused verification outcome: 50 tests completed with 14 independent failures. XML proves every emitted `CANNOT_ASSIGN_TO_IMMUTABLE` in the covered family now has the expected selector/identifier range on PSI and LightTree; the remaining failures are separately classified declaration-completion ranges, missing temporary-object field immutability, and missing assignment-to-`this` diagnostics.
- Class regression command: `.\gradlew.bat :cfir:analysis-tests:test --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt._class.ClassGenerated' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt._class.ClassGenerated' -x :cfir:analysis-tests:generateTestGeneratorForCfirAnalysisTestsTests --no-configuration-cache --no-daemon --max-workers=1 --console=plain --no-build-cache`.

## Generic-instantiation member ambiguity retains inheritance provenance and reports once per trigger

- problem type: generic class/interface instantiation must preserve distinct inherited generic-interface member origins until concrete substitution, report `GENERIC_INSTANTIATION_CAUSES_AMBIGUOUS_FUNCTIONS` once per actual instantiation trigger, and report instantiated duplicate supertypes on the complete type reference without diagnosing declaration supertype parameter forwarding.
- root cause: `CfirClassUseSiteMemberScope` flattened substituted inherited functions to their root declarations, losing the generic owner instance that distinguishes `I<X>.foo` from `I<Y>.foo`; after provenance was restored, the file checker still keyed reports by source plus function name, so one `II<Int32>` trigger produced separate `foo` and `fs` diagnostics. The trigger collector also lacked a structural boundary between `class B<X,Y> <: A<X,Y>` forwarding and actual concrete/repeated instantiation, while duplicate-supertype reporting narrowed the retained type-ref source to the classifier token.
- official Cangjie evidence: cjc 1.0.5 reports exactly four ambiguity diagnostics in `interface_default_implemented_func_invalid_6.cj` (`B<X,X>`, inferred `B`, inferred `test`, and explicit `test<Int64>`) and none on `class B<X,Y> <: A<X,Y>`. For `interface_generic11.cj`, cjc reports exactly six ambiguity diagnostics, one on each concrete `II<...>` supertype reference, even though every reference produces both instance `foo` and static `fs` conflicts; additional probes with multiple conflicting names likewise retain one diagnostic per trigger source. `external/cangjie_compiler/src/Sema/InheritanceChecker/InstantiatedChecker.cpp:56-119,412-433` keeps member signatures distinct but anchors the top-level diagnostic at the instantiation trigger; `include/cangjie/Basic/DiagnosticEngine.h:540-570` deduplicates diagnostics by source range and severity; `src/Sema/TypeChecker.cpp:2545-2565,2597-2697` separates declaration duplicate-interface checks from concrete type/expression instantiation checks.
- Kotlin counterpart files consulted: `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/providers/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/scopes/impl/FirTypeIntersectionScopeContext.kt`; `AbstractFirUseSiteMemberScope.kt`; `FirClassSubstitutionScope.kt`; `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/checkers/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/analysis/checkers/declaration/FirImplementationMismatchChecker.kt`; `FirSupertypesChecker.kt`; and `external/kotlin/compiler/frontend.common/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/diagnostics/impl/DeduplicatingDiagnosticReporter.kt`. Kotlin retains overridden members with their base scopes in provider layers and deduplicates declaration diagnostics by source plus factory at the checker/reporter boundary.
- CFIR owner files changed: `cfir/providers/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/scopes/impl/CfirClassUseSiteMemberScope.kt`; `CfirClassSubstitutionScope.kt`; and `cfir/checkers/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/analysis/checkers/declaration/CfirGenericInstantiationChecker.kt`.
- repair principle: provider scopes retain complete direct-input/root member, generic owner instance, substitution, and base-scope provenance; the checker structurally suppresses only one-to-one declaration-supertype parameter forwarding, checks concrete/repeated/mixed instantiations, and deduplicates this diagnostic by trigger source rather than deleting any callable member.
- fixtures covered: PSI and LightTree class `interface_default_implemented_func_invalid_4/5/6.cj` and `generic_ok_2/3.cj`; all `class/class_instantiation_check` fixtures; `generics/inst_dup_functions001..007,009,010.cj`; complete `interface/generic_interface_inheritance/GenericInterfaceInheritanceGenerated`, especially `interface_generic9.cj` and `interface_generic11.cj`; `Extend/extend_instantiation_function_ambiguous1..4.cj`; and `invariant_override_returntype_02.cj`.
- fixture correction: `interface_generic13.cj` and `interface_generic14.cj` were corrected separately below; no generic-instantiation ambiguity expectation was weakened. Instantiated `SUPER_TYPES_DUPLICATE` ranges now cover the complete `II<...>` type reference according to the Diagnostic Range Policy.
- focused verification command: `.\gradlew.bat :cfir:analysis-tests:test --tests '*ClassGenerated.testInterfaceDefaultImplementedFuncInvalid6' --tests '*GenericInterfaceInheritanceGenerated.testInterfaceGeneric11' --tests '*LambdaCaptureGenerated.testCaptureNotInit02' -x :cfir:analysis-tests:generateTestGeneratorForCfirAnalysisTestsTests --no-configuration-cache --no-daemon --max-workers=1 --console=plain --no-build-cache`.
- focused verification outcome: `BUILD SUCCESSFUL in 3m 52s`; both PSI/LightTree paths passed for the declaration-forwarding boundary, per-trigger deduplication/range, and the independently corrected capture fixture.
- family verification command: `.\gradlew.bat :cfir:analysis-tests:test --tests '*ClassGenerated.testInterfaceDefaultImplementedFuncInvalid4' --tests '*ClassGenerated.testInterfaceDefaultImplementedFuncInvalid5' --tests '*ClassGenerated.testInterfaceDefaultImplementedFuncInvalid6' --tests '*ClassGenerated.testInterfaceDefaultImplementedFuncGenericOk2' --tests '*ClassGenerated.testInterfaceDefaultImplementedFuncGenericOk3' --tests '*GenericsGenerated.testInstDupFunctions*' --tests '*ClassInstantiationCheckGenerated' --tests '*GenericInterfaceInheritanceGenerated' --tests '*ExtendGenerated.testExtendInstantiationFunctionAmbiguous*' --tests '*ExtendGenerated.testInvariantOverrideReturntype02' -x :cfir:analysis-tests:generateTestGeneratorForCfirAnalysisTestsTests --no-configuration-cache --no-daemon --max-workers=1 --console=plain --no-build-cache`.
- family verification outcome: `BUILD SUCCESSFUL in 1m 30s`; the complete selected PSI/LightTree generic-instantiation and inheritance guards passed.

## Nested callable capture of uninitialized instance storage

- problem type: a nested named function or lambda inside an instance constructor that assigns an uninitialized instance field must report `CAPTURE_BEFORE_INITIALIZATION`, must not initialize the outer constructor state, and must leave constructor completion reporting intact.
- root cause: `CfirInitializationFlowAnalyzer` already traversed nested callable bodies in an isolated state and discarded their assignment effects, but `InitializationState.shouldReportCaptureBeforeInitialization` classified only `LOCAL_VARIABLE`; constructor-tracked fields are `INSTANCE_MEMBER`, so their nested writes skipped the capture diagnostic while correctly remaining uninitialized at constructor exit.
- official Cangjie evidence: cjc 1.0.5 reports exactly `CAPTURE_BEFORE_INITIALIZATION(a)` plus `CLASS_UNINITIALIZED_FIELD(a)` for `class_init_with_nested_func0.cj` and `_1.cj`, and no initialization error for `_2.cj`. `variable_use_before_init_07.cj` proves local `var`/`let` nested writes report capture while outer reads remain used-before, whereas static storage does not report capture and remains statically uninitialized. `external/cangjie_compiler/src/Sema/LegalityOfUsage/InitializationChecker.cpp:190-212,752-813,862-895,1481-1521,1600-1656` classifies cross-callable capture, explicitly prevents captured assignments from updating initialization state, preserves illegal-member priority for nested reads, excludes global/static storage, and reports constructor completion.
- Kotlin counterpart files consulted: `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/resolve/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/resolve/dfa/cfg/ControlFlowGraphBuilder.kt`; `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/semantics/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/resolve/dfa/cfg/CFGNode.kt`; `ControlFlowGraph.kt`; `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/checkers/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/analysis/cfa/VariableInitializationCheckProcessor.kt`; `FirPropertyInitializationAnalyzer.kt`; and `util/PropertyInitializationInfoCollector.kt`. Kotlin separates local/anonymous callable subgraphs from outer initialization flow and treats callable-scope crossing independently from storage-kind diagnostic classification.
- CFIR owner files changed: `cfir/checkers/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/analysis/checkers/declaration/CfirInitializationCheckers.kt`.
- repair principle: make capture eligibility an explicit tracked-storage property (`LOCAL_VARIABLE` and `INSTANCE_MEMBER`, excluding `STATIC_OR_GLOBAL`) while preserving member-initializer illegal-access priority, nested-state discard, callee-effect isolation, and return-target identity.
- fixtures covered: PSI and LightTree `class_init_with_nested_func0/1/2.cj`; `InitializationCheck/capture_func_rules_local_0101.cj` and `variable_use_before_init_07.cj`; `function_capture1/2/3.cj`; `lambda_capture/capture_not_init_01/02.cj`; `lambda_spawn.cj`; and `super_this_12.cj` as the independent open/abstract `this`-capture boundary.
- fixture correction: cjc reports three `CAPTURE_BEFORE_INITIALIZATION` diagnostics in `capture_not_init_02.cj` (assignment, do-while condition, and return) because the captured assignment does not advance nested state; the fixture was corrected to include the two missing later references without changing source grammar.
- family verification command: `.\gradlew.bat :cfir:analysis-tests:test --tests '*ClassGenerated.testClassInitWithNestedFunc0' --tests '*ClassGenerated.testClassInitWithNestedFunc1' --tests '*ClassGenerated.testClassInitWithNestedFunc2' --tests '*InitializationCheckGenerated.testVariableUseBeforeInit07' --tests '*InitializationCheckGenerated.testCaptureFuncRulesLocal0101' --tests '*FunctionGenerated.testFunctionCapture1' --tests '*FunctionGenerated.testFunctionCapture2' --tests '*FunctionGenerated.testFunctionCapture3' --tests '*LambdaCaptureGenerated.testCaptureNotInit01' --tests '*LambdaCaptureGenerated.testCaptureNotInit02' --tests '*LambdaGenerated.testLambdaSpawn' --tests '*SuperThisGenerated.testSuperThis12' -x :cfir:analysis-tests:generateTestGeneratorForCfirAnalysisTestsTests --no-configuration-cache --no-daemon --max-workers=1 --console=plain --no-build-cache`.
- family verification outcome: 24 tests completed with 6 failures, all confined to three independently classified PSI/LightTree guards: extra `CANNOT_ASSIGN_TO_IMMUTABLE` on the local `let` first assignment, missing `USE_FUNC_CAPTURE_VAR_ALONE` on one lambda, and missing `ILLEGAL_CAPTURE_THIS` for open/abstract classes. Every capture-before diagnostic, static exclusion, nested-state isolation, constructor completion pair, and corrected three-reference lambda case matched.
- Class regression command: `.\gradlew.bat :cfir:analysis-tests:test --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt._class.ClassGenerated' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt._class.ClassGenerated' -x :cfir:analysis-tests:generateTestGeneratorForCfirAnalysisTestsTests --no-configuration-cache --no-daemon --max-workers=1 --console=plain --no-build-cache`.
- Class regression outcome: 464 tests completed with 20 failures, down from the verified 26-failure baseline. `testClassInitWithNestedFunc0`, `testClassInitWithNestedFunc1`, and `testInterfaceDefaultImplementedFuncInvalid6` passed on both PSI and LightTree, with no new Class failure.

## Generic-interface direct implementation return diagnostic ownership

- problem type: a directly declared interface function that implements/redefines a merged inherited signature with incompatible return types must use the project diagnostic corresponding to official `sema_return_type_incompatible`, not the obsolete override-mismatch marker.
- root cause: `interface_generic13.cj` and `interface_generic14.cj` retained historical `OVERRIDING_RETURN_TYPE_MISMATCH` inline markers even though ordinary override/redef/implementation return incompatibility had already been centralized in `CfirInheritanceDeepChecker` as `RETURN_TYPE_INCOMPATIBLE`; no production checker emits the obsolete factory for this path.
- official Cangjie evidence: cjc 1.0.5 reports one `sema_return_type_incompatible` on direct `foo` in each fixture. For two inherited returns it notes `C` is not a subtype of `D`; for three it aggregates `D` and `E` into the same single diagnostic. Removing direct `foo` changes the official diagnostic to declaration-level `sema_inherit_member_type_inconsistent`, proving the direct implementation relation owns the original error. `external/cangjie_compiler/src/Sema/InheritanceChecker/MergeInheritedMemberHelper.cpp:87-179` merges same-signature inherited functions; `StructInheritanceChecker.cpp:876-899,1331-1407` checks the direct member against every merged return and reports one return-type incompatibility.
- Kotlin counterpart files consulted: `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/providers/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/scopes/impl/FirTypeIntersectionScopeContext.kt`; `AbstractFirUseSiteMemberScope.kt`; `FirClassSubstitutionScope.kt`; `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/checkers/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/analysis/checkers/declaration/FirOverrideChecker.kt`; and `FirImplementationMismatchChecker.kt`. Provider provenance retains the full inherited set; diagnostic ownership remains in declaration/inheritance checkers.
- CFIR owner files changed: no production owner change; fixture corrections only in `cfir/analysis-tests/testData/llt/interface/generic_interface_inheritance/interface_generic13.cj` and `interface_generic14.cj`.
- repair principle: preserve all inherited generic-interface provenance and correct stale expectations to the shared inheritance-relation diagnostic instead of changing scope merge or reintroducing a duplicate override checker.
- fixtures covered: PSI and LightTree `interface_generic12.cj`, `interface_generic13.cj`, `interface_generic14.cj`, and `interface_generic15.cj`; broader guards are the complete `GenericInterfaceInheritanceGenerated` suites and existing Class/Generics return-type ownership entries.
- fixture correction: replaced `OVERRIDING_RETURN_TYPE_MISMATCH` with `RETURN_TYPE_INCOMPATIBLE` in `interface_generic13.cj` and `interface_generic14.cj`, licensed by official `sema_return_type_incompatible` and the existing project diagnostic ownership.
- verification command: included in the complete generic-instantiation family command above.
- verification outcome: both complete PSI/LightTree `GenericInterfaceInheritanceGenerated` suites passed within the successful family run.

## Callable override type parameters are compared by alpha-equivalence

- problem type: parent and child generic functions that override/redefine each other must compare corresponding callable type parameters in one shared type-parameter space after owner substitution; distinct symbols created for the parent use-site copy must not manufacture return-type conflicts.
- root cause: `CfirClassSubstitutionScope` correctly rebuilt the inherited function copy and its callable type parameters, and `CfirOverrideChecker` already mapped the parent callable parameters to the child by position. `CfirInheritanceDeepChecker.functionReturnTypeConflict`, however, compared the parent copy return type and child return type directly, so `V_parent_copy` and `V_child` were treated as unrelated symbols and produced false `RETURN_TYPE_INCOMPATIBLE` / `RETURN_TYPE_INVARIANCE` diagnostics.
- official Cangjie evidence: cjc 1.0.5 reports only the call-site generic constraint mismatch, the two illegal primitive upper bounds, and `GENERIC_CONSTRAINT_NOT_LOOSER` for `class_redef_11.cj`; it reports no return-type diagnostic. It accepts `class_redef_12.cj` with zero diagnostics. Thus corresponding parent/child callable type parameters are alpha-equivalent for return comparison even after the parent owner type has been instantiated.
- Kotlin counterpart files consulted: `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/providers/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/scopes/impl/FirClassSubstitutionScope.kt`; `FirStandardOverrideChecker.kt`; `FirAbstractOverrideChecker.kt` (`buildSubstitutorForOverridesCheck`); `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/providers/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/scopes/FirOverrideChecker.kt`; and `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/checkers/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/analysis/checkers/declaration/FirOverrideChecker.kt`.
- CFIR owner files changed: `cfir/providers/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/scopes/CfirOverrideSignatureUtils.kt`; `cfir/checkers/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/analysis/checkers/declaration/CfirOverrideChecker.kt`; and `CfirInheritanceDeepChecker.kt`.
- repair principle: construct one providers-layer parent-current-copy to child callable type-parameter substitutor, require equal arity, and make every override return/constraint comparison consume it before invariance or subtype checks; never truncate the mapping or compare un-substituted fallback types.
- fixtures covered: PSI and LightTree `class_redef_11.cj`, `class_redef_12.cj`, all `class_redef*` fixtures, `generic_override01/02/06.cj`, `class_impl_interface_abstract_func7/8.cj`, `interface_implement_invalid_2.cj`, and `class_redef_9.cj` as concrete incompatibility guards.
- fixture correction: `class_redef_11.cj` marked only the first character of `Int32` for `GENERIC_TYPE_ARGUMENT_NOT_MATCH_CONSTRAINT`; the marker now covers the complete `Int32` token according to the project Diagnostic Range Policy. Source grammar and expected diagnostic identity are unchanged.
- focused verification command: `.\gradlew.bat :cfir:analysis-tests:test --tests '*ClassGenerated.testClassRedef11' --tests '*ClassGenerated.testClassRedef12' -x :cfir:analysis-tests:generateTestGeneratorForCfirAnalysisTestsTests --no-daemon --max-workers=1 --console=plain --no-build-cache --no-configuration-cache`.
- focused verification outcome: `BUILD SUCCESSFUL in 2m 8s`; all four PSI/LightTree `class_redef_11/12` tests passed.
- family verification command: `.\gradlew.bat :cfir:analysis-tests:test --tests '*ClassGenerated.testClassRedef*' --tests '*GenericsGenerated.testGenericOverride01' --tests '*GenericsGenerated.testGenericOverride02' --tests '*GenericsGenerated.testGenericOverride06' --tests '*ClassGenerated.testClassImplInterfaceAbstractFunc7' --tests '*ClassGenerated.testClassImplInterfaceAbstractFunc8' --tests '*ClassGenerated.testInterfaceImplementInvalid2' -x :cfir:analysis-tests:generateTestGeneratorForCfirAnalysisTestsTests --no-daemon --max-workers=1 --console=plain --no-build-cache --no-configuration-cache`.
- family verification outcome: `BUILD SUCCESSFUL in 2m 33s`; all alpha-equivalent cases, generic-constraint cases, and concrete return-incompatibility negative controls passed on both entry paths.
- Class regression command: `.\gradlew.bat :cfir:analysis-tests:test --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt._class.ClassGenerated' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt._class.ClassGenerated' -x :cfir:analysis-tests:generateTestGeneratorForCfirAnalysisTestsTests --no-daemon --max-workers=1 --console=plain --no-build-cache --no-configuration-cache`.
- Class regression outcome: 470 tests completed with 10 failures, down from the current verified 14-failure baseline. The only remaining failures are the pre-existing `class_static_04`, `member3`, `class3`, `class_access_control17`, and `invalid_super_call` in PSI and LightTree; no new Class failure appeared.

## Declaration-tailed function bodies preserve Unit semantics and declaration ownership

- problem type: an explicitly non-Unit function whose body ends in a declaration must treat the declaration as producing `Unit`, report the mismatch on that declaration, and remain distinct from a genuinely empty body.
- root cause: `CfirFunctionBodyTypeMismatchChecker` converted the final statement directly to `CfirExpression`. A declaration tail therefore became `null`, which correctly implied `Unit` but incorrectly lost the declaration source and made the checker classify the body as empty, producing `RETURN_TYPE_MISMATCH` over the whole block instead of `TYPE_MISMATCH` over the final declaration.
- official Cangjie evidence: cjc 1.0.5 reports `sema_mismatched_types` on the complete `var value: Data = Data()` declaration in `class3.cj`, with `expected 'Int64', found 'Unit'` and the note `definitions and declarations are always of type 'Unit'`. A minimized empty body instead reports `sema_mismatched_types_because` over the body. `external/cangjie_compiler/src/Sema/TypeCheckExpr/Block.cpp:21-40,70-80` gives declaration tails the `Unit` type and diagnoses the final declaration; `TypeChecker.cpp:134-144` reserves the body-level path for an empty body; `Diags.cpp:195-207,248-264` owns the two diagnostic forms.
- Kotlin counterpart files consulted: `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/resolve/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/resolve/transformers/body/resolve/BodyResolveUtils.kt:122-131`; `FirExpressionsResolveTransformer.kt:852-868`; `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/checkers/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/analysis/checkers/declaration/FirFunctionReturnChecker.kt:37-43`; `FirErrors.kt:1021`; `PositioningStrategies.kt:663-669`; and `LightTreePositioningStrategies.kt:1214-1228`. Kotlin likewise separates the block result expression from the syntactic final statement/source.
- CFIR owner files changed: `cfir/checkers/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/analysis/checkers/expression/CfirFunctionBodyTypeMismatchChecker.kt`; fixture correction in `cfir/analysis-tests/testData/llt/class/class3.cj`.
- repair principle: model `tailStatement`, `tailExpression`, `actualType`, and `resultSource` separately so declaration tails remain semantically `Unit` while preserving their own diagnostic ownership; only `statements.isEmpty()` may use the empty-body source and diagnostic path.
- fixtures covered: PSI and LightTree `class3.cj`; `class/static_call_non_static/class_static_call_non_static1.cj`; `record/static_call_non_static/record_static_call_non_static1.cj`; `join_meet/join_05_2.cj`; and the getter-empty-body portion of `enum/enum17_prop2.cj`.
- fixture correction: added project `TYPE_MISMATCH` around the complete final declaration in `class3.cj`, exactly matching official declaration ownership without changing source grammar.
- focused verification command: `.\gradlew.bat :cfir:analysis-tests:test --tests '*ClassGenerated.testClass3' --tests '*StaticCallNonStaticGenerated.testClassStaticCallNonStatic1' --tests '*StaticCallNonStaticGenerated.testRecordStaticCallNonStatic1' --tests '*JoinMeetGenerated.testJoin052' -x :cfir:analysis-tests:generateTestGeneratorForCfirAnalysisTestsTests --no-daemon --max-workers=1 --console=plain --no-build-cache --no-configuration-cache`.
- focused verification outcome: `BUILD SUCCESSFUL in 2m 2s`; all eight PSI/LightTree declaration-tail and empty-body tests passed. A separate run including `EnumGenerated.testEnum17Prop2` retained the expected getter-empty-body `TYPE_MISMATCH`; that fixture remained red only because of two pre-existing, unrelated `CANNOT_ASSIGN_TO_IMMUTABLE` range differences.
- Class regression command: `.\gradlew.bat :cfir:analysis-tests:test --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt._class.ClassGenerated' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt._class.ClassGenerated' -x :cfir:analysis-tests:generateTestGeneratorForCfirAnalysisTestsTests --no-daemon --max-workers=1 --console=plain --no-build-cache --no-configuration-cache`.

## Bare static qualifier function references form overload ambiguity before visibility filtering

- problem type: a type-name-qualified, standalone static function value with no expected type must form its overload set before accessibility filtering, so an inaccessible private static overload still participates in `AMBIGUOUS_USE`; ordinary calls, typed function references, and object-receiver values retain their distinct visibility order.
- root cause: `CfirCandidateCollector` retained both static overload discoveries from the same best tower group, but `CfirCallResolver.reduceCandidates` applied `candidate.isSuccessful` to every standalone function value before preserving the overload set. Outside the declaring class, the private overload therefore disappeared before ambiguity construction, while the same source inside the class kept both candidates and behaved correctly.
- official Cangjie evidence: cjc 1.0.5 reports `sema_ambiguous_use` for both the in-class and out-of-class `Test1.foo` expressions in `class_access_control17.cj`, listing the public `foo()` and private `foo(Int64)` candidates. A typed out-of-class `(Int64) -> Int64 = Test1.foo` instead selects the private overload and reports `sema_invalid_access_function`, while `Test1.foo(1)` follows ordinary call resolution and excludes the private overload before argument checking. `external/cangjie_compiler/src/Sema/TypeCheckExpr/NameReferenceExpr.cpp:634-681`, `TypeCheckReference.cpp:376-394,449-495`, `TypeCheckAccess.cpp:93-126`, and `Diags.cpp:452-470` separate static target discovery, bare-reference ambiguity, later access checking, and ambiguity reporting.
- Kotlin counterpart files consulted: `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/resolve/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/resolve/calls/candidate/CallKind.kt`; `stages/ResolutionStages.kt`; `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/semantics/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/resolve/calls/ResolutionDiagnostic.kt`; `candidate/CandidateCollector.kt`; and `FirCallResolver.kt`. Kotlin remains visibility/applicability-first, so it supplies only the candidate-discovery and diagnostic-layer architecture; the pre-visibility bare-static rule is Cangjie-specific and comes solely from official Cangjie evidence.
- CFIR owner files changed: `cfir/resolve/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/resolve/body/CfirCallResolver.kt`.
- repair principle: in the shared function-value candidate-selection policy, preserve the collector's same-best-group, pre-visibility overload set only for standalone `NamedValueAccess` with no expected type, no explicit type arguments, a real qualifier scope, and more than one genuinely static `CfirFunction`; typed references are handled separately by expected-type target selection, while ordinary calls, no-expected-type object receivers, and explicit-type-argument paths continue to use their accessibility-first rules.
- fixtures covered: PSI and LightTree `class_access_control15.cj`, `class_access_control16.cj`, `class_access_control17.cj`, `class_access_control18.cj`, `overload/overload_resolution_1.cj`, and `call/call_reference_inference_01/02/03.cj`.
- verification command: `.\gradlew.bat :cfir:analysis-tests:test --tests '*ClassGenerated.testClassAccessControl15' --tests '*ClassGenerated.testClassAccessControl16' --tests '*ClassGenerated.testClassAccessControl17' --tests '*ClassGenerated.testClassAccessControl18' --tests '*OverloadGenerated.testOverloadResolution1' --tests '*CallGenerated.testCallReferenceInference01' --tests '*CallGenerated.testCallReferenceInference02' --tests '*CallGenerated.testCallReferenceInference03' -x :cfir:analysis-tests:generateTestGeneratorForCfirAnalysisTestsTests --no-daemon --max-workers=1 --console=plain --no-build-cache --no-configuration-cache`.
- verification outcome: `BUILD SUCCESSFUL in 8m 24s`; XML reported 16 tests across six PSI/LightTree generated suites with 0 failures and 0 errors.

## Typed function references select by signature before reporting target legality

- problem type: a standalone function value with an expected function type must select declarations by callable signature before reporting accessibility or other candidate-local legality errors; a uniquely matching private declaration reports `INVISIBLE_MEMBER`, while only a genuine absence of signature-compatible declarations reports `NO_MATCH_FUNCTION_DECLARATION_FOR_REF`.
- root cause: `CfirCallResolver.reduceCandidates` filtered every typed standalone function value through `candidate.isSuccessful`, dropping inaccessible discoveries before expected-type selection. Its isolated `completedFunctionReferenceType` probe also rejected any unsuccessful candidate, so a signature-compatible private target was reclassified as a type mismatch and `ConeNoMatchingFunctionReferenceError` shadowed the existing visibility diagnostic path.
- official Cangjie evidence: cjc 1.0.5 selects private static `A.f()` for `let f: () -> Int64 = A.f` and then reports `sema_invalid_access_function`; `(Int64) -> Int64 = A.f` selects the accessible overload and succeeds. The same expected-type-before-access order holds for object receiver function values, whereas no-expected-type object values and ordinary calls filter inaccessible declarations first. `external/cangjie_compiler/src/Sema/TypeCheckExpr/NameReferenceExpr.cpp:327-388,634-681`, `TypeCheckReference.cpp:635-665`, `TypeCheckAccess.cpp:93-126`, and `TypeCheckCall.cpp:1824-1859,2260-2308` own those separate selection and legality orders.
- Kotlin counterpart files consulted: `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/resolve/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/resolve/calls/candidate/CallKind.kt`; `stages/ResolutionStages.kt`; `stages/CheckCallableReferenceExpectedType.kt`; `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/semantics/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/resolve/calls/ResolutionDiagnostic.kt`; `external/kotlin/compiler/resolution.common/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/resolve/calls/tower/CandidateApplicability.kt`; `candidate/CandidateCollector.kt`; `FirCallResolver.kt`; and `ResolveUtils.kt`. Kotlin keeps expected-type compatibility and candidate legality as distinct stages and preserves the selected candidate's own error.
- CFIR owner files changed: `cfir/resolve/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/resolve/body/CfirCallResolver.kt`; official-evidence guard added to `cfir/analysis-tests/testData/llt/class/class_static04.cj`.
- repair principle: typed function-reference compatibility is determined only by parameter shape, resulting callable type, constraint consistency, completion, and final function-type subtyping; candidate-local diagnostics never mean signature mismatch and remain attached to the selected original candidate for the normal diagnostic mapper.
- fixtures covered: PSI and LightTree `class_static04.cj` for both static and object private targets; `func_assign_var.cj`, `constraint_check/expose6.cj`, and `Extend/extend_subtype3.cj` as genuine no-match guards; `overloaded_func_01.cj` and `overload_resolution_1.cj` as successful expected-type overload selection; `function_access.cj` as ordinary-call visibility; `class_access_control15/16/17/18.cj` and `call_reference_inference_01/02/03.cj` as no-expected-type visibility and ambiguity guards.
- fixture additions and corrections: `class_static04.cj` now includes the official object-receiver typed-private guard and expects `INVISIBLE_MEMBER` on the complete `f` token. `func_assign_var.cj` moved the unchanged `AMBIGUOUS_USE` marker from the entire `Sub().ff` expression to the complete selector token `ff`, matching the project Diagnostic Range Policy and the existing `class_access_control16/17` selector ownership.
- verification command: `.\gradlew.bat :cfir:analysis-tests:test --tests '*ClassGenerated.testClassStatic04' --tests '*FunctionGenerated.testFuncAssignVar' --tests '*FunctionGenerated.testOverloadedFunc01' --tests '*FunctionGenerated.testFunctionAccess' --tests '*ConstraintCheckGenerated.testExpose6' --tests '*ExtendGenerated.testExtendSubtype3' --tests '*ClassGenerated.testClassAccessControl15' --tests '*ClassGenerated.testClassAccessControl16' --tests '*ClassGenerated.testClassAccessControl17' --tests '*ClassGenerated.testClassAccessControl18' --tests '*OverloadGenerated.testOverloadResolution1' --tests '*CallGenerated.testCallReferenceInference01' --tests '*CallGenerated.testCallReferenceInference02' --tests '*CallGenerated.testCallReferenceInference03' -x :cfir:analysis-tests:generateTestGeneratorForCfirAnalysisTestsTests --no-daemon --max-workers=1 --console=plain --no-build-cache --no-configuration-cache`.
- verification outcome: `BUILD SUCCESSFUL in 2m 56s`; XML reported 28 tests across twelve PSI/LightTree generated suites with 0 failures and 0 errors.
- Class regression command: `.\gradlew.bat :cfir:analysis-tests:test --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt._class.ClassGenerated' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt._class.ClassGenerated' -x :cfir:analysis-tests:generateTestGeneratorForCfirAnalysisTestsTests --no-daemon --max-workers=1 --console=plain --no-build-cache --no-configuration-cache`.
- Class regression outcome: 470 tests completed with 4 failures, down from the verified 8-failure baseline before the two function-reference repairs. The only remaining failures are `member3` and `invalid_super_call` in PSI and LightTree; no new Class failure appeared.

## Non-header `this(...)` calls retain constructor-recursion semantics

- problem type: an illegal non-first `this(...)` call must still resolve its target constructor and participate in recursive-constructor detection; the independent illegal-placement diagnostic does not suppress the dependency edge.
- root cause: after non-header constructor-delegation calls recovered their nominal constructed type, `record_this.cj` correctly exposed `RECURSIVE_CONSTRUCTOR_CALL`, but the fixture still expected only `ILLEGAL_PLACE_OF_CALLING_THIS_OR_SUPER`. The same fixture also retained the pre-policy whole-assignment range for `CANNOT_ASSIGN_TO_IMMUTABLE` instead of the final selector token.
- official Cangjie evidence: `cjc 1.0.5` reports both `sema_illegal_place_of_calling_this_or_super` on the non-first `this(1)` and `sema_recursive_constructor_call` on the enclosing `init`; self-cycle, acyclic, and two-constructor probes confirm that every resolved non-header constructor reference enters the cycle graph. `external/cangjie_compiler/src/Sema/Utils.cpp:42-46,102-176,188-214` recognizes every instance-constructor target and traverses all such references, while `external/cangjie_compiler/src/Sema/TypeChecker.cpp:1336-1367` checks illegal placement independently without clearing the target.
- Kotlin counterpart files consulted: `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/tree/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/declarations/FirConstructor.kt` and `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/checkers/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/analysis/checkers/declaration/FirCommonConstructorDelegationIssuesChecker.kt`; Kotlin's first-delegation property is an AST-structure reference only and cannot replace the broader official Cangjie dependency rule.
- CFIR owner files changed: fixture correction only in `cfir/analysis-tests/testData/llt/record/record_this.cj`; the shared recursion graph in `CfirRecursiveConstructorCallChecker.kt` was intentionally retained.
- repair principle: keep constructor-target resolution, placement legality, and recursive dependency analysis as orthogonal shared stages, and correct stale fixture expectations rather than filtering valid graph edges.
- fixtures covered: PSI and LightTree `record_this.cj`; `function/recursive_ctor_00.cj` through `recursive_ctor_08.cj`; `class/super_this/recursive_constructor_call.cj`; `record/constructor/call_other_init/record_constructor_call_other_init01.cj`; and all three diagnostics2 entry paths for `constructorDelegationMatrix.cj` and `recursiveConstructorCall.cj`.
- fixture correction: added `RECURSIVE_CONSTRUCTOR_CALL` to the complete `init` token in `record_this.cj`, preserved `ILLEGAL_PLACE_OF_CALLING_THIS_OR_SUPER` on `this`, and moved `CANNOT_ASSIGN_TO_IMMUTABLE` to the complete selector token `length` according to the Diagnostic Range Policy and the existing immutable-assignment family contract.
- focused verification command: `.\gradlew.bat :cfir:analysis-tests:test --tests '*RecordGenerated.testRecordThis' -x :cfir:analysis-tests:generateTestGeneratorForCfirAnalysisTestsTests --no-configuration-cache --no-daemon --max-workers=1 --console=plain --no-build-cache`.
- focused verification outcome: `BUILD SUCCESSFUL`; PSI and LightTree `record_this` both passed.
- family verification command: `.\gradlew.bat :cfir:analysis-tests:test --tests '*FunctionGenerated.testRecursiveCtor*' --tests '*SuperThisGenerated.testRecursiveConstructorCall' --tests '*RecordGenerated.testRecordThis' --tests '*CallOtherInitGenerated.testRecordConstructorCallOtherInit01' --tests '*InvalidDeclarationGenerated.testConstructorDelegationMatrix' --tests '*InvalidDeclarationGenerated.testRecursiveConstructorCall' -x :cfir:analysis-tests:generateTestGeneratorForCfirAnalysisTestsTests --no-configuration-cache --no-daemon --max-workers=1 --console=plain --no-build-cache`.
- family verification outcome: `BUILD SUCCESSFUL`; XML reported `30 tests, 0 failures, 0 errors` across PSI, LightTree, diagnostics2 PSI, diagnostics2 LightTree, and diagnostics2 no-alias-expansion entry paths.

## Illegal-position `super()` preserves its nominal receiver type

- problem type: a resolved `super(...)` call outside the constructor-header position must report its independent placement error while retaining the constructed superclass type for following member access; a missing member reports `NOT_MEMBER_OF`, and an existing member resolves without an unresolved-reference cascade.
- root cause: `CfirExpressionsResolveTransformer.transformConstructorDelegationCall` assigned `Unit` to every successfully resolved `this(...)` / `super(...)` call. That is correct only for the declaration-level constructor-header delegation. A non-header `super()` therefore lost its nominal receiver and made `super().a`, `super().c`, and `super().c++` resolve against `Unit` or an error receiver.
- official Cangjie evidence: `cjc 1.0.5` reports `sema_illegal_place_of_calling_this_or_super` plus `sema_not_member_of` for `super().a`, but only the placement diagnostic for `super().c` and `super().c++`. `external/cangjie_compiler/src/Sema/TypeChecker.cpp:1336-1367` checks constructor-call placement independently, and `external/cangjie_compiler/src/Sema/TypeCheckExpr/NameReferenceExpr.cpp:295-325` reports missing members only after confirming that the base expression has a correct nominal type.
- Kotlin counterpart files consulted: `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/resolve/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/resolve/transformers/body/resolve/FirExpressionsResolveTransformer.kt`, `FirCallCompletionResultsWriterTransformer.kt`, and the generated `FirDelegatedConstructorCall` model; Kotlin keeps the constructed type reference and completed constructor candidate on the call node instead of replacing the resolved receiver model with an unrelated fallback type.
- CFIR owner files changed: `cfir/resolve/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/resolve/body/CfirExpressionsResolveTransformer.kt`.
- repair principle: constructor-call resolution always preserves the selected constructed type, while only the constructor-header expression surface is projected to `Unit`; placement legality remains a declaration-checker concern and never destroys receiver typing.
- fixtures covered: PSI and LightTree `class/invalid_super_call.cj`; guards include `record/record_this.cj`, `class/super_this/recursive_constructor_call.cj`, class and record `constructor/primaryConstructor/primaryConstructor8.cj`, and the complete PSI/LightTree `ClassGenerated` suites.
- fixture correction: none for `invalid_super_call.cj`; its existing `NOT_MEMBER_OF` and placement expectations now follow the official receiver behavior. The related `record_this.cj` correction is logged separately above.
- focused verification command: `.\gradlew.bat :cfir:analysis-tests:test --tests '*ClassGenerated.testInvalidSuperCall' --tests '*RecordGenerated.testRecordThis' --tests '*SuperThisGenerated.testRecursiveConstructorCall' --tests '*PrimaryConstructorGenerated.testPrimaryConstructor8' -x :cfir:analysis-tests:generateTestGeneratorForCfirAnalysisTestsTests --no-configuration-cache --no-daemon --max-workers=1 --console=plain --no-build-cache`.
- focused verification outcome: the Class, super-this, and primary-constructor guards passed on both PSI and LightTree; `record_this` exposed only the separately verified official recursive-constructor fixture correction.
- Class regression command: `.\gradlew.bat :cfir:analysis-tests:test --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt._class.ClassGenerated' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt._class.ClassGenerated' -x :cfir:analysis-tests:generateTestGeneratorForCfirAnalysisTestsTests --no-configuration-cache --no-daemon --max-workers=1 --console=plain --no-build-cache`.
- Class regression outcome: `BUILD SUCCESSFUL`; XML confirms LightTree `235 tests, 0 failures, 0 errors` and PSI `235 tests, 0 failures, 0 errors`. The Class phase is fully green with no remaining `member3` or `invalid_super_call` failure.

## Previously verified diagnostic range contracts are propagated to the remaining fixtures

- problem type: stale inline expectations still used RHS-only declaration-tail ranges, the first character of `public`, whole field declarations, or whole qualified l-values after the shared diagnostic owners had already been verified to report the complete tail declaration, complete field-name token, or final selector token.
- root cause: the earlier range repairs covered their triggering Class fixtures but did not migrate every fixture owned by the same source-selection contracts. The current Record regression therefore exposed old expectations even though PSI and LightTree emitted identical, already-verified ranges.
- official Cangjie evidence: this is range-only and is governed by the repository Diagnostic Range Policy rather than `cjc` CLI anchors. Fresh PSI and LightTree XML for `record_access_control3.cj`, `record_recursive.cj`, and `record_6.cj` had unchanged source text and identical diagnostic-name multisets; only the inline ranges differed. The policy requires the complete relevant token, and the previously closed contracts at `Declaration-tailed function bodies preserve Unit semantics and declaration ownership` and `Immutable assignment diagnostics retain the final selector token` remain authoritative.
- Kotlin counterpart files consulted: `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/checkers/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/analysis/checkers/expression/FirFunctionReturnTypeMismatchChecker.kt`; `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/checkers/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/analysis/checkers/declaration/FirTopLevelPropertiesChecker.kt`; `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/checkers/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/analysis/checkers/expression/FirReassignmentAndInvisibleSetterChecker.kt`; `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/checkers/checkers-component-generator/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/checkers/generator/diagnostics/FirDiagnosticsList.kt`; `external/kotlin/compiler/frontend.common-psi/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/diagnostics/PositioningStrategies.kt`; and `LightTreePositioningStrategies.kt`. Kotlin keeps result-element ownership, declaration-name positioning, and qualified-selector positioning as centralized PSI/LightTree-symmetric contracts.
- CFIR owner files changed: none. Existing owners were retained: `CfirFunctionBodyTypeMismatchChecker.kt`, `CfirInitializationCheckers.kt`, `CfirDiagnosticsList.kt`, and `CfirAssignmentLegalityChecker.kt`; only stale fixture markers changed.
- repair principle: once a shared diagnostic owner and its PSI/LightTree range contract are verified, every ordinary fixture using that owner must express the same declaration/name/selector range, while optional-chain, subscript, VArray, increment/decrement, and other specialized contracts remain separate.
- fixtures covered: PSI and LightTree `record_access_control3.cj`, `record_recursive.cj`, `record_6.cj`, `PatternMatching/MatchExpression/match019.cj`, `record/mut/record_mut_invalid_3.cj`, `record/mut/record_mut_invalid_5.cj`, `class/let_in_init/let_in_init10-1.cj`, `let_in_init10-2.cj`, `let_in_init11-1.cj`, `let_in_init12-1.cj`, `assign/assign_const_instance_var.cj`, `assign/assign_008.cj`, `assign/assign_011.cj`, `enum/enum17_prop2.cj`, `let/mut_1.cj`, `let/mut_3.cj`, `let/let_value_assign.cj`, `let/let_value_assign_2.cj`, `record/constructor/access_control/record_constructor_access_control01.cj`, and `array/array_invalid_api.cj`.

No Cangjie source, diagnostic name, or independent semantic expectation changed. A marker-stripped comparison against `HEAD` confirmed source text unchanged in all 20 fixtures.
